The content script tells the worker of the entry's press as it is made (`agent/entered`, fire-and-forget, before the entry waits on what the press brings: `pressEntry`'s `onPress`), taken from the agent's tab's top frame for a document it asked to enter, so a press that takes the document away still counts the job begun.
- **Fixtures and the converter:** `scripts/mhtml-to-fixture.mjs` takes the four new platforms and their candidate hosts, and normalises each one's per-load values (`normaliseTokens`: Workday's `input-<n>` ids and beecatcher UUID, iCIMS's `hashed=` and Auth0 `state=`, SuccessFactors' `_s.crb=`, `requestParams=` and `jobSecKey`, Oracle's `primary-email-<n>` and `honey-pot-<n>`, the hCaptcha site keys, in `data-sitekey` and in its frames' addresses, and each widget's id) in the page, its header's source and its route map. 22 cases converted (docs/FIXTURES.md); iCIMS's, Taleo's, SuccessFactors' and Oracle's keep only the style rules their own elements match (`pruneCssInPage`: the new fixtures went from 25.4 MB to 7.8 MB, every expectation unchanged); the smoke routes gain an application page on each new host (`fixtures/smoke/workday-form.html` and `oracle-form.html`, for the two late adapters); `fixtures/synthetic/workday/account.html` reproduces Workday's account views for e2e.
- **Copy:** the unsupported page listed the five with "Sign-in and posting pages; its application forms are coming." (`COPY.panel.unsupported.sites[].note`), and the disclosure named them and the email-first step (version 5). Since the snapshot build only Taleo and SuccessFactors keep that note (Workday's, iCIMS's and Oracle's say what is left to the person), and the disclosure is version 7.
- **Tests:** each platform's `url.test.ts` and `<platform>.browser.test.ts` (every hook on every snapshot against its expectations, each account page's scan, the sign-in reading and its refusals, the entry press); `src/content/workday-sign-in.browser.test.ts` (Sign-in Auto on Workday's real Sign In, Create Account (Workday's and Salesforce's) and landing, the Forgot Password pause, Oracle's gate, the refusals, a CAPTCHA before and after the press, the password box re-read, the bounded wait for a button and the password written empty again, `guardSubmission` with real `requestSubmit()`); `src/dom/fillers/sign-in-doors.browser.test.ts` (the prelude and sign-in doors on their own, the one test lint lets name them); the committed fixtures' integrity (`test/mhtml-to-fixture.test.ts`, comment-aware); `src/background/agent-worker.test.ts` (the gate's answer carries no password); `e2e/specs/42-workday.spec.ts` (from the landing to the next step filled, never a GET; Create Account; Forgot Password; Manual); `e2e/specs/38-agent.spec.ts`'s synthetic sign-in pages moved to a Taleo apply address (Workday's own `/login` is no application now).
- **The live check of 2026-11-05** (read-only: the e2e build, never connected, on ten live public postings from the capture; every request but GET, HEAD and OPTIONS aborted, and anything to localhost; nothing typed, no account page's button pressed; the adapters' own hooks read in the page by a probe bundled from this code; the one press the agent's entry): on each of Workday (Workday's wd5, Snap's myworkdaysite), iCIMS (East West Bank, GDMS, in the wrapper's frame), Taleo (HDR, Chicago Public Schools), SuccessFactors (Cipla; Danfoss's posting closed during the session, its "no longer available" page read as no posting) and Oracle (elhr, JPMC) the extension registered the page under the platform's application key and read the posting's own text under it (a clean policy reading), and the adapter found its entry control. Workday: one agent/enter pressed Apply and then the choice's Apply Manually, and the account landing showed, read 'sign-in' with its "Sign in with email" prelude, the flow page claimed as the form root under the same key. Taleo and SuccessFactors: their GET sign-in addresses showed 'sign-in', the declared form passing every refusal rule as served. Oracle: APPLY NOW reached the email gate, 'email-gate', its declared form passing on elhr and refused ('method') on JPMC's uncovered region-less host. The first pass found Taleo's text unreadable (an html background image under its white body: the backdrop rule above), the second read it (5,878 and 5,700 characters). Aborted on the way: analytics, Taleo's `jobdetail.ajax`, Oracle's user tracking, Workday's SEEK widget.
- **Waits for the owner** (docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md, "Snapshots and owner-only inputs still needed"; the snapshots of 2026-11-06 answered Workday's steps and its sign-in texts, iCIMS's steps and its "Create a login", Oracle's code page and its one-page flow): each platform's page after Submit where no snapshot shows it, iCIMS's returning candidate's password step on login.icims.com (and whether the every-site sign-in should cover that host), Taleo's registration ("New User", a post-back), its "Apply as Guest" and whether accepting its Privacy Agreement is the extension's, SuccessFactors' terms dialog, its DPCS dialog at Apply and its application form after the account, Oracle's multi-page flow and Easy Apply, a user-name slot in the saved sign-in (Taleo), and the agent's addresses for Workday, Taleo, SuccessFactors and Oracle (TailorIT's API 5 reads the five platforms' addresses, and API 6 gives the agent iCIMS's).

## The audit of 2026-11-06 (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §12 Decision 14)
The security and correctness audits of `94c5ba6`, as fixed; the rules are Decision 14's, the decisions Claude made are docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md §11. Where to find each:
- **S-1:** `content/ui/visible.ts` `CONTROL_SEEN_MS` (600); `page-ui.ts` `counts(control)` (one gate for the launcher, the card, Edit with AI and its Generate, the tab's ×, grip and menu: `shownFor` at the event, never `settled()` afterwards); `panel.tsx` (the capture gate before React: the panel seen that long and the click inside it; the dock's own button too). The DOM tests reproduce the audit's popover uncovered on mouseenter (`content/ui/ui.testkit.ts` `uncoveredAsThePointerComes`). The e2e helper `e2e/helpers/page-ui.ts` clicks a control only once its box has held still for `CONTROL_SEEN_MS` + 300 ms.
- **S-8:** `content/ui/shadow.ts` `CONTAINED_EVENTS` and `ai-edits.ts` `PROMPT_EVENTS`.
- **S-2, S-3:** `core/guard/attention.ts` `BOT_CHECK_ATTENTION`, `PERSON_ONLY_ATTENTION`, `botCheckOf`, `namesBotCheck`; `core/resolver/resolve.ts` `classifyField` (bot checks first: reason `bot-check`, category `attentionCheck`); `core/plan/answers.ts` `namesUnanswerableTopic` (and `background/ai-tasks.ts` `answerItemOfCore` again); `content/orchestrator.ts` `challengedNow()` (a `bot-check` row left for the person holds Next Auto, Submit Auto and its re-check, and raises the notice `bot-check`); `background/agent.ts` `agentVerdict` (`bot-check`: needs-you `captcha`).
- **S-4, S-5:** `core/resolver/labelRules.ts` `AI_TERM`, `OWN_WORK`, `asksHowApplicationMade` (cause `how-made`, reason `how-application-made`), `namesBindingWaiver` (cause `binding-waiver`); `policy.ts` `ConsentContext.list`.
- **S-6:** `background/runstate-store.ts` `markPrivate` (`privrun:<tab>`), `index.ts` (frame/hello from a private tab; launcher place and hide), `telemetry.ts`, the accept book (`accepts.forget`).
- **S-7:** `dom/pause-detect.ts` `pageChallenged(doc)` (the response fields' parents); `content/press.ts` (`pressStep`, `pressEntry`) and `orchestrator.ts`.
- **S-9:** `adapters/address.ts` `readableAncestors`, `framedByEmployerPage`; `entrypoints/page-bridge.content.ts`; `content/runtime.ts` `startContentRuntime` (an inert runtime).
- **S-10:** `core/plan/drafts.ts` `rewriteItemOf`, `background/ai-tasks.ts` `draftBatches`.
- **M1, m9:** `background/job-cards.ts` (`jobchoice:<tab>`, `JobCards.ready`, per application); `ui/panel` (`useTabReports` `relinked`). **M4:** `background/settings.ts` `mergeSettings`, `settings/save`'s `base`, `ui/options/settings.ts`. **m1:** `pushSettings` per frame (`contentSettingsFor`). **m6:** `index.ts` `KEEP_KEY_AFTER_PRESS_MS`. **m7:** `Panel.tsx` `viewKey`. **m8:** `ui/panel/letter.ts`.
- **M5, m14:** `background/agent.ts` (above). **M6, m4:** `background/runs.ts` `noteAttached` (the RunRecord's `jobId`, `resumeId`), `submissions.ts` (the arm's `url`; `dropTokenData` drops arms and page states together).
- **M2, m5:** `content/loop.ts` (`waitsForLetter` for a cover-letter text box too; one Skip slot across the AI wait); `ui/report/Report.tsx` (Skip offered again on `expired`). **M3, m10, m13:** `content/runtime.ts` (`continueRun` keeps `dryRun`; `SUBMIT_WATCH_MS` in `submitted.ts`; a paused run aborted before a new one). **m2:** `content/verify.ts`. **m3:** `orchestrator.ts` (a resumed run offering Continue reports `review`; `buttonsNow()` offers nothing then). **m11:** `dom/widgets/fill.ts`.
- **The surviving mutations** each have a killing test: the disclosure gate on the lookup on load (`background/disclosure-gate.test.ts`), Submit Auto's re-check (`content/submit-recheck.browser.test.ts`), the read-back mismatch (`content/verify.browser.test.ts`), a touched write under Skip (`content/skip.browser.test.ts`), the live report on an equal `updatedAt` (`reports.test.ts`), the panel told of recording and `dropTokenData` (`submissions.test.ts`), the sign already on the page (`content/runtime-audit.browser.test.ts`), and the agent's tab, result, session, frame and `pressedAt` checks (`agent.test.ts`).
- **Date-only postings** (TailorIT's audit, D12): TailorIT keeps a posting date given as a day alone as exactly midnight UTC; the job card's "Posted N days ago" counts such a date by calendar days from that UTC date to the person's local today (`ui/panel/posted.ts` `postedDaysAgo`), and any other moment by whole days of time as before.
- **Not done:** m12 (Lever writing while it reads the resume) waits for a snapshot (docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md).

## The snapshot build of 2026-11-06 (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §12 Decisions 15 to 18)
What follows holds over every note above where they differ. The owner shared 249 Chrome page snapshots (.mht) of real application flows, taken signed in, and asked that the work be completed from them, **not guessed**: every selector, label reading, step name, button, widget behaviour and success sign below comes from markup seen in a snapshot, and its code comment or fixture case names that snapshot; what no snapshot shows is left out and listed as still unknown (docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md §12). The snapshots hold the owner's real data: they never enter the repository, and every fixture made from one is converted and redacted (docs/FIXTURES.md "The owner's snapshots of 2026-11-06", docs/PRIVACY.md). The build ran in waves, each later one holding over the earlier where they differ: the owner's snapshots of the platforms already built (Workday, iCIMS, Oracle, SuccessFactors, the boards) and a new one, SmartRecruiters (Decision 15); the new ATSs' shared layer and their fifteen adapters (Decision 16); four core passes over what the platform builders could not change (X, Y, Z1, Z2: Decision 17); then an adversarial security review (S1 to S13) and a behaviour review (B1, M1 to M9, minors) of the merged build, both fixed (Decision 18), and TailorIT's API 6 (above).

### Workday's application steps (`src/adapters/workday/application.ts`)
From 79 pages of 11 tenants on `*.myworkdayjobs.com`, through "Autofill with Resume" (`/apply/autofillWithResume`) and "Apply Manually" (`/apply/applyManually`). The flow page (`main > [data-automation-id=applyFlowPage]`) keeps one address for every step and draws each step in place.
- **stepSignature:** the progress bar `ol[data-automation-id=progressBar]` (one `li` per step: `progressBarCompletedStep`, `progressBarActiveStep`, `progressBarInactiveStep`, each with "current step 2 of 7" and the step's name); the heading is the active step's name, the progress its "current step N of M", the anchor the shown step page's `data-automation-id` (`applyFlowAutoFillPage`, `applyFlowMyInfoPage`, `applyFlowMyExpPage`, `applyFlowPrimaryQuestionsPage`, `applyFlowSecondaryQuestionsPage`, `applyFlowSupplementaryQuestionsPage`, `applyFlowVoluntaryDisclosuresPage`, `applyFlowSelfIdentifyPage`, `applyFlowReviewPage`), or `signInContent` on the account step. No URL part.
- **isFinalStep:** Review on the bar's last step is `final`; a known step page before the last, its footer button shown, `not-final`; the account view, an unknown step page or a bar that disagrees `unknown` (nothing pressed). **nextButton:** `button[data-automation-id=pageFooterNextButton]` ("Save and Continue"; "Continue" on Autofill with Resume) on a `not-final` step, withheld while a required date the step shows is empty; **submitButton:** the same footer button on Review only ("Submit"), pressed only through the Submit gates. Back (`pageFooterBackButton`) is never pressed.
- **sections:** each `div[role=group]` whose `aria-labelledby` names a heading inside it ("Legal Name", "Address", "Phone", "Terms and Conditions", "Resume/CV", "Websites 1"), else the step's name. **labelFor:** a checkbox group (`fieldset[data-automation-id$=-CheckboxGroup]`) by the outer fieldset's legend; an Application Questions dropdown by its fieldset's rich-text legend; a drop zone's file input by the label its `attachments-FileUpload` names. **requiredFor:** the star drawn in the label or legend (`<abbr aria-hidden="true">*</abbr>`, or `<abbr title="required">*</abbr>`), judged drawn by its own style and box (`aria-required` is set on text boxes alone). **descriptionFor:** on Self Identify, the form's title ("Voluntary Self-Identification of Disability") is each question's help text, so the EEO and sensitive rules read it; a drop zone's input gets its group's heading ("Resume/CV") as help text, since its own label ("Upload a file (5MB max)") names no document, so the résumé rule reads My Experience's Resume/CV as the résumé (before, no run ever uploaded one there).
- **Widgets:** the dropdown, `button[aria-haspopup=listbox]` in a `formField-*`, a select-only `combobox` whose answer is the button's words while its `value` names an option; opened by its own click, it says `aria-expanded="true"` and `aria-controls` its `ul[role=listbox]` at the end of the body. `dom/widgets/aria.ts` `isActionable` takes a declared combobox whose element is such a button (`opensListbox`): its list is found by ARIA once it shows; one that never names it stays `no-list`. The prompt (`[data-automation-id=multiSelectContainer]`'s search box: How Did You Hear About Us?, Country Phone Code, Field of Study, Skills, School, an attachment's Category) is a `typeahead-remote` whose answer is its first pill; no snapshot shows its list open, so the helpers never type into it (`widget-unsupported`, the person's). No page-world bridge. (Since snapshot round 2 the prompts are searched and Country and State hold stored lists: "Snapshot round 2" below.)
- **Dates** (`[data-automation-id=dateInputWrapper]`): each part is an `aria-hidden` display over a 1 px, `scale(0.01)`, `z-index: -1000` spin button, which the scanner reads as hidden: dates are never fields and never written; a required one is the person's, and Next waits for it. (Since snapshot round 2 a date is one field, typed part by part: "Snapshot round 2", Workday: dates.)
- **Uploads:** `uploadWidgets` pairs each drop zone's `select-files` button (never pressed) with its `file-upload-input-ref` input; `uploadState` is confirmed once the field shows `file-upload-item` with that name and "Successfully Uploaded!", pending otherwise; `uploadHeld` (core pass Y) reads a field showing a file the page took as held ("Successfully Uploaded!" shown beside the file's name): My Experience's Resume/CV after "Autofill with Resume" shows the resume Workday read while its input holds none, and the person's mode decides (below, "Résumé already on the page"); Workday's input takes several files (`multiple`), so Upload mine again adds the person's beside the shown one.
- **repeatables** (My Experience): Work Experience and Education, each `div[role=group][aria-labelledby=<Kind>-section]` with its `<Kind>-<n>-panel` blocks and its Add (`add-button`); a Work Experience block's `currentlyWorkHere` box is its `currentlyHere`. `dom/scan.ts` `group()` makes same-named checkboxes one question only inside one repeatable block (`repeatableBlockOf`), so each block's "I currently work here" stays its own. Languages and Websites are not declared.
- **errors:** the generic `aria-invalid` ones and, on the account view, `div[data-automation-id=errorMessage][role=alert]` above the form (Sign-in Auto's `errorShown` asks the adapter with the form, so a failed sign-in or an unverified account ends 'failed' at once; "Resend Account Verification" is never pressed). **isBusy:** `wd-LoadingPanel` while seen. **neverPress** adds Back, the cookie notice's buttons, the resend, an upload's delete and select, a pill's `DELETE_charm`, a block's Delete, the calendar and the phone's terms link.
- No success sign: no snapshot follows Review's Submit.

### iCIMS after the gate (`src/adapters/icims/`)
From 47 snapshots of ten portals, every step after the email gate.
- **Addresses** (`url.ts`): under `/jobs/<id>/[<slug>/]` the application's words are `login`, `candidate` (the Candidate Profile, opened from the email's link with `eem` and `code`, or after the gate with `csrf`, `&uploadResume=` once a resume was read), `questions` (`?global=1` the Candidate Questions, none the Job Specific Questions), `eeo` and `form` (an IForm page); `job?mode=submit_apply` is the confirmation. The key stays the host and the posting's id. An IForm's own form frame (`<co>.icims.com/forms?...`, its address naming no posting) is no application by its address; the worker keys it by the chain (below). `/referral` stays none.
- **Frames:** most portals draw every page in `iframe#icims_content_iframe` of the company's wrapper at the same address; two serve each page as the top document. The adapter names that frame as the wrapper's application frame (`applicationFrame`, on a page with no step form or gate of its own), so the agent, Sign-in Auto, Next and Submit act there (below, "The frame that claims the application"); S-9 holds as before (a step's frame below its own wrapper speaks, below a portal page that shows no posting it stays silent).
- **The steps' forms** (`detect`, each with its own Submit): the profile `form#profileForm` with `input#cp_form_submit_i` "Submit Profile"; the questions `form#questions` with `#quesp_form_submit_i`; EEO `form#profileForm` with `#eeop_form_submit_i`; an IForm's `form[name=icims_form].iCIMS_FormMainStyle` with `input[name=icims_submit]`; the gate `form#enterEmailForm` first, as before.
- **The step bar** (`div.iCIMS_Steps`): `li#Step_<step>` per step, `li.iCIMS_Steps_Current` the shown one, "Step 4 of 5" in its sr-only text. It is read only where it cannot be misread (`certainBarOf`): one bar, each item one of the seven step ids the snapshots show and none twice, each sr-only count fitting its place and the bar's length, one current item. `isFinalStep` is `'final'` on its last item, `'not-final'` only while a later step shows (`laterStep`), `'unknown'` otherwise (no bar, two bars, an unknown or repeated id, a count that does not fit, a hidden later step: nothing is pressed). Every step's button says "Submit", so the adapter declares the profile's, the questions' and EEO's by their ids as `stepSubmitNext` (below, "A step's own Next whose words say Submit"); the last step's Submit is pressed only through the Submit gates. An IForm's `icims_submit` is not named (its frame holds no bar).
- **stepSignature:** the current item's id, the step heading (`div.iCIMS_PageStepText`, else the h2 above the form), the address's word, and the step's Submit and an IForm's own field names as anchors; never a block's numbered names, so "Add More" keeps the step.
- **The profile's "Create a login"** (Login, Password, Password (Re-enter), in a `div[role=group]` of `form#profileForm`) is the adapter's `accountSection` (below, "An account section inside an application form"): the profile fills, the login is never a field (`excluded`), and Sign-in Auto types the saved sign-in there. A gate that also asks for a consent's scope (`select#gdpr_consent_type`) or a phone is Sign-in Auto's to type the email into and the person's to finish (`personPresses`). After the gate's Next, "Please verify your email account ..." (no form) is `authState` 'email-code'.
- **Repeatables:** on the profile, `div.iCIMS_CollectionContainer` per block, the visible "Add More (<name>)"; Education is `CandProfileFields.Education`, experience the collection whose legend is "Professional Experience (<n>)". A block's Delete is never pressed.
- **Uploads:** a file field's input inside its visible "My Computer" button; once a file is on file the page hides the buttons ("Replace Resume" shown), and the adapter excludes that part, so no file field is offered under either choice of the résumé mode (uploading again would make iCIMS read the resume afresh). The résumé input (`#PortalProfileFields.Resume_File`): `isBusy` while "Parsing resume, please wait..." (`#PortalProfileFields.Resume_Loading`, `.iCIMS_loadingImageResume`) shows; `uploadState` 'pending' while that block shows in the input's own row or the row names no file or another one, 'confirmed' once its `.iCIMS_CurrentFile` shows the written file's name; `uploadHeld` while the row shows a current file.
- **Dropdowns** (`a.dropdown-select[role=combobox]` over `select[icimsdropdown-enabled="1"]`): every one keeps at most one option, the answer it holds, its list loading from the server (61 such selects on 7 portals), so there is no native list to fill; they stay `widget-phase-4`, the person's. The selects iCIMS draws natively (`icimsdropdown-enabled="0"`) are filled as any select (`dropdowns.browser.test.ts` pins both readings).
- **Errors:** `div[role=alert].iCIMS_ErrorMessage` with `li.iCIMS_pageError` per question, and the row marked `.iCIMS_TableRow.iCIMS_HasError`; never the inactivity warning, the cookies or the share notice. **The success sign:** `.iCIMS_Message.iCIMS_SuccessMessage` "Your application has been submitted successfully." on `job?mode=submit_apply` (`icimsSubmitted`, read by `content/submitted.ts`); as captured it sits inside the posting's text, so it counts by the confirmation address, never as a sign drawn in the form's place.
- **Never pressed** (added): Finish Later, Save & Return Later, "Keep me logged in", the profile's social sign-ins, the cloud upload buttons, Delete, a block's Delete, an IForm's "Show Less", the header's Welcome page, Dashboard and Logout.
- The EEO and self-identification forms follow the existing EEO rules (the profile's answer or the form's decline). The IForm's frame is read once the chain keys it: its signature box (its binding sentence beside the box, outside its label) is `consent.signature`, left to the person (`consent-requires-user`). `?global=1` stays in the step signature: it marks the Candidate Questions, kept after its Submit, while the Job Specific Questions post with `global=` empty. The US Employment Application and the Jibe fronts are described in the snapshots' notes only (not committed; nothing built for the Jibe fronts, whose Apply opens iCIMS's gate).

### Oracle Recruiting Cloud and SuccessFactors
- **Oracle's government cloud:** `<tenant>.fa.ocs.oraclegovcloud.com` serves the same Candidate Experience application, so it joins `ATS_MATCHES` (`https://*.fa.ocs.oraclegovcloud.com/hcmUI/CandidateExperience/*`), `HOST_PLATFORMS` (by the candidate path), `oracle/url.ts` and the converter; only the `fa.ocs` region seen is matched, and all of `*.oraclegovcloud.com` joins the employee HR systems the sentinel never reaches.
- **The code page:** after the gate's NEXT, Oracle draws "Confirm Your Identity" at the gate's own address: `div.apply-flow-pin-code-required` with six `input.pin-code-input__input` boxes (no maxlength), "Keep me signed in", Verify and "Send New Code" (never pressed). `authState` is `email-code` by those boxes; Jobright's reading (four one-character boxes) was dropped. The code stays the person's.
- **The apply flow** (`/apply/section/1`, a one-page flow with every section and one Submit at its foot): the adapter detects `form.apply-flow__content.apply-flow__content-form`; labels and requires an answer-pill group (`cx-select-pills`, its row's `label.input-row__label`) and the phone's number box; `uploadWidgets` a document's hidden input behind its "Upload Cover Letter" label; `submitButton` and `isFinalStep` ('final' where the flow shows its Submit); `errors` a timeline entry marked `--invalid-block` (an error shown on load is the person's: below, "The agent") and, document-wide, the shown notification `li.notifications__item.cx-notifications__item--error` (after a refused Submit; while the modal checklist dialog is open it makes `div.app` aria-hidden, so no form is detected until it closes); a hidden Submit is no Submit (`isFinalStep` 'unknown'); `neverPress` Send New Code, Import Profile Again, the attached resume's download and Remove Resume, each combobox's clear, Edit email address, Back to Job Details and the Add buttons. Its `cx-select` and `single-select-combobox` lists are in no snapshot open: undeclared, the person's.
- **It reads as captured:** the flow's form is a 75 % translucent panel over the tenant's cover image, which the colour rules could not judge before core pass Y's bound (below, "A translucent layer over an image"); now the gate, the code page and the apply section read with their image kept, so Sign-in Auto takes the gate (its email typed, its terms box ticked) where it refused before.
- **SuccessFactors:** DFIN's account creation is the same `form#careerform` as before (a new case, no new rule); its required reCAPTCHA checkbox now holds every press while the account pauses (below, "A checkbox CAPTCHA"). The application form after the account, posted to `/portalcareer?_s.crb=<token>`, names no job: no chain page (the behaviour review's minor 9), so the extension stays dormant there until a fixture shows it; its markup (collapsible sections, `rcmpaginatedselectinput` comboboxes, UI5 date pickers in shadow roots) is noted for a later adapter. A `/career` post-back with `_s.crb` and no job is a chain page.

### SmartRecruiters (`src/adapters/smartrecruiters/`, a new platform)
- **Hosts:** `https://jobs.smartrecruiters.com/*` (`ATS_MATCHES`, `HOST_PLATFORMS`, a public job host for the bridge); not `careers.smartrecruiters.com` (no snapshot shows a posting there) nor `www.smartr.me`. Since API 6 TailorIT lists it.
- **Addresses** (`url.ts`): a posting `/<Company>/<id>-<slug>` (`?trid=` on share links); the one-click application `/oneclick-ui/company/<Company>/publication/<uuid>` and its `.../screening` step (`?dcr_ci=<Company>`), one application. The posting and its application share no key by address (the posting names its id, the application a publication UUID); the chain (below) gives the one-click page its posting's key when reached from it.
- **The adapter:** Angular pages of SmartRecruiters' own web components (`spl-*`, `oc-*`, `sr-*`), nearly all in open shadow roots, which the scanner reads. `detect` `oc-oneclick-form` or `oc-screening-questions`; `postingText` `main.jobad-main .job-sections`; `entryControl` the visible "I'm interested" (`a.js-oneclick[data-sr-track=apply]`) whose address is its own company's one-click application (`entryNeverPressed`), none on an expired posting; `excluded` `oc-easy-apply` (its resume parsing, Apply with LinkedIn, Indeed and SEEK); `labelFor` the phone's number box, the resume's drop zone by its section title, a screening question's drop zone by its question, and a screening Yes/No group's `label[slot=label]` (with `requiredFor` its fieldset's `aria-required`); `uploadWidgets` each `spl-dropzone`'s input; `widgetRoots`/`widgetFor` each screening `spl-radio-group` as a radio widget whose members are its slotted `spl-radio[role=radio]` (fields since core pass Y: below, "The scanner across shadow roots"); `nextButton`, `submitButton`, `isFinalStep` and `stepSignature` (`urlPart` 'first' or 'screening'); `errors` an `oc-validation-summary` that holds words; `neverPress` the posting's buttons, Indeed, SEEK, LinkedIn, the Add buttons and the screening step's Back. Its comboboxes (`spl-autocomplete`, `spl-select`) show no list open: undeclared, the person's. No success sign (no snapshot after Submit).

### The boards (Greenhouse, Ashby, Lever, Workable) and company pages embedding Greenhouse
- **Greenhouse:** the email code after a Submit (`div.email-verification > fieldset#email-verification`, eight `security-input-N` boxes) is `excluded` and `authState` 'email-code', so nothing in it is a field and Next and Submit hold (seen in 7 of 23 captures); a hidden block, or every box hidden, is none. `uploadState` finds the group by the input's id, since Greenhouse takes the input out of the page once a file is in it and shows `div.file-upload__filename` with "Remove file" (never pressed): confirmed when the name shows; a confirmed upload whose input left is verified (below, "Uploads and the read-back"). `isBusy` Submit disabled with `div.loading-spinner`. The error text after a Submit sits beside a control with `aria-invalid="true"`, read as before.
- **Ashby:** `isBusy` Submit disabled and classed `_loading_` with `svg.loadingSvg`, every input disabled; the uploaded file item and "Autofill completed!" are now seen on real pages and read as built.
- **Lever:** the posting page with no form is a fixture; nothing new on `/apply` (no "Analyzing resume...": m12 stays open).
- **Workable:** a confirmed upload joins the name the drop zone cuts in two spans (`div[data-id="filename"]`). **Workable's job-search site** (`jobs.workable.com`, in `ATS_MATCHES` and `HOST_PLATFORMS`): `/view/<id>/<slug>` shows the application form in a modal dialog at the same address; `workable/url.ts` reads it as the application, board unknown, canonical `https://jobs.workable.com/view/<id>`; every other page of the site is no posting. Every hook reads the form as on apply.workable.com; the dialog's own Cancel and Submit, the page's "Apply now", "Share job" and "Search for jobs" are never pressed; no `entryControl` (the agent does not take it). Since jr8 (below) the page's "Apply now" is its `entryControl`, pressed by the entry door alone. Its eight controls below the dialog's fold are fields since core pass Y (below, "Visibility").
- **Company pages** (ABC Legal, SeatGeek, WEKA): Phase 8's rules hold on real markup: the sentinel recognises none as captured, `siteFormOf` claims nothing beside the embed, the Greenhouse frame is an ATS match read by the Greenhouse adapter, WEKA's frame is the job board (no application).

### The new ATSs: the configured layer (`src/adapters/configured/`)
Fifteen platforms from the owner's snapshots (DESIGN-NEW-ATS, the coordinator's design for them, kept outside the repository; its snapshot ids, `adp-0` and so on, name the snapshots in code comments: Jobvite, JazzHR, BambooHR, Rippling, Breezy HR, CareerPlug, Pinpoint, Paycor, HiBob, UltiPro, Dayforce, Paylocity, Dice, ClearCompany and ADP Workforce Now), each written as data over one shared layer, its code hooks only where the data cannot say it.
- **`types.ts`:** `AtsConfig`, one per platform, with its rules (`PageState`, `SignInRule`, `QuestionRule`, `RequiredRule`, `SectionRule`, `WidgetRule`, `CheckedRule`, `UploadRule`, `RepeatableRule`, `StepRule`) and `ConfiguredHooks` (an `Adapter` without `id`, `matchesUrl` and `canonicalApplicationUrl`). Every rule carries `from`, the snapshot or fixture case it was read from. Pure types.
- **`index.ts`:** `configuredAdapter(config)` (a whole `Adapter`), `configuredHooks(config)` (the same hooks, for a full adapter, ADP's and UltiPro's, to spread under its own), `configProblems(config)` (a config's contradictions) and `configuredSuccessSigns(doc, config)`. Nothing runs at import; reads go through `dom/visibility.ts`, `visible-text.ts` and `scan-cache.ts`.
- **`registry.ts`:** `ATS_CONFIGS`, read by `content/submitted.ts` for the success signs and by the layer's test, which holds every registered config to `configProblems`.
- **What each rule becomes:**
  - `parse`: `canonicalApplicationUrl`; `matchesUrl` the config's own, else `platformOfUrl(url) === platform`.
  - `states`: the first state whose `when` matches an element a person can see decides the page: `detect` gives its `root` (none: no form), `authState` its `auth`, `signInForm` its `signIn` form (the hook is always present, so a configured platform never gets the standard sign-in rule), `nextButton` its `next` (`isFinalStep` 'not-final' while it shows, and on a state filled like a step while its sign-in container and that button show, else 'unknown'; the container's button stays Sign-in mode's, never Next's, so the run waits for the page to move on), `stepSignature` `state:<name>`, `entryControl` its `entry` on an `'entry-choice'` state only. A state may also declare `signInContainer` (a page filled like a step, with an account or code button: below) or `entrySubmits` (a consent form its entry submits: below). Where the address's own adapter reads a state, the generic adapter never takes the page (the behaviour review's M3, `content/runtime.ts` `pickAdapter`): ClearCompany's account step is found by its own root and pauses as an account's creation, nothing filled from the profile; Jobvite's Data Consent is no form, so the agent's entry presses its "I Accept".
  - `root`: the first shown match of `css` holding `holds`. `questions`: the first rule whose container is the control's nearest `container` ancestor; `labelFor` its `title` inside the container (else the nearest preceding `titleBefore` sibling), `descriptionFor` its `help`, `groupOf` the container with `group: true`. `required`: a seen `mark` inside the control's labels, title or container, or `is` on those or an ancestor. `sections`: a container's first heading. `widgets`: `widgetRoots` and `widgetFor`. `checked`: `checkedOf`. `proxies`: `proxyFor` within its `scope`. `uploads`: `uploadWidgets` (the `control` inside the area, else inside the nearest ancestor holding one), `uploadState` (failed, pending, done within `area`; with the optional `name`, the file's name as a shown `done` sign writes it, 'confirmed' only while a sign shows the written file's name, so an older file's sign never confirms a new upload) and `uploadHeld` (a shown `done` sign in the input's area while the input holds no file: a file the page already holds; a sign naming no file, where `name` is declared, holds none). `name` is declared where the snapshots show a whole file name: Breezy, Jobvite, Paycor, Rippling, Dayforce and ClearCompany (not CareerPlug's, whose one snapshot shows no extension there). `repeatables`: the Add button outside every block. `steps`: heading, progress, anchors (an `attr`'s value, else `<index>:shown`, so ADP's four step bodies stay apart) and `urlPart`; `isFinalStep` 'final' when Submit shows and Next does not, 'not-final' the other way round; without `steps`, a one-page form, 'final' when Submit shows. `submit`, `excluded`, `isBusy`, `postingText`, `autosaves`, `quirks`: as named. `errors`: the generic ones plus the shown matches inside the root. `success`: the signs a person can see now (`configuredSuccessSigns`), read by `content/submitted.ts` through `ATS_CONFIGS` under its rules (new, shown where the form was, outside the posting text), so a sign the page holds hidden from the start (Breezy's) is new once shown. `neverPress`: every rule's matches, `steps.back`, `posting.entry` and each state's `entry`; a declared entry sets `entryNeverPressed`. `code`: spread last, so it wins.
- **A platform plugs in** with `url.ts` (pure; it reads its host and path itself, mirroring `shared/hosts.ts`, which a URL parser may not import: `url.test.ts` holds the two to one table), `config.ts`, `index.ts`, and one line each in `PLATFORM_ADAPTERS` (`src/adapters/index.ts`), `URL_PARSERS` (`address.ts`) and `ATS_CONFIGS`.
- **`Adapter.groupOf(control)`** (core hook): the element holding one question's options where the page draws no fieldset or group; `dom/scan.ts` asks it first (`adapterGroupOf`) for checkboxes and for a radio with no name, only inside the form root.
- **Hosts** (`src/shared/hosts.ts`): `ATS_MATCHES` gains 16 patterns, path-scoped where the host also serves other pages (JazzHR `/apply/`, Breezy `/p/`, CareerPlug `/jobs/`, Pinpoint `/en/postings/`, Paycor `/career/`, Paylocity `/Recruiting/`, Dice `/job-applications/`, ClearCompany `/careers/`, ADP's two recruitment paths, BambooHR `/careers/`); `HOST_PLATFORMS` entries take an optional `paths` (Oracle's candidate path is one now; `platformOfUrl` is null on a path-scoped host's other paths, compared case-sensitively as Chrome does); Pinpoint's and CareerPlug's `app.` hosts and ClearCompany's `cc-` hosts are no tenant. `PUBLIC_JOB_HOSTS` (`adapters/bridge-address.ts`) gains the candidate-only hosts (Jobvite's, Rippling's, UltiPro's, Dayforce's and HiBob's). `HR_HOST_MATCHES` and `isHrHost` gain `workforcenow.adp.com` and `www.paycomonline.net` (those hosts alone), `*.bamboohr.com` and `*.clearcompany.com`, and with the security review's S5 the new vendors' employee systems: `*.dayforcehcm.com`, `*.ultipro.com`, `*.ukg.net`, `*.paylocity.com`, `*.paycor.com`, `*.rippling.com`, `*.hibob.com`, `*.adp.com` and `app.careerplug.com`; `scripts/check-build.mjs` `HR_EXCLUDED` and `HR_ADDRESSES` follow. The HR list reaches only the sentinel's registration and `isCompanySiteUrl`; a candidate host stays a static ATS match. Paycom has no match (it waits), and DFIN stays a company career site. The sentinel's budget is 17 KiB (it holds the ATS list to rule those pages out: 16,917 bytes when the new hosts went in).
- **Platforms and copy:** `PLATFORMS` gains the fifteen and `smartrecruiters` (TailorIT lists them since API 6); telemetry counts, message schemas and stored keys follow `PLATFORMS` (`MAX_TELEMETRY_COUNTS` grows with it). `COPY.platforms` names each; "where it works" (`COPY.panel.unsupported.sites`) lists them with no note, `COPY.strip.none`, the telemetry platform field and `COPY.options.sites.intro` name them, and `COPY.options.sites.hint` names the employee pages of ADP Workforce Now, BambooHR, Paycom and ClearCompany among the HR systems the sentinel never reaches.

### The new ATSs, one by one
Each is `src/adapters/<platform>/` (`url.ts`, `config.ts`, `index.ts` and its browser test against its fixtures' expectations). Every rule names its snapshot (DESIGN-NEW-ATS's ids, `adp-0` and so on). CAPTCHAs and Turnstile stay the person's everywhere; résumé parsers, "Apply with LinkedIn/Indeed" and "Finish later" are never pressed.
- **ADP Workforce Now** (a full adapter over `configuredHooks(ADP_CONFIG)`): the guest page `/mascsr/default/mdf/recruitment/recruitment.html` and every wizard step `/mascsr/applicant/mdf/recruitment/postLogin.html` are one application, keyed by the employer's `cid`, the `jobId` (written twice on the wizard, required to agree) and the `ccId`; the canonical URL is the guest page's with those three. States: `code` (the emailed-code dialog, `input#oneTimePassWord`: `email-code`, Verify and Resend Code never pressed), `guest` (`div.guest-login-container`, filled from the profile like a step; its Continue, `sdf-button#recruitment_login_recaptcha`, which mails the code, is its `signInContainer` button: pressed only by Sign-in Auto, below; the step reads 'not-final' while it shows), else the wizard (root `#jobApplicationSubmit`). Steps: the header item with `aria-current="true"`, the four step bodies seen as anchors, Next `#ja_sv_cw_next_footer_btn`, Submit `#submitApplication` (only on the last step), Previous `back`. Code: `labelFor` for drawn Yes/No groups (`label.qLabel`), Self-ID groups, the referral, each phone, the guest page and the e-signature; `labelSuffix` "electronic signature" after "Please type your full name.", so it classifies `consent.signature` (its "I agree" box is the person's); `requiredFor` the label's `required-indicator` or `required`; react-select (`input.MDFSelectBox__input`, `bridge: 'react-select'`, its box `.MDFSelectBox__control`); the referral (`#refferalOptions`) a radio whose members are the real `sdf-radio-button[role=radio]` inside each `wfn-radio-button` host's shadow root (core pass Z2), read back by their `aria-checked`. Rules: `.vdl-checkbox` proxies, errors, busy `#recPageLoadWaitingIndicator`, `neverPress` (the social sign-ins, Join Our Talent Community, the left nav, Finish later, Exit, the header, cookies, help). The guest page fills since core pass Z1 (its Continue is a bound reCAPTCHA button, no drawn challenge: below). No success sign.
- **Dayforce** (`configuredAdapter`): `/[<locale>/]<clientNamespace>/<careerSiteXRefCode>/jobs/<id>/apply/manualApplication`, steps 2 and 3 with `?...&step=<n>`, one key (canonical: step 1's as written). Root `div#jobPostingApplication`; steps by the step body's test-id and the steps bar's `aria-current="step"` item; Next `[test-id=application-next-step]`, Submit `[test-id=application-submit]`, busy the loading Next. Rules: radio questions titled by `.ant-form-item-label > label`, required `label.ant-form-item-required`, Ant selects as widgets (`readOnlyBox` for the select-only ones), checked by Ant's wrapper classes, three uploads by their sections, Import Resume excluded; never Update, Cancel, a record's Edit or Delete, Remove file, Sign In, Cookie Preferences. Code: a dial-code select labelled by its phone's label with "country code"; each upload by its section's `h2`. Its invisible reCAPTCHA's badge holds no press (the behaviour review's B1); its image challenge on Submit pauses as always.
- **UltiPro** (a full adapter over `configuredHooks(ULTIPRO_CONFIG)`): `/<tenant>/JobBoard/<uuid>/OpportunityApply?opportunityId=<uuid>` the application, `OpportunityDetail` the posting, and the account step `/<TENANT>/AuthCode/Register?state=returnUrl=<that path>|ta=<TENANT>`, read through its returnUrl (by hand: `URLSearchParams` is banned in url.ts). States: `register` (`form#registrationDetailsForm`, filled like a step; its Create account is its `signInContainer` button, the native `button.ukg-button-native[type=submit]` the `ukg-button[data-automation=registrationDetails-submit-button]` host's open shadow root draws, outside the form; the step reads 'not-final' while it shows: `registerContainer`), `review` (the résumé pre-fill review, its Next "Save and continue"), else the application (root `#OpportunityApply`, one final page; its Submit the `ukg-button[data-automation=btn-submit]` host, whose native button is the adapter's Submit whatever its words: S9). Rules: sections the `div[role=region]` panels, required `label.control-label.required`, errors, never the Cancels, Sign Out, a Remove, a document's delete, the résumé parser and its saved-résumé choice, the navigation and the EEOC link. Code: the start date's three boxes in `ukg-date-input-text`'s shadow root labelled "month", "day", "year"; a period box by its group's label and its own ("To Month"); the Skills typeahead by its panel's `h2`. The register labels read since core pass Z2 (words slotted into a shadow root); its three boxes still read camouflaged in the fixture (their box is drawn by a constructed style sheet no .mht holds), so its required boxes stay empty there and its press holds.
- **Jobvite** (AngularJS): states `consent` (the Data Consent gate: `entry-choice`, its "I Accept" the entry, which submits only `form[name="consentForm"]` with the residence select `select#jv-country-select`: `entrySubmits`, below; "I Decline" and Back never pressed) and `residence` (its one select filled like a step). Root `body.jv-page-apply` holding `form[name="scopeData.applyForm"]` (the file inputs sit in body-level popovers outside the form). Steps: the shown `div.jv-apply-step`'s `ng-form` and `h3.jv-step-header`; Next `button[aria-label="Next"]`; Submit "Send Application". Uploads `#file-input-0` (the resume: its control the opener while no file is in, else its file row; done the row, pending its spinner) and `#file-input-1` (the cover letter, no sign). Code: the inputs' labels and sections; the self-identification form's closing name and date (`[ng-model="applyData.ofccpFormMap"] .JVA_NAME` / `.JVA_DATE`) read "Your Name signature" and "Today's Date signature" first (`labelFirst`, core pass Z2), so the name is a `consent.signature` and the date the person's. Excluded: the paste boxes. Busy "Sending Application". Posting text `div.jv-job-detail-description`, entry `a.jv-button-apply`.
- **Dice** (Next.js, React Aria): root the parent of `div[role=meter][aria-label="Application progress"]`; steps by the meter's values and the step's `h2`; Submit and Back told by their words; final only at the meter's maximum with Submit shown and no Next. The Work Authorization select is one declared `combobox` on its `div.group` (its answer read from the trigger only when it is one of the mirror select's options); its list is named only while open and no snapshot shows it: read (label, required, answer), the person's. The city is a `typeahead-remote`. The cover letter's sr-only input is labelled by its card's title; the résumé card holds no input while a résumé is in (File options never pressed). An open File options menu leaves the page inert: no root. No sign-in form (Dice's login is off the matched path). Its postings (`/job-detail/<uuid>`, the wizard's own uuid) are not mapped until TailorIT's `parseAtsUrl` reads them (`url.test.ts` holds the two readings equal).
- **ClearCompany** (Backbone): state `account` (root `form#personal-info-form`, its questions each `div.form-group` titled by its own label: `create-account`, its declared form the email, the email again and Continue, `personPresses`). The run finds the step and pauses there as an account's creation (the `auth` notice), nothing filled from the profile; Sign-in Auto types nothing there yet, since its declared form has no password box, which `content/sign-in.ts` requires on a wall that is no email gate. `quirks.settleCapMs` is 30 s (its own notice says the page may take up to 60 seconds; 30 s is the most any adapter may ask). Root `form.section-form`; steps by the active breadcrumb's `page-<n>` class; Next and Submit the one `button.submit-button` by its `data-localized-key`. Questions `div.form-field` titled by its `.field-title`, its star required; checkbox questions through their wrapping label (`proxies`); uploads `input[name="files[]"]` in each `div.qa-resume-upload`, done its `tr.file-row`; busy the spinners, "Creating your account..." and "Saving...". Code: each repeated section as a repeatable (its kind by the core's `sectionKindOf`, its "Add Additional Row", its rows' "Present" boxes); its rows reuse the first row's ids, so each row's labels are read within its own block (core pass Z2).
- **Paylocity** (React, no `<form>`): root `#app` holding `p.progress-header`; steps "Step N of 4" with the step page's `[id^=pcty-wr-]` and `h2`; Next and Submit both `#btn-submit`, told by `data-automation-id`. Questions: a `div.form-group`'s sibling label, an input-select's own label span (its footnote as help). Widgets: react-widgets DropdownLists (`combobox`, their list the one `aria-owns` names) and Paylocity's input-selects (`multiselect` where chips show, else `combobox`). Never the résumé parser, the review's Edit buttons, "Edit Application", shares, OneTrust. Step 1 is in no snapshot: standard rules.
- **JazzHR:** `<tenant>.applytojob.com/apply/<code>/<slug>`, the posting and its form on one page; root `form#form_submit_new_resume`; each two-box Yes/No checkbox question one group (`groupOf`), answered since core pass Z2 (below, "A Yes/No pair of checkboxes"); Submit the link `a#resumator-submit-resume`, and "Submitting...please wait..." busy; posting text `#job-description`. Its reCAPTCHA checkbox holds every press, never the fill (below, "A checkbox CAPTCHA"). The start date (a datepicker with no written format) stays free text.
- **BambooHR:** `<tenant>.bamboohr.com/careers/<id>`; root `form#job-application-form`; required by the label's `Mui-required`; its five Fabric dropdowns declared comboboxes (opened by `button.fab-SelectToggle`), whose menus no toggle names by ARIA: labelled, required, lazy, the person's; Submit `button[type=submit][form="job-application-form"]` outside the form; never Cancel, the shares, remove file, Clear Selection. Its LinkedIn URL box reads drawn since core pass Z2 (its wrapper holds an icon alone).
- **Rippling:** `ats.rippling.com/<board>/jobs/<uuid>` and its `/apply`; root `main#main-content form`; the phone's country code with "country code" after its label; custom questions titled by their block's `p`; the SMS consent's drawn radios a radio widget; uploads the hidden input inside its drop zone label, `div[data-testid=chip]` the file taken; Submit `button[data-testid=Apply]`, but its step never reads final: no snapshot shows what follows Apply (the progress bar stands at 50 % on every page seen), so Submit mode Auto never presses it and the person does. The posting: text `div.ATS_htmlPreview`, entry "Apply now"; the runtime never scans a posting address, so the footer's language picker is no field. Its text boxes, drawn on a padded wrapper, read drawn since core pass Z2.
- **Breezy HR:** `<tenant>.breezy.hr/p/<id>-<slug>/apply`; root `form[name=form]`; titles from each field's `h3`, the cover letter's header, Desired Salary's three controls (currency and period told apart by their `ng-model`), the SMS consent, the EEO questions; the EEO groups required by `ng-required`; the résumé `input#main-attachment` with its opener (no visible words name it: the person's), its upload area the input's own `div.file-input-container`, its sign and name the file link there (the grey "Attached" opener, elsewhere in the form, is no sign); errors an `error-container` without `ng-hide`; success `div.confirmation-container .application-confirmed`, which the form page holds hidden from the start (a press is armed with the signs shown at it: below, "Success signs").
- **CareerPlug:** `<tenant>.careerplug.com/jobs/<id>/apps/new` (never `app.careerplug.com`); root `form#new_app`; uploads titled by their group's for-less label; the paste editors excluded; the EEO block's section `p.eeoc-title`; Submit `input[name=commit]`. Its State select reads "States" since core pass Z2 (a select's prompt).
- **Pinpoint:** `<tenant>.pinpointhq.com/en/postings/<uuid>`, `/applications/new`, `/applications/thanks`; root `form#application-form`; required `.external-form__label--required`; each Yes/No question one group (`groupOf`); its react-select declared for the bridge; Submit `button[name=button]`; the posting's text and Apply Now; the confirmation by its address and `section#application-thanks`. Its panels fade in over 1 s: the first scan waits for it (below, "A form that fades in"). Since API 6 the agent takes Pinpoint.
- **Paycor:** `recruitingbypaycor.com/career/SubmitResume.action?clientId&id&specialization`; root `div.gnewtonSubmitResume` (both forms); the résumé's off-screen input with `#resumeDropping`, `#resumeDropped` and `#fileUploadErrorMessage` as its signs; Submit `button[ns-qa=submitBtn]`; never Clear All Fields, the drives, the job list.
- **HiBob:** `<tenant>.careers.hibob.com/jobs/<uuid>/apply`; root `careers-ui-job-ad-application-form`; Country (`b-single-select`) a declared combobox with no list seen (the person's); the Yes/No question titled by its `div.label`, its answer read from the wrapper's `selected`, the consent's from `aria-checked`; Submit `button#apply-to-job-btn`.

### The core passes: the run, the agent and the worker
- **A step's own Next whose words say Submit** (iCIMS; `Adapter.stepSubmitNext: { buttons, laterStep(doc) }`; `dom/fillers/not-submit.ts` `stepSubmitNextException`): a submit word does not refuse a click only when it is the step machine's Next press (`allowNext`), the element is exactly the adapter's `nextButton` and one of its declared per-step ids, `isFinalStep` reads `'not-final'`, and `laterStep` answers an element that is connected, in the button's own document, neither the button nor inside or around it, and visible. The adapter's Submit, a button holding or inside either, the never-pressed list and every other submit word stay refused; a hook that throws refuses. The behaviour review pinned each guard (`icims-core.browser.test.ts`: two current steps, counts that do not fit, a later step around or inside the button or in another document, a label naming it, a key's target).
- **An account section inside an application form** (`Adapter.accountSection(root)`; `src/dom/account-section.ts` `accountSectionOf`, `accountFormOf`, `accountLeft`): the element inside the form root holding the adapter's declared create-account form and nothing else of the application, where that form has a password box and every box of it is inside the section (else the step pauses as an account's creation, as before). `pause-detect.ts` leaves its boxes out of the sign-in reading; the adapter excludes it, so it is never filled from the profile. With Sign-in Auto the run types the saved sign-in (email, password, password again) into it once per step, before the rest of the form fills (`orchestrator.ts` `signInAccountSection`, `content/sign-in.ts` with `section: true`; the declared form must say `personPresses`; nothing is pressed); with Manual it is the person's. While one of its required boxes is empty, auto-advance refuses Next (`'account-left'`, notice `account-left`), and Submit mode Auto and the panel's Submit count those boxes as required-empty.
- **The frame that claims the application** (`Adapter.applicationFrame(doc)`; `content/chain-frame.ts`; `background/frames.ts`): a subframe claims the application (`claimsApplication`) only when its parent is the top frame, of its own origin, whose adapter names this very frame element as its application frame, and both addresses read to one key; the top frame is then a wrapper (`wraps`). The hello says `claimed` and `wraps`. `claimFrameOf` is the top frame when it draws its application itself, else the one subframe whose hello said `claimed` on the wrapper's origin and key (two: none). The agent never enters or starts a run in a wrapper; it acts in the claim frame (and the chain frames it frames: `agentFramesOf`), reads a report from it, and its retry goes to the top frame or a frame it acts in, never another subframe (S12). Sign-in Auto answers the claim frame (`sign-in.ts` `claims`), and the content side signs in where `isTop || claimed`. S-9, "commands only from the worker" and the frame-id checks are unchanged.
- **Success signs:** `content/submitted.ts` reads iCIMS's (above) and each configured platform's (`configuredSuccessSigns`). A press is armed with the signs a person can see at it (`shownSignsOf`, the behaviour review's M1; a configured platform's `configuredSuccessSigns` answers shown signs alone), so a confirmation the form page holds hidden from the start (Breezy's) counts once shown, and one already shown at the press is never that Submit's.
- **The address TailorIT is asked with** (`src/adapters/tailorit-address.ts` `tailoritAddressOf`, for every lookup, job add and applied report; `job-cards.ts` judges "Add this job" on it too): iCIMS's every page of a posting by `https://<host>/jobs/<id>/job`; SmartRecruiters' posting by `https://jobs.smartrecruiters.com/<Company>/<id>`, a one-click application by its canonical publication address (or, chained, its posting's); `jobs.workable.com` by `/view/<id>`; a chain page by the application the chain knows (`index.ts` `tailoritFrom`), else its origin and path alone. Every other page on an ATS host goes through `src/adapters/ats-address.ts` `atsAddressOf`, pure, the first match winning: TailorIT's own reading of the posting (`parseAtsUrl`'s canonical address: so Lever's `/apply` and Workday's apply steps are named by their posting page); else the platform parser's `applicationUrl`; else the origin and path with the query and fragment dropped (a `gh_jid` or `ashby_jid` kept: `originPathAndPosting`, shared with `site/index.ts`), where a path segment that looks like an id (a uuid, 16 or more hex digits, or 5 or more digits) after an application segment (`apply`, `application(s)`, `applicant(s)`, `candidate(s)`, `job-applications`) names the page by its longest shorter path rule 1 or 2 reads as a posting, else drops those segments (S11). So no query token, state, code, OTP, session parameter or per-application id goes to TailorIT (UltiPro's `AuthCode/Register?state=…`, ADP's `OTP_login`, Dice's and ClearCompany's application ids, SuccessFactors' `_s.crb`, a Greenhouse embed's `validityToken`). `url.test.ts` holds every address of its table on an ATS host to this.
- **A page whose address names no job takes the tab's chain** (`src/adapters/chain.ts` `chainPageOf`, `chainFamilyOf`, pure; `src/background/chain.ts`): a chain page is SuccessFactors' `/career` post-back with `_s.crb` and no job (not `/portalcareer`: minor 9), SmartRecruiters' one-click application and its screening step (top frames), and iCIMS's IForm frame on `<co>.icims.com/forms` (a subframe). Families: SuccessFactors per career host, SmartRecruiters per company, iCIMS per company (the portal hosts `careers-<co>`, `uscareers-<co>`, `us-careers-<co>` and the forms host `<co>.icims.com` share it) (S13).
  - **A top frame's chain:** the content script reads `document.referrer` in its isolated world and names it in its hello by a 16-hex hash only. The worker keeps, per tab in `storage.session` (`chain:<tabId>`), the last six claimed top documents (`CHAIN_HISTORY`): their address hash, key, canonical address, family, document and last hello's time. A chain page takes a record's key only when the hash equals its referrer's, in the same tab, platform and family, from another document, within `CHAIN_MS` (30 minutes) of that page's last hello; two records that could answer give none. A keyed chain page is recorded in turn (SmartRecruiters' screening step chains from its first page).
  - **A subframe's chain** (iCIMS's IForm; the security review's S4 replaced a token posted between frames, which told any page framing `/forms` that the extension is there): no frame posts anything to a page or a window. The chain frame's hello says only `chain: { frame: true }`; the worker keys it from its registry (`chains.inheritFrame`): the tab's claim frame gives its application only where it is the one frame of the tab, the asking one aside, holding an iCIMS application on a portal of the asking frame's company whose chain origin is the asking frame's origin, is neither chained nor a wrapper, and said hello within `CHAIN_MS`. The canonical posting address comes from the record the worker keeps when a portal frame holding an application says hello (`chains.notePortal`, `storage.session` `chainportal:<tabId>:<frameId>`), read only for the claim frame's current document. A forms frame that said hello first is keyed once it says hello again: the worker's sweep pings the tab's frames when a new document says hello (`frame/ping`). Two portal frames of the company in one tab give none (the registry knows a tab's frames, not which frames which).
  - **The result** (registry `chained`, `chainUrl`, `chainedFrom`): the hello's answer says `chained {applicationKey}`, and the content runtime reads its page again with that key while its address stays a chain page of the family. RunState and the posting's policy hits reach the form (a posting's "no AI" note becomes run/begin's `postingPage: 'hit'`), TailorIT is asked with the application's canonical address, and `submit/armed`/`detected` read the chained address. Never across tabs, hosts or companies, from a stale page, within one document, from a referrer that is no claimed page of the tab, or in a private tab.
- **The agent** (the behaviour review): an error the page showed before anything was written is the person's (minor 7: at a step's first scan in a document's first run, not resumed or continued, the adapter's visible errors are kept, `noteLoadErrors`; when the validation's errors are all among them the review adds the notice `errors-on-load`, and the agent reads it as needs-you `validation_error`, never failed: Oracle's profile item marked invalid on load is the case). After the job's Submit (`pressedAt`) a page with no form is never entered; its confirmation is awaited, then needs-you `other` (minor 13).
- **New log codes:** `content.account-section`, `content.chained`, `frame.chained`, `frame.chain-refused`, `content.sign-in-container` (flags and counts only).

### The core passes: what the page shows
- **The scanner across shadow roots** (`src/dom/composed.ts` `holdsComposed`, `composedOrder`): `adapterParts` keeps a widget root an adapter names where the form root holds it across open shadow roots (SmartRecruiters' six screening Yes/No groups are fields now), `anatomyOf` keeps a declared radio's members the same way (ADP's referral), the merge of controls and widget roots sorts in composed order (a host, its shadow tree, then its own children: Chromium's `compareDocumentPosition` orders nodes of two trees by no stable rule), and `dom/sections.ts` `precedingHeading` orders a heading and a field in composed order, so a field in a shadow root falls under the heading before its host, every time.
- **A file a field shows held** (`Adapter.uploadHeld(input)`): an empty file input whose field the page shows holding a file it took reads `{ kind: 'files', count: 1, held: true }` in every scan, before any upload is planned, and the planner follows the person's mode (below, "Résumé already on the page"); a file in the input itself (the person's, or the run's own earlier write) is never `held`. Workday, iCIMS and every configured platform with a `done` sign declare it. "An input the upload emptied is the upload taken" (LC-3) reads the written input's own files.
- **Uploads and the read-back** (`content/verify.ts`, `pass.ts`, `loop.ts`): a write records whether its input left the page (`WrittenField.inputLeft`); `verifyWritten` verifies a file of ours, written, that the page shows confirmed by name (`uploadState` 'confirmed') and whose own input left, never `field-disappeared` (Greenhouse); the loop's books keep its row when later scans miss it (`ScanInput.uploaded`, `TrackedField.taken`).
- **Visibility** (`src/dom/visibility.ts`): rule 5 reaches a box that moves with a container a person can scroll down (overflow-y auto or scroll, more content than it shows, at least `MIN_BOX_PX` tall) between it and its fixed ancestor within that container's scroll area (down to its scroll height, across within its width), the container's own visible part reachable in turn; the search follows containing blocks, so a box placed against the fixed box past the scroller is judged by the viewport, and a fixed container that does not scroll holds its content to the viewport as before. Rule 6 lifts a clip above such a scroller only where that clip shows the scroller's whole visible part vertically (half a pixel either way: the security review's S1, after a 4 px slit made a whole scroll area reachable), across only (`scrolledWithin`). jobs.workable.com's dialog reads in full; a slit still cuts.
- **A translucent layer over an image** (`src/dom/camouflage.ts`, `src/dom/color.ts`; `docs/design/runtime.md` §5.2): where no opaque layer gives the colours behind content, a translucent layer of plain colour painted wholly under the content, held to every rule an opaque one is (`translucentOver`), bounds what shows between the layer over black and over white, channel by channel (`Rgba.hi`, `between`); the farthest such layer is tried first. Content reads only where its least contrast over every colour in that range passes the existing threshold (`leastContrast`; judging the two ends alone is not enough: grey words between them vanish over a matching grey); a range that fails is 'unknown' (hidden), never 'camouflaged'. Where the backdrop comes from that bound, any box with a background that is not one of the words' ancestors, inside the layer the translucent ones lie on, overlapping a line of the words by `MIN_BOX_PX` on both axes, makes them 'unknown' as before the bound (`boxAtTheWords`, S8). Known gap P-1 (older than the bound): on an opaque panel, or with no image, compositing follows ancestors, not painted layers, so a positioned sibling in the words' colour under them is not seen (its test is an expected failure).
- **Words slotted into a shadow root** (`ownTextBox`): a slot's own text is the nodes assigned to it (else its fallback), not the slot's empty box (UltiPro's `ukg-label`); white words slotted on a white card still read camouflaged.
- **A wrapper drawn as the control's box** (`drawnByWrapper`, `onlyDecorations`): a bordered wrapper whose only other content is decoration (no text with a letter or digit, no native control or hidden input, nothing pressable; open shadow roots read too), whose content box hugs the control and its decorations within `WRAPPER_HUG_PX`, each decoration at most `WRAPPER_DECORATION_MAX_PX` (32 px) each way, the control spanning more than `WRAPPER_CONTROL_MIN_SHARE` (half) of the content box on each axis (S6). Rippling's padded boxes and BambooHR's adornment read drawn.
- **Labels** (`src/dom/labels.ts`): where a control's id repeats in its tree and it sits in one of the adapter's repeatable blocks (asked of the scan's root, `LabelContext.root`), its explicit labels are the block's own `label[for]` naming that id (`explicitLabels`, `optionLabelOf`: ClearCompany's rows). `Adapter.labelFirst` reads the adapter's label (with its suffix) before `label[for]`, `aria-labelledby` and a wrapping label where the hook says so (a hidden or empty one leaves the standard order: Jobvite's signature pair). For DOM-10 only, a single-choice select whose first option has an empty value and words that are its aria-label's own (a word and its plural alike: `selectPrompt`, `sameWords`) shows a visible placeholder, so its aria-label names it (CareerPlug's State); a prompt in other words ("Select...") does not.
- **A Yes/No pair of checkboxes** (`src/core/resolver/yesNoPair.ts`): a checkbox group of exactly two options whose first words are "yes" and "no" (`isYesNoPair`) is classified and valued as the Yes/No radio it asks (`askedAs`); the value is a set of the one matching box, written only where nothing else is ticked (a pair whose other box is ticked is flagged for the person, never unticked); the read-back (`dom/fillers/verify.ts` `holds`) holds both boxes.
- **The resolver** (`src/core/resolver`): an "I did not use AI" statement past the label's 300-character cut is read from the whole visible label the frame keeps (`ResolvedLabel.whole`, up to `TEXT_CAP_DESCRIPTION`; a flag `FieldSpec.statesNoAi` crosses, never the text) and the description, sentence by sentence (`statesNoAiUse`, S-4's rules), with the field's kind since the jr6 integration fixes (C-1, RULES item 22, SR-3: `dom/scan.ts` passes `kind: draft.kind` for a label past the cut too), so a long essay label's "in your own words" over a text box is an instruction and turns no AI off, while a checkbox's or a choice's statement still does; `AI_ATTESTATION` adds `NOT_MADE_BY_AI` (a denial in two joined participles, "not generated or substantially assisted by any AI tool"); a field the rules leave unclassified whose frame read such a statement is label.consent-ai's attestation (`noAiStatementHit`; on a list, the person's), and the frame goes profile-only (Dropzone's policy). A need for employer-sponsored work authorization is sponsorship (`SPONSORED_NEED`: ClassPass's "require employer-sponsored work authorization"). A company-knowledge true/false (SeatGeek's) is an ordinary question: no rule changed.
- **The first scan and a form that fades in** (`src/dom/entrance.ts`; `content/runtime.ts`): at start-up, where no adapter finds a form root on an application page, the runtime waits for the finite CSS animations and transitions running on elements that hold a field control, at most `ENTRANCE_CAP_MS` (2 s), then reads the page again (Pinpoint's 1 s fade-in); nothing is finished or paused, an endless one is not waited for, and a page whose form shows at once is not delayed.
- **Lists named only on open:** Workday's dropdown (`opensListbox`, above) is the one shape a snapshot shows; Dice's, HiBob's and BambooHR's lists are in no snapshot open (BambooHR's leftover menus are tied to their toggles by `data-menu-id` alone), so no mechanism was added for them.

### The core passes: presses, sign-in and CAPTCHAs
- **A button bound to an invisible CAPTCHA is no drawn challenge** (`dom/pause-detect.ts` `drawnWidget`, `boundButton`): besides a `<button>`, an `<input>` or a link carrying `.g-recaptcha` (or another vendor's class), an element whose own role is `button`, carrying `g-recaptcha` or `h-captcha`, with an accessible name of its own (its `aria-label`, `aria-labelledby` words or its text), a button's box (drawn, at most `BOUND_BUTTON_MAX_HEIGHT_PX`, 64 px, tall: under the vendors' 78 px checkbox) and no frame in its light DOM or open shadow roots, is an invisible binding (S7): ADP's guest Continue, so its page fills. A nameless host, one as tall as a widget, a visible checkbox widget, a shown challenge frame, and the vendors that bind to no button (Turnstile, PerimeterX's `#px-captcha`) still count. Known limit: a named, button-sized host whose closed shadow root draws a widget is not seen.
- **The invisible reCAPTCHA badge holds no press** (`challengeLikeFramesShown`, the behaviour review's B1): a frame inside `.grecaptcha-badge`, and every frame on a vendor's challenge host, is left to the vendor rules (`unsolvedCaptchas`, which knows a solved widget; `vendorFramesShown` for Sign-in Auto); a visible frame of an unknown origin that reads like a challenge still counts, and the badge's challenge popup (its bframe) counts once shown.
- **A checkbox CAPTCHA holds every press, never the fill** (the owner's decision, the behaviour review's M6): each challenge `shownCaptchas` finds says whether it is a checkbox widget: reCAPTCHA v2's anchor at `https://www.google.com/recaptcha/api2/anchor?...&size=normal` or hCaptcha's `.../hcaptcha.html#frame=checkbox` (`checkboxFrame`), in a container whose visible frames are all such frames, at least one (`checkboxWidget`; reCAPTCHA's hidden helper frame is not counted; a container showing no frame is not known to be one). `detectPause` no longer pauses for such a widget not yet solved: the step fills. `unsolvedCaptchas` and `pageChallenged` still count it, so every press holds until the person solves it (its token in the vendor's field): auto-advance's Next (`captcha-unsolved`, notice `captcha-next`), Submit mode Auto (`submit-held-captcha`), the panel's Next and Submit (`blocked`), the agent's entry (`captcha`), a sign-in container's press and Sign-in Auto (`vendorFramesShown`, which counts the anchor solved or not); the agent maps a widget left at the Submit to needs-you `captcha`. A shown challenge popup (reCAPTCHA's bframe, hCaptcha's `#frame=challenge`), an enterprise or compact anchor, a container with no frame, Turnstile, Arkose and every other vendor still pause the fill. On an account page the adapter names (SuccessFactors'), the pause is the account's. A vendor's response field (`g-recaptcha-response`, `h-captcha-response`, `cf-turnstile-response`) is never a field, shown or not; a widget rendered into the page's own element is known solved by the parent of its response field (`responseParentOf`). The run never touches the widget. (Since v5 Phase 1 no press holds for such a widget: `pageChallenged` and `vendorFramesShown` leave it out, the site decides, and a refusal for it pauses the run: "v5 Phase 1", "CAPTCHA like Jobright".)
- **A declared sign-in container** (`Adapter.signInContainer(doc)`, `dom/sign-in-form.ts` `signInContainerOf`; configured `PageState.signInContainer: { container, email?, terms?, button }` on a state with a root and no auth): on a page filled like a step, its account or code-sending part as a `SignInForm` whose `container` stands in for the adapter's own `<form>`. `signInRefusal` holds it (`containerShape`): only a declared form names one, never beside a `form`; no password or confirmation box in it; every box in it of one form owner; the `<form>` its press would send (`containerOwner`) is the container or inside it and held to the POST and same-origin rules; the button the one the adapter names, in the container's document, a press control, a native one of no other form; it may hold no box. `guardSubmission` guards the container's `<form>` where it has no box (UltiPro's); ADP's has none to guard. The door (`clickAdapterSignIn(..., 'container')`) refuses a button in another document and reads the never-press list and the form's own Submit and Next across shadow roots (`flatContains`). Sign-in Auto asks the worker as at an email gate (`CONTAINER_WALL`), so the password never leaves it; it types the saved email only into a declared email box (neither adapter declares one), ticks declared terms and presses through the door, with every guard of a wall's sign-in. The run (`orchestrator.ts` `signInContainerNow`) presses once per step, when it rests at review, only with Sign-in acting as Auto, not in a dry run, in the top or claim frame, with no required question left, none of the container's required boxes empty (`containerLeft`), no error shown and no challenge; the page the press brings is followed by the run's watch (ADP's code dialog pauses as an email code). Manual leaves it to the person. The behaviour review pinned each gate (`sign-in.browser.test.ts`, `account-section.browser.test.ts`).
- **The agent's entry may submit a consent form** (`Adapter.entryConsentForm(doc)`; configured `PageState.entrySubmits: { form, selects? }` on an entry-choice state with an entry): `clickAdapterEntry` still never presses a control that submits a form, except where `consentEntryAllowed` holds: the adapter reads `'entry-choice'`, its declared consent form is the control's own form owner (through the prototype), and that form's `elements` (a `form=` attribute's controls counted) are only that control, the declared selects and fieldsets. Any application field, hidden input, other button or undeclared select refuses it (Jobvite's "I Accept").
- **The adapter's Submit, Next and never-pressed list across shadow roots** (`not-submit.ts` `refusalOf`, `submitException`: `flatContains`, S9): a native button inside a declared host's shadow root is that host (UltiPro's Submit).
- **The own adapter's page state holds the page** (M3, above under the configured layer).

### Résumé already on the page (the owner's mode, settings version 5)
The owner's decision of 2026-11-06 (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §12 Decision 18): re-uploading over a résumé the page already shows is the person's setting.
- **The choice:** `modes.resumeOnPage`, `keep` ("Keep it", the default) or `upload` ("Upload mine again"), on Options › Modes and in the side panel's gear (`src/ui/page/modes.tsx` `SHOWN_MODES`, one component for both). The planner reads it in the worker; content scripts do not need it.
- **What a held field is:** a file field the page shows holding a file it took while its input holds none (`CurrentValue` `files` with `held: true`, from `Adapter.uploadHeld`: Workday's, iCIMS's and the configured layer's).
- **Keep it:** the held file is kept and flagged `prefill-kept`, as every held file was before.
- **Upload mine again** (`src/core/plan/frame.ts` `uploadsHeldResume`): a held field the resolver reads as the résumé (`file.resume`) is planned like an empty one, and the person's chosen résumé goes through the one upload path (`pass.ts` `writeField`, the file filler, `awaitUpload`), the page's own input taking it as it does (Workday's adds it beside the shown file; Rippling's replaces its chip). A held cover letter or other file stays kept. The page's Remove and Delete stay never pressed.
- **No file input offered:** a held résumé whose input is disabled is flagged `resume-held-no-input` by the planner (`needsUserAction`), and so is one whose input the write finds withdrawn (disabled or gone: `guardRefusal`); a hidden input the page disables or removes leaves the scan (`dom/proxy.ts`), and its row is the loop's `field-disappeared`. Nothing is written either way. Where a held résumé shows no input at all (iCIMS hides it with its buttons; Oracle's attached résumé has none), no field is offered under either choice.
- **Settings:** version 5 (`src/core/types/settings.ts`): `migrateSettingsV4` keeps every choice and adds Keep it, versions 1 to 3 migrating through it; the worker (`background/settings.ts`) migrates a stored version 4 value and stores it again (`settings.migrated`, `to: 5`); `mergeSettings` carries the mode field by field. The stored versions' schemas (V1 to V4), which only the worker reads to migrate, moved from `src/shared/settings.ts` (which the content script imports) to `src/shared/settings-stored.ts`, keeping the content script under its 1 MiB budget.

### The page-world bridge's size
The bridge's install decision moved, unchanged in what it answers, to `src/adapters/bridge-address.ts` (`bridgeInstallsAt`, `readableAncestors`, `framedByEmployerPage`, `PUBLIC_JOB_HOSTS`), re-exported by `adapters/address.ts`. It reads only `SHARED_DOMAIN_PARSERS`, the parsers of the sixteen platforms with a candidate host outside the public job hosts (Workday, Workable, iCIMS, Taleo, SuccessFactors, Oracle, ADP, ClearCompany, Dice, Paylocity, JazzHR, BambooHR, Breezy, CareerPlug, Pinpoint, Paycor): a public host installs before any parser is read, and a frame's readable ancestors are of its own origin, so of its own platform. A "host and path rules only" version was not clean (on the shared domains the decision depends on each parser's posting-or-form reading: iCIMS's `/jobs/search` against `/jobs/<id>/job`, Taleo's `jobsearch.ftl` against `jobdetail.ftl`, Paycor's `CareerHome.action` against `SubmitResume.action`). `url.test.ts` checks every address of its table, and every same-origin pair, against the reading with every parser, and that every shared-domain platform is listed. The bundle is about 21 KB (20,921 bytes at core pass Z1; it was 28,897 with every parser), under check-build's 24 KiB `BRIDGE_BUDGET` (`test/check-build.test.ts` matches).

### Copy
`COPY.panel.unsupported.sites` notes: Workday "Its application steps are filled; dates and search lists are left to you." (since snapshot round 2: "Its application steps are filled, dates and search lists included."), iCIMS "Its application steps are filled; its own dropdowns are left to you.", Oracle "Its application form is filled; its lists are left to you."; Taleo and SuccessFactors keep "Sign-in and posting pages; its application forms are coming."; SmartRecruiters and the fifteen new ATSs carry none; Workable's hosts include jobs.workable.com, Oracle's the government cloud. The review's notices gain `account-left` and `errors-on-load`, the reasons `resume-held-no-input`, and Options › Modes "Résumé already on the page" (Keep it / Upload mine again; drafts). "Record applications I submit myself" names the sites that show a submission went through (Greenhouse, Lever, Ashby, Workable, iCIMS, Breezy HR and Pinpoint).
