On the page, `action.onClicked` (`WorkerPlatform.action`): a page a script of ours may draw on (`iconReachesPage`: an ATS host's, a company's own https page by `isCompanySiteUrl`, the e2e fixture origin; never the Chrome Web Store) gets its launcher shown again where the person hid it (site, everywhere, next visit) and `sidebar/open {reveal}` at frame 0. Where no content script answers, a company page's top document gets one first (`sites.ts` `injectTop`: only while Company career sites act, never in a private tab, the address read again from the frame by `scripting.topDocument`, recorded first as page/recognised's injections are) and is asked again up to `ICON_OPEN_TRIES` times while it starts. An ATS tab no content script answers (loaded before an install or update) gets the static one in its top document (the review's F7, `sites.ts` `injectAts`: after reading again from the frame that its address is an ATS page; never in a private tab; nothing recorded, and the script's own `RUNNING` guard keeps a second copy from running). TailorIT's own https origin is never a company page (the review's S11): excluded from the sentinel's registration (`registrationsFor({sites, own})`) and refused by page/recognised and `injectTop` (`onOwnOrigin`); the development origin is http, so nothing changes there today. Any other page (a browser page, the Web Store, LinkedIn, an HR system, an http page off the ATS hosts) gets Chrome's side panel inside the click; a page that still answers nothing gets it too, which Chrome may refuse then (the gesture is gone).
- **The header's notes:** `panel/notes` and `panel/closeNote {note: 'workday'}` (page messages), kept in `storage.local` `notes:v1` (the note's name only).
- `reports.ts`: `report/update` goes to an in-page card under `'page'` (an embedded frame's); the sidebar reads the tab's reports through its `panel` port.

### The page (`content/runtime.ts`, `content/ui/{edge,sidebar,push,page-ui}.ts`; since the review also `content/ui/{shadow,visible}.ts`, `dom/visibility.ts`)
- **The pill** (`edge.ts`): a white horizontal pill at the right edge, 56 px high, the launcher's 40 px tile and a teal grip, `EDGE_TUCK_PX` (24) past the edge until the pointer is over it or the focus is in it (a 2D move over 0.5 s; none under reduced motion), its "×" a 20 px bubble at its top-left only then (its click counted 600 ms after it showed), the hide menu as before (its items in Title Case). The whole pill drags up and down (a trusted press on its seen launcher or grip; past `EDGE_DRAG_PX`, 4, it is a drag, and the click that ends it opens nothing: `Edge.endedDrag`); the grip's arrow keys and the saved place as before. No badge (`.edge .badge` hidden); its name is "Open TailorIT Autofill" whatever the state. Its click opens the panel (the sidebar under `'page'`, `panel/open` inside the click under `'side'`) and never starts a fill in the top frame. It shows on a supported page with or without a form (`PageUi.setPageShown`: a posting, its form, a job page; never the platform's page after submitting) and hides while the sidebar is open.
- **The sidebar's frame** (`sidebar.ts`): `aside[data-sidebar]` in the page UI's closed root, `SIDEBAR_WIDTH_PX` (384), fixed from top to bottom at the right edge, white, a 1 px inset line and its shadow (`IN_PAGE_SHADOWS.sidebar`), sliding in, scrolling on its own (`.sidebar__body`). A capture gate on its clicks: an untrusted one refused; a trusted one only once the frame was seen without a break for `CONTROL_SEEN_MS`, landed inside it, its body and on the control (`landed.ts`, shared with the card). The review's fixes to it:
  - **S2:** a click on a step's or the main button (`[data-step-action]`, `[data-main-action]`: Continue, Submit Application, Submit Anyway, Autofill) counts only once that button itself was seen `CONTROL_SEEN_MS` as drawn: a MutationObserver on the container watches each as the views draw it and starts its time again when its action, its words or `hidden` change (never `disabled`, which the views' own hold decides: "The views"); one never watched never counts.
  - **S3:** a click counts only while the frame stands where it is drawn (`inPlace`: its right edge at the root's `clientWidth`, its top at 0, `SIDEBAR_WIDTH_PX` wide or the window's width, the window's height, 1 px of slack; a page CSS zoom within 0.5 to 1.5 scales the width). Its box is read every `BOX_CHECK_MS` (100) while it is open, and its seen time starts again whenever the box changes or is out of place. The host itself: `shadow.ts` puts back the host's own style at once when a page restyles it (its style attribute, or an attribute it adds, which goes), logs `content.ui-restyled`, and calls its `onRestyled` listeners: the page UI (the seen time of every control it watches starts again, `visible.ts` `resetAll`) and the applied window (its two buttons').
  - **S4:** a page's `document.execCommand` edits the focused box with trusted input events. An `input` in a sidebar text box counts only after the person's own trusted keydown, paste, cut, drop, composition or beforeinput on that box, in the same task (execCommand fires no beforeinput); any other is stopped before the views hear it and the box gets back the value it held (log `content.ui-input-refused`). Chrome's own autofill into a sidebar box is undone the same way.
  - A trusted, seen click on a link to the options page or to Chrome's side panel goes to the worker (`sidebar/options`, `panel/open`) inside the click.
  - The views mount on opening and unmount on folding (their ports closed); their Minimize (`SidebarTarget.minimise`) folds it back to the pill; choosing Chrome's side panel closes it.
- **Opening** (`runtime.ts` `openSidebar`, `autoOpen`, `pageShownNow`): under `'page'` the top frame's sidebar opens by itself once per document on a supported page under Expanded, unless the launcher is hidden on the site or everywhere, never in a hidden tab (the review's S1: there it opens once the tab is shown, `visibilitychange`), and never in the agent's tab (`view` minimized there); it then mounts its views `byItself`, so its job card is asked for as a page that loads ("The worker"); the pill's click and the worker's `sidebar/open` open it on any page of ours. The bundle is asked for once per document (`sidebar/load`; its answer names the tab). An embedded frame keeps its corner launcher (whose click still starts a fill, its badge the percent) and its in-page card (`openCard`, its views loaded the same way), or Chrome's side panel where the frame is too small.
- **The page push** (`push.ts`): while the sidebar is open, `html[data-tailorit-sidebar]` and one document stylesheet (adopted, built with the page's own `CSSStyleSheet`, which a page CSP does not govern; a `<style>` in the head where the browser cannot adopt one) pad the body on the right by 384 px; the visible fixed elements are marked `data-tailorit-push`: `span` (from the left edge under the sidebar: held to the page's part), `shift` (a smaller one on the right, moved left) or `wide` (in flow, wider than the body now leaves: held to it). Never a form, a form control, anything aria-hidden, hidden, see-through or under 40 × 8 px (a honeypot never is), nor our host; since the review (S5, F6) nor an element wholly past the window's right edge (an off-canvas drawer the shift would bring in), one an ancestor's overflow clips to nothing (each read carries its ancestors' clip down), or one that holds a CAPTCHA widget or a vendor's frame (`isCaptchaVendorUrl`). It reads at most `PUSH_SCAN_CAP` (1,500 since F6; 4,000 cost too much on busy pages) elements, `PUSH_SCAN_DEPTH` (6) deep, at most once per `PUSH_SYNC_MS` (500 ms), a read that took long holding the next back `PUSH_COST_FACTOR` (20) times its own length, on animation frames after the page's own element changes or a resize, and never while a fill runs. Closing removes every mark and the stylesheet. No per-host fixes yet.
- **What the fill counts as on screen** (the review's S5): while the sidebar is open `page-ui.ts` calls `dom/visibility.ts` `coverRight(view, SIDEBAR_WIDTH_PX)` (0 when it closes), so rule 5 of the visibility checks reads the window as that much narrower: nothing in the strip the sidebar covers is on screen to the run.
- **Toasts** sit left of the sidebar while it is open (`SIDEBAR_CLEARANCE_PX`, 32, clear of its shadow).

### The views (`ui/panel/{Panel,Sheet,Modals}.tsx`, `v5-styles.ts`; `ui/report/Report.tsx`, `model.ts`; `ui/page/workday-note.tsx`; `ui/brand/font.ts`)
- **The head:** `PanelProps.shell` (the sidebar's) gives the gear and Minimize (`data-panel-minimise`). Over every view's head, on a Workday page (`PageState.platform`), the amber note "Please stay on this page while Autofill fills the form." with a ×, closed for good (`panel/closeNote`), in Chrome's side panel too. No feedback button: TailorIT has no endpoint for it (Phase 3).
- **The main view** fills its host's height (`height: var(--tia-panel-height, 100vh)`): the head, the stage (`.tia-stage`, scrolling in `.tia-stage__scroll`), the progress sheet over the stage's bottom, then the step dock (`StepDock`, drawn by the panel under the stage, no longer inside the report), whose button takes no click until what it says (its frame, its target, Continue or Submit, or Submit Anyway after a required-empty answer) has been shown `CONTROL_SEEN_MS`, disabled meanwhile (the review's S2: `ui/panel/seen.ts` `useShownFor` on the container's clock; the in-page card's report passes its own), so a click aimed at Continue never presses a Submit drawn under the pointer, and a double-click on Submit never answers the question it raised.
- **The progress sheet** (`section.tia-dashboard.tia-sheet[data-open]`; `Report`'s `sheet?: SheetControl {open, onToggle?}` and `wall?`): up while a run works (scanning, filling, or Sign-in Auto signing in for it), sliding (0.35 s; none under reduced motion) to just under the job card (`.tia-sheet-anchor`, measured by ResizeObserver and the stage's scroll; never less than `SHEET_MIN_UP_PX`, 240, of it showing); up when the run rests and when the panel opens on a run at rest (`useSheetOpen`); its header (`button[data-action="toggle-sheet"]`) folds it at rest to a `SHEET_STRIP_PX` (56) strip ("7/9 Required Fields Filled | 77%" and a chevron) and brings it back; no fold while a run works (Cancel then). The header reads the run status when nothing is counted (no "0/0"). While a run waits for the person's Continue (a hand-off, a sign-in at rest; never while Sign-in Auto signs in) the header offers Continue (`.tia-progress-line button[data-action="continue"]`, `Report`'s `onContinue`, held `CONTROL_SEEN_MS`), which sends `panel/continue`, since the sheet lies over the main button; the sheet stays up so what the run waits for stays in view (the review's F4; the dock's Continue stays). Without `sheet` the in-page card's report is as before.
- **Entries** (`model.ts` `sheetListsOf`, `blockOf`, `entryKindOf`): Education and Work Experience fields in a block of their section are entries under it (`section.tia-entries[data-section-kind]`): the heading with the run's count chip ("2/3 Added", `button[data-action="toggle-entries"]`, unfolded at first), each `li.tia-entry[data-entry-place]` ("Education 1") with Go To (`button[data-action="go-to-entry"]`: `panel/focus` of its first field) and its rows, then the profile's entries the form shows no block for (`[data-not-added]`, "Not Added"). A repeatable count with no rows stays a line ("Languages 1/2 Added"); Required and Optional list the rest; every row is counted as before. Labels, categories, section keys and counts only.
- **The account step** (`model.ts` `accountStepOf`, `li.tia-row--step[data-account-step]`): a run at `auth-needed` shows "Signing In" (or "Creating Account" at a create-account wall), a spinner while Sign-in Auto signs in for it (the header says so, with no percent), an empty circle while the page waits for the person (its toast says what).
- **The modals** (`Modals.tsx`: `.tia-modal` inside the main view, never over the page; a 320 px `[role="alertdialog"][aria-modal]`): raised by `Panel.tsx` `stopOf` from the main button's answer: `panel/fill` reaching no frame on a page with no form (`no-form`) or with one (`unavailable`), a refusal other than not_connected or rate_limited (`failed`), or, where the host passes `refreshPage` (the sidebar) and the runtime is invalid, `updated` (Refresh Page), raised by itself too, once, when the link is gone under such a host. Try Again sends the same message again. The history check (`historyGaps`: whether `experience` and `education` hold any entry, never an entry; since the review's S6 worked out by the worker and answered by name in `panel/profileCheck`'s `history`, "Profile completeness" in v4): a `panel/fill` (never a Continue) with either missing opens Cancel, Edit Autofill Information (the editor, its Back to the main view) and Autofill Anyway (the held message). The actions wait `CONTROL_SEEN_MS` (`seen.ts` `useShownFor`); the focus starts on Cancel and Tab cycles; Escape closes and the focus goes back.
- **The look:** Inter (`ui/brand/fonts/inter-latin-normal-var.woff2`, the variable upright Latin face, TailorIT's own OFL file) under the family "TailorIT Inter" (`palette.ts` `INTER_FAMILY`, first in `FONT_STACK`), added to the document by `ui/brand/font.ts` `addInter` from the bundle's bytes (`FontFace` from an ArrayBuffer: nothing fetched, no `font-src`): the sidebar's page, the side panel and the options page; the system font elsewhere. On a page the face goes again when the sidebar unmounts (`removeInter`, the review's S10). The labels that start or send something (`[data-main-action]`, `[data-step-action]`, `[data-modal-action]`, I Understand, `.tia-cta`, a toast's action) are drawn in `GENERIC_FONT` (`palette.ts`: `system-ui, sans-serif`; the review's S9), never a face a page could add under our font's name.

### The applied window (`content/ui/applied.ts`; `background/submissions.ts`)
Once POST /applied recorded the tab's application (a Try again's too; never the agent's own tab, never one not recorded or refused), `SubmissionsDeps.applied(tabId)` has `index.ts` send the tab's top frame `applied/popup`; `runtime.ts` takes it from the worker's own script alone (`fromWorker`), in the top frame alone, and calls `showApplied(doc, {trackerUrl})`. A native modal `<dialog>` (showModal) in a closed shadow root of its own (`createUiHost`; its sheet `APPLIED_CSS` replacing the page UI's): TailorIT's mark and name, ×, our own drawing (`SUCCESS_DRAWING`), "Application Submitted!", "This job was added to your TailorIT tracker.", "Go To Application Tracker" (TailorIT's `/tracker`, `window.open` with `noopener` inside the click, then closed). Trusted clicks only, each counted once the button has been seen `CONTROL_SEEN_MS`; Escape (the dialog's trusted cancel) closes; Tab cycles × and the button. A hidden tab shows it at `visibilitychange` to visible, never after `pagehide`; one at a time per document. It carries no data.

### Edit with AI (`content/ui/ai-edits.ts`)
The box opens above its button (`data-place="above"`; under it only where the window has no room above). Generate closes the box at once; the button reads "Generating…" (`data-state="busy"`, disabled) until the rewrite's outcome; a rewrite written, or the same text, rests it (`idle`); a failure, refusal or sensitive screen turns it red (`data-state="failed"`: the danger tone, "Try Again", aria-label `aiEdit.retryLabel(why)`) until the next Generate, and its click opens the box again, whose status says why. Its words show on hover or focus, while busy, open or red. It is drawn only on a box at least `AI_EDIT_MIN_BOX_PX` (40) tall: a one-line box the run offers keeps it hidden. Its states' sheet (`AI_EDIT_STATES_CSS`) goes only into a shadow root (the layer's root), never the page's document; `content/ui/styles.ts` `applyStyles` assigns `adoptedStyleSheets`, so calling it again on that root after the layer exists would drop the states. Not built: "at any time" (before a run, or while one fills): the rewrite still needs a resting run.

### Copy (`src/shared/copy.ts`, its `// v5 p1-shell` and `// v5 p1-panel` blocks)
- Title Case (PLAN decision 3) for buttons and headings: the report, the main view, set-up, the Settings view, the job card, Ask, the profile editor's sections and Add buttons, the dock ("Continue To The Next Page", "Submit Application", "Submitted!"), "Application Submitted", Edit with AI ("Edit With AI", "Generating…"), the hide menu, the pill's name and Minimize. The settings' names and helps stay sentence case ("Where the panel shows"). New: `report.{signingIn, creatingAccount, partial, partialLabel, sectionToggle, entry, goToButton, goToEntry, notAdded, showSheet, hideSheet}`, `aiEdit.{retry, retryLabel}`, `applied.*` and `sidebar.*` (PAGE_COPY), `modals.*` (COPY); the review's S6 and S7 added `onPage.{openSidePanel, sensitive, disclosure, raise, preview}` (COPY), drafts for the owner.
- **PAGE_COPY**, the words the content script carries, no longer holds `reasons`, `statuses`, `provenances` and `stages`: they moved to COPY (no page UI draws them since v4; the in-page card's views come with the sidebar's bundle, which carries COPY). `SENSITIVE_NOT_SHARED` and `ARRANGEMENTS` are named once, since a member read of REASONS or JOB inside OPTIONS or PROFILE kept those whole blocks in the content bundle.

### CAPTCHA like Jobright (`dom/pause-detect.ts`; `content/{press,sign-in,orchestrator}.ts`; `core/steps/machine.ts`)
It holds over "The core passes: presses, sign-in and CAPTCHAs" (the checkbox CAPTCHA holding every press) and "Toasts in place of the notices" (the challenge check first).
- **No hold for a checkbox widget:** `pageChallenged` counts only challenges that are no checkbox widget (`shownCaptchas(...)`, a widget not a checkbox) or a frame that reads like one; `vendorFramesShown` leaves a checkbox widget's own frame out (`checkboxFrame`); `unsolvedCaptchas` still counts every widget; `checkboxCaptchasUnsolved` is new. So Next (auto-advance's and the panel's), Submit (Submit mode Auto's and the panel's), the agent's entry (`pressEntry`, `pressEntryChoice`), the sign-in prelude (`not-submit.ts` `clickAdapterSignInPrelude`), Sign-in Auto (`sign-in.ts` `signInChallenged` = `pageChallenged || vendorFramesShown`) and a sign-in container's press all press beside an unticked reCAPTCHA v2 or hCaptcha checkbox; the site decides. A pop-up (the bframe, `#frame=challenge`), an Enterprise or compact anchor, a frameless container, Turnstile, Arkose and every other vendor still pause the fill (`detectPause`, unchanged) and hold every press. The widget is never touched.
- **The site's CAPTCHA-required error** (`isCaptchaError`: an error whose id, class, name or first 300 characters read `captcha`, `not a robot`, `are/be human` or `human check/verification`; `captchaErrorsShown`: the adapter's errors, and the alerts, `role=alert` or `aria-live=assertive`, on the root or the body): JazzHR declares `#recaptcha-required-error` in its `errors` (`adapters/jazzhr/config.ts`, from Jobright 1.25.2's submission rules, not seen in a snapshot). It counts in `press.ts` `refusalNow` while a box is unticked (so the runtime's arm ends the Submit's wait), and is left out only once the page shows at least one checkbox widget holding its token and none unticked (`withoutSolvedCaptchaErrors`, `checkboxCaptchasSolved`: stale, as the site clears it only at the next press), there and in the run's `errorsNow`. On a page that shows no checkbox widget (an invisible or a score-based reCAPTCHA) it stands as any error (the review's F3).
- **The refusal** (`captchaRefusalShown(doc, root, adapter, {afterPress})`: a checkbox widget unticked over or inside `root`, the pressed step's form, and either the site's error or `afterPress`; since the review's F8 never one elsewhere on the page, a footer newsletter's: `checkboxCaptchasUnsolved(doc, root, cache)`, its root second). In the run (`orchestrator.ts`, its `// v5 p1-rules` blocks): at validation (`validateStep`, never a dry run), a Next that left the step as it was (lastFill cleared: our `TIMED_OUT`, the person's `STEP_UNCHANGED`), or the site's error shown, gives `PAUSE {reason: 'captcha', refused: true}`; at rest (`refusedForCheck(kind: 'refused' | 'unconfirmed' | 'next')`, which reads the page each time: F8, no sticky answer), a refused Submit (`submitRefused`), one unconfirmed within `SUBMIT_CONFIRM_MS` (`submitUnconfirmed`) and the panel's Next answered not accepted give that pause instead of submit-errors or submit-unconfirmed (a watch at rest ends with it, `checkRefused`; a run still working takes it as it next rests, `refusedLate`; else `pauseAtRest`), and Submit mode Auto's once-per-step mark (`autoPressKey`) is released. An unconfirmed Submit is the check's only when the step's form showed the box unticked as it was pressed (`noteCheckAtSubmit`, read right before `pressStep`, for the panel's Submit and Submit mode Auto's) or the site shows its CAPTCHA error; otherwise it is submit-unconfirmed and the once-per-step mark stays (the review's F2: an AJAX Submit that went through and then reset its widget is never pressed twice). The pause shows the existing challenge toast; `watchPauseGone` reads `pauseShownNow` (the page's pause, else the refusal while the box is unticked), so the run carries on once the person ticks it (scan, fill, validate, and Next or Submit again where the modes say). Log code `content.captcha-refused`.
- **The machine:** `PAUSE` takes `refused?: true`; a CAPTCHA pause is taken at `review` too; a refused one gives the step back the attempt its press used, so the resumed run may press again (each such press needing the person's tick: never a loop by itself). `StepFacts.captchaUnsolved` now means a challenge that is no checkbox widget, or a bot check.
- **Sign-in Auto** types and presses beside an unticked box. `outcomeAfter`: an error in the form, the site's CAPTCHA error, or nothing within the wait, while a box is unticked over or inside the sign-in's own form (F8), is `'challenged'`; the boxes keep what was typed for the person's own press, `SIGN_IN_KEPT_MS` (180 s, `SignInContext.keptMs`) at most (the review's S8, `keepForPerson`): the password boxes are written empty then, or at once when the sign-in's signal ends (the run's Stop or Cancel, its end, the document going), but a box the person typed into since (a trusted input; a page's own input event never counts) is theirs and left as it is; the email stays. The document is recorded (`refusedFor`), and `signInRefusalWaits(doc)` keeps the run's pause watch from trying again on that wall (the worker answers a tab once per wall): the person ticks the box and presses the site's button, and the run carries on once the page moves on. The agent's entry still reads `'challenged'` as block `captcha`; a refused press is the hand-off CAPTCHA pause, needs-you `captcha` (`background/agent.ts`, comments only).

### Every site at install (`wxt.config.ts`, `shared/hosts.ts`, `background/sites.ts`, `ui/options/Options.tsx`)
It holds over "v3 Phase 8: company career sites" (its manifest and Options) and "Manifest".
- **The manifest:** `host_permissions` = `ALL_SITES_HOST_PERMISSIONS` (`https://*/*`, `http://*/*`) and TailorIT's origin; no `optional_host_permissions` (the built manifest's hosts are exactly those three). The static content scripts are unchanged (the ATS list, both).
- **Company career sites on by default:** `chosen()` reads `sites:v1` as on unless `{sites: false}`; `active()` reads Chrome's permission first (a worker with no site access reads no more storage than before); `set(false)` gives no permission back (a required one). Options (`WhereItWorks`): turning it on still calls `requestHosts([SITES_MATCH])` inside the click (Chrome answers at once unless the person withheld site access), and a choice that could not be saved gives nothing back. Copy: `COPY.options.sites.intro`, `COPY.panel.unsupported.companySites`; the side panel on an https page with no form says "No Application Form Found Here Yet" with the reload hint.
- **Still https only for company pages:** the sentinel's registration matches `https://*/*`, `isCompanySiteUrl` requires https, and `senders.ts` `offAtsSenderOf` refuses http frames (a company's http form would send the answers unencrypted). The http permission serves the toolbar icon and the panel, and TailorIT's own origin.
- **Vendor frames** (`shared/hosts.ts` `CAPTCHA_VENDOR_MATCHES`, `isCaptchaVendorUrl`): `*://*.google.com/recaptcha/*`, `*://*.recaptcha.net/*`, `*://*.hcaptcha.com/*`, `*://challenges.cloudflare.com/*`, `*://*.arkoselabs.com/*`, `*://*.funcaptcha.com/*`, `*://*.captcha-delivery.com/*`, `*://*.protechts.net/*`, `*://*.geetest.com/*`: excluded from the sentinel's registration and refused by `isCompanySiteUrl` (the sentinel's recognition, the worker's injection checks, `injectTop`, the site adapter); no static content script's host is one of them.

### Disclosure version 10 (`shared/stored.ts` `DISCLOSURE_VERSION`, `COPY.options.connection.disclosure`)
docs/PRIVACY.md holds it word for word (`src/shared/copy.test.ts`): the panel on the page (at the right edge, opening by itself, moving the page aside, its contents out of the site's scripts' reach though the site can tell it shows, the page's key and input listeners hearing what is typed into it, Chrome's side panel a choice, the toolbar icon), every https and http page granted at install and Company career sites on unless turned off, never in a CAPTCHA frame, and the CAPTCHA presses (never ticked or solved; pressed beside an unticked box, the site deciding; a site's request for the check waited out and carried on after; a refused sign-in left typed for the person; a pop-up check pausing). "Its side panel" became "its panel" throughout. The review's fixes changed two phrases, the version still 10: the company page's address goes to TailorIT "when its panel opens there (by itself, or because you open it)" (S1), and a sign-in it typed stays in the form "for three minutes at most" (S8).

- Host permissions exactly `https://*/*`, `http://*/*` and TailorIT's origin (never `<all_urls>`, file, ftp or websockets: `BEYOND_WEB_PAGES`); `optional_host_permissions` absent; no static content script matching a vendor frame (`VENDOR_ADDRESSES`); the registration excluding every `CAPTCHA_VENDOR_MATCHES` pattern and reaching no vendor address (`patternMatches` reads `*://`); `sidebar-app.js` in every build, at its root, listed nowhere in the manifest, naming no WXT content-script context.

After the review's fixes (the verify of `de81ea1`): the content script 823,625 bytes in production and 826,419 in the e2e variant, the sidebar's bundle 753,104 and 753,206; the budgets unchanged.
