## v1.3 (TailorIT v1.3): submitted answers, known questions and the run-start AI batch (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §16)
What follows holds over every note above and below where they differ. The owner's words of 2026-11-09 ("every job will have the question for job application for sending answer quickly … If user send the application, the answer will be stored on server, and user can see it in the future"; the rest in §16), under "I approved all" and the earlier approval of 2026-11-08 to upload form values to the owner's own TailorIT; the coordinator's decisions 3, 5 and 7, built by builder B on main `bcf3830` as `64f41f4` (branch `v13-answers`), against TailorIT's v1.3 forms contract (API 6, additive: POST /api/autofill/submissions, POST /api/autofill/forms/known, the lookup's `formKnown`; the copy was then pinned to TailorIT v1.4 `d48e133`, and since v1.5 to TailorIT v1.5 `2ad0fbf`: docs/CONTRACT.md "v1.3 and v1.4" and "v1.5"). Decisions taken under "I approved all": docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md §17.

### Capture (`src/content/form-capture.ts`; `runtime.ts`, `orchestrator.ts` `RunEnv.stepLeaving`, `FillRun.canariesNow`)
- The form's questions and the person's answers are read into the document's memory (`createFormCapture`, one per document and application) as each step is left and at every armed Submit: the run's own Next (the machine's press-next and the panel's "Continue to the next page": `RunEnv.stepLeaving`, before the click), the person's own trusted click on the adapter's Next (capture phase, before the page's own handlers) and the step shown at the Submit press (`arm`). A step is named by the adapter's step signature (a form without steps is one step); reading a step again replaces what it held. A form whose steps are separate documents keeps only the last document's (docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md item 320).
- `captureStep` reads a fresh scan as `readAnswer` reads a field: a text, cut to `CAPTURE_TEXT_MAX` (10,000 UTF-16 units, never half a pair; its comment says code points); the chosen options' clean labels (never their places or native values; a remote search's typed choice as one label; a choice answer with a label over 300 characters or more than 50 chosen is dropped, not cut); a box's state; and for an upload only its first file's name (`fileNameOf`: no folder, no control characters, at most 255). A question with nothing in it is kept as a question. Help text and options' native values are never captured.
- Never captured, question or answer: passwords and one-time codes (the scanner makes no field of them, `dom/scan.ts`); never sent, question or answer: a national identity number the person typed (since the jr7 review fixes, A-7: `background/submitted-forms.ts` `NEVER_SENT`, `identity.nationalId`, left out of the body and filed under no category); CAPTCHA (its response field the scanner skips, vendor frames the content script never runs in, and any question that asks one in words: `botCheckOf`); a field `checkHoneypot` refuses (hidden, or named by no visible label), a camouflaged or disabled one; any field whose label, help text or an option holds a tripwire, or whose texts or answer hold a canary (the run's canaries in memory, `FillRun.canariesNow`, empty when the person submits a form no run filled, and those its own texts carry); our own UI.
- At the press the frame sends `submit/form {applicationKey, fields, posting?}` right after `submit/armed` (both at once: the page may leave as it sends). `posting` (top frame only, and only where the page's title gives the company and the job: `postingNamesOf`, Greenhouse's "Job Application for … at …" and Lever's "Company - Title"): those two names and the visible text outside the form root; none otherwise.

### Send (`src/background/submissions.ts`, `submitted-forms.ts`, `server-client.ts` `submissions`)
- `submit/form` is taken like `submit/armed` (rate limit, no private tab, the application must be the frame's, the disclosure acknowledged) and held in the worker's memory only (`forms`, never storage), for the arm of the same frame and document. The frame sends it at the press, not after the record, because the site's confirmation is often a new document (Lever's /thanks, Greenhouse's /confirmation page) whose frame memory no longer holds the form. Nothing is sent to TailorIT before the confirmation. A held form leaves memory when it is sent, when a newer `submit/form` of the same tab and application replaces it, when the tab closes, the token changes or a record is refused for good, or with the worker; `formOf` refuses one older than `SUBMIT_ARM_MS` (10 minutes), and no timer drops it before then. (The handler holds a form even where the arm itself was refused, a person's own Submit with "Record applications I submit myself" off: with no arm nothing is recorded, so it is never sent.)
- Once the site confirmed that Submit (`record()`):
  - a job TailorIT has (the arm names its id): POST /applied first, as before; once it answered, POST /submissions `{jobId, resumeId?, questions, answers?}`, whatever the tab's page state shows by then; its outcome changes nothing of the record. The arm is already dropped then, so a send that fails here is not tried again (docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md item 323);
  - a posting TailorIT has no job of (the arm names only its address): POST /submissions `{url, posting?, questions, answers?}` **in place of** /applied. TailorIT does /applied's work in one transaction and adds the posting (from its ATS, else from the page's posting), so a job not on the lake joins it with its first application. A 400 changed nothing on TailorIT's side, so POST /applied then records it as before (`submit.form-fallback`); any other refusal ends the record; offline or TailorIT's failure keeps the form and the arm for Try again;
  - the auto-apply agent's own tab: once its result recorded the job (`agent.submitted` answered `recorded`), POST /submissions with the arm's job id (the tab's chosen job, which `agent.act` sets from the task), else its address with the page's posting.
- The body (`submissionBodyOf`): each question's field key as `ref`, `normalizeLabel` as `questionKey`, its label on one line, kind, options' clean labels and their hash (none for a list never read), section (`sectionKey`, so a known question names the field's section again), required, step, position, and `category` (`categoryOfSpec`, the resolver's `classifyField`; `formCategoryOf` names EEO `sensitive_eeo`, legal `sensitive_legal`, pay `sensitive_compensation`, consents, attention checks and free text as TailorIT does: its screen for which answers it ever gives another job); `jobId` or `url`, never both and never `employerUrl`; `resumeId` where the fill's upload verified. Answers only while "Keep my submitted answers in TailorIT" is on (off: the `answers` key is left out, and TailorIT keeps the questions only, earlier answers as they were), each with its provenance (the run's row: its provenance where the run wrote it and the person left it so; `person` where they typed or changed it; null with no row). Each question and answer is checked against the contract on its own (one that does not fit is left out); a body the contract refuses is never sent.
- One send per arm: the form is taken out of memory as it is sent (two signs of one Submit can record twice: item 316), and put back only after a transient failure, where a Try again can still send it.

### Settings version 9 (`core/types/settings.ts`, `shared/settings.ts`, `shared/settings-stored.ts`, `background/settings.ts`)
- `modes.keepAnswers: 'on' | 'off'` ("Keep my submitted answers in TailorIT"), On by default; drawn with the modes after "Record applications I submit myself" (`ui/page/modes.tsx` `SHOWN_MODES`), on the options page and in the panel's Settings. `migrateSettingsV8` gives every version 8 value On; versions 1 to 7 go through it (`SettingsV8Schema` reads version 8 to migrate it). The content script never reads it (`ContentModesSchema`): the worker leaves the answers out.

### Known questions and earlier answers (`background/known-forms.ts`; `job-cards.ts`, `run-begin.ts`, `planner.ts`)
- POST /forms/known `{jobId}` (else `{url}`, the canonical address through `tailoritAddressOf`) is read ahead as a page loads when its on-load lookup names a job and says `formKnown: true` (loadCard's relay), and at run/begin once the lookup names the job (unless it says `formKnown: false`; never in a dry run). A read by job id is kept `KNOWN_FORM_TTL_MS` (10 minutes) in memory; reads by address and `fields` reads are not kept (what `fields` brought is merged into the run's memory); all go with the token, and a failed read is not kept.
- Every plan waits for the first read `KNOWN_FORM_WAIT_MS` (3 s) at most. Then the questions the plan leaves open that TailorIT keeps none of for the job are read once each (`fields`: their `questionKey` and `optionSetHash` alone, at most 300 a read and `KNOWN_FIELDS_READS` (8) reads a run; `planner.ts` `readOpen`): a planned key with no write and no provenance, a row not blocked (a tripwire in a label blocks it), not disabled, passing `checkHoneypot`, no lone box (whose label is often an answer itself), no upload, a label that parses, and none whose folded label holds any of the person's profile values of three characters or more (`canaryWords`). The plan is then made again with the answers that brought, so the person's answers to the same question on other jobs fill them too. So nothing the profile fills, and nothing the honeypot check refuses, is named to TailorIT before the Submit (the trap specs' invariant: no profile value in any request but an AI request's page text). `readOpen` has no camouflage or canary screen of its own, as capture has. The answers become the resolver's `SubmittedAnswer` list (`submittedAnswersOf`: a Greenhouse board-API question's key and option set computed as the extension computes them; an upload's answer never one), handed to `planFrame` as `PlanInput.submittedAnswers`.

### The resolver step (`core/resolver/submitted.ts`, `resolve.ts`; `core/guard/provenance.ts`; `core/enums.ts`, `types/fill.ts`, `types/match.ts`, `types/intent.ts`, `types/submitted.ts`)
- New provenance `submitted-before` (`{submittedId, sameJob}`; trusted, as `saved`), reasons `submitted-before` (a note: written, not flagged) and `submitted-option-gone` (flagged, not written), classifier `submitted`.
- Order: the tripwire, attention and bot checks, the rules' hand-overs, a saved answer, then the profile's value (or the policy's: EEO, legal, consent) as before; **only where those leave a gap** (`SUBMITTED_GAPS`: no profile value, an unknown question, free text, an EEO or legal question the profile leaves open, or the decline default the policy chose for want of an answer) does an earlier answer fill it. The profile wins because the person keeps it up to date, while an answer typed into one form is that day's fact; and every earlier answer still beats any AI.
- Which answer: this job's first (same normalized label and option set; else by label, an options answer checked against the options shown now: one no longer offered is `submitted-option-gone` and no AI answers it, except that where the EEO decline default applies it stands and is written; a list not read yet is planned as a choice, so the loop reads it), the newest among equals, a form's same-labelled questions told apart by section and then by place; then another job's, by the same label and option set (a list not read yet by its label, the hash deciding once read), only for the profile and unknown classes, never a question the classify screen withholds, never free text. Never a consent, attention check, bot check or upload. An EEO, legal or sensitive question, or one the classify screen withholds, takes this job's own answer only while the sensitive part is loaded (`sensitiveState === 'loaded'`).
- An earlier answer is never an AI input: answer and draft items skip a field classified `submitted`, and `submitted-option-gone` is no AI gap (`core/plan/answers.ts`, `drafts.ts`).
- The Guard: `checkProvenanceShape` (an id and a flag, never a file or a date), `checkProvenance` (the run holds the answer, for this question's label, whose it says), and the category policy (`submitted-before` allowed for the profile, unknown, free-text, EEO, legal and sensitive classes; another job's only for profile and unknown).

### The run-start AI batch (`planner.ts` `prestart`; `ai-tasks.ts` `RunAi.prefetch`, `signatureOf`, `claimedVerdict`, `capFits`)
- At a run's first plan/request, when TailorIT keeps the job's questions (and the run is no dry run, not profile-only, with AI answers on), every known question but an upload is planned once as a spec of its own (`knownSpecsOf`: key `known|<id>`, never a field's; no value; no cache; no resume; the sensitive part never loaded, the plan made as `unavailable`, so those questions wait for their step), with the frame's posting text and canaries. The answer items that plan asks (those neither the profile nor an earlier answer covers, across every step) go to `RunAi.prefetch` at once, as one answer batch (or as few as the caps allow), on the run's two task places, streamed like any. Drafts are not asked ahead: a long free-text box still drafts at its step (item 319).
- A later plan's answer item with the same signature (normalized question, kind, shape, scope, option set and its options' label hashes, layout, one-round) takes that answer: at once when it is in, else with its batch and stream; a choice's index is the field's own option with the same label hash; a text only where it fits the field's cap. A failed batch's items are asked again as before.
- The same screens as any plan: planFrame's own (sensitive questions, closed contexts, scopes) and `answerBatches`'. The known questions are page text other people's browsers captured (or Greenhouse's public board gave TailorIT): they travel only as their text, quoted as untrusted, through the same item screens.

### Messages, logs, copy, mock
- `submit/form` (content → worker, `DoneAnswerSchema`); `CapturedFieldSchema`, `CapturedAnswerSchema`, `CapturedPostingSchema` (`messaging/schemas.ts`, `// v1.3 B` blocks). `ValueOriginSchema` takes `submitted-before`.
- Log codes (counts only): `content.capture-failed`, `submit.form-held`, `submit.form-refused`, `submit.form-sent`, `submit.form-fallback`, `forms.known`, `forms.known-failed`, `ai.known-batch`.
- Copy (`shared/copy.ts`): `COPY.options.modes.keepAnswers` (its name and help), the two reasons, the provenance "Your earlier application": drafts for the owner.
- The e2e mock answers both routes (docs/CONTRACT.md "The mock in v1.3 and v1.4").

### Tests
`src/core/resolver/submitted.test.ts`, `src/core/guard/provenance.test.ts` (the policy), `src/core/types/settings.test.ts`, `src/background/{submitted-forms,submitted-send,known-forms,ai-known-batch,settings,run-begin,agent-worker}.test.ts`, `src/content/form-capture.browser.test.ts`, the options and panel look tests, and e2e `64-submitted-answers` (fixture `fixtures/synthetic/submit/greenhouse-questions.html`: the first fill asks the AI about the one unknown question; after the person's Submit exactly one /submissions goes, holding no password and no file's bytes, its labels and answers as the form held them; a second fill takes that answer as `submitted-before` with no AI request about it). The shared request invariant (`e2e/helpers/invariants.ts`) sets aside /submissions' `questions`, `answers` and `posting` from the profile-value check alone; the hidden-marker and canary check still reads the whole body; trap specs 20, 21 and 29 and `fixtures/traps` are unchanged (§16, accepted by the coordinator).

## v1.4 (TailorIT v1.4): tailoring, Preview and Edit, and the templates (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §16)
What follows holds over every note above and below where they differ; the inline preview under the résumé line or a review row, and "Open it larger" ("v3 Phases 3b and 4", "v4", "v5 Phase 1") are gone. The owner's words of 2026-11-09 on v1.4 ("Tailoring resume and coverletter", "User can preview and edit built resume and coverletter. If user click preview, the resume will be shown as whole page dialog. If user click edit, will start resume build page with the current content", "User can select resume and coverletter templates on autofill settings"); the coordinator's decisions 16 to 18, built by builder F as `ce7538d` (branch `v14-tailor`, from main `bcf3830`, with the contract pulled from TailorIT's builder D at `b81b53b`), merged with B's `64f41f4` at `b8f75fa`. No extension setting was added: B's settings version 9 stands. Everything new is marked `v1.4 F` in the code.

### Tailoring on the job card (`background/tailor.ts`, `ui/panel/tailor.tsx`)
- **The controls.** Under the résumé line ("Résumé: <label> · Preview · Edit · Change"; Preview only for a PDF, Edit for any attached resume, neither under "Apply without resume"), "Tailor Resume For This Job"; where the form asks for a cover letter, a line "Cover letter: <yours | written by TailorIT | written for this job | none yet | checking…> · Preview · Edit" (Preview and Edit only when there is a letter) with "Generate Cover Letter" under it. Each is a mint pill with a sparkle and a "?" (our own words for what Jobright's custom-resume and cover-letter buttons do), and each line shows until a run lists that file's own row, as the résumé line always has. Both wait while one tailoring runs.
- **The trusted, seen click.** Each control takes a click only once it has been shown `CONTROL_SEEN_MS` (`useShownFor`, the main button's rule); on the page, the sidebar's gate holds it too (`content/ui/sidebar.ts` `HELD_BUTTONS` gains `[data-tailor-action]`: trusted only, the button itself seen as drawn, its time started again when its meaning changes).
- **`panel/tailor {tabId, start?}`.** Without `start`: the tab's tailoring for the application its card frame shows now, from the worker's memory (nothing asked). With `start` (`'resume' | 'coverLetter'`), refused in this order: `TAILOR_LIMIT` (4 a minute per tab); not connected; AI-written answers (`llm.drafts`) off (`ai-off`); no card frame (`unavailable`); the same application already tailoring (its view is the answer); a page that cannot be read (`unavailable`); a posting that forbids AI under profile-only, an "I did not use AI" statement unless `llm.whenForbidden` is `use-ai`, or a run of the tab that went profile-only (`ai-off`, as Write it now's `cover-letters.ts` refuses); no job and no names (`no-job`). One at a time per tab.
- **The request** (POST /api/autofill/tailor, scope `ai:run`; `tailorRequestOf`): with the lookup's job id (worker memory only) `{jobId, resumeId?, coverLetter, force?}`; without one, the page's visible posting text, its company and title as the page's title names them (`postingNamesOf`: Greenhouse's and Lever's shapes; with any of the three empty nothing is sent, `no-job`) and its canaries (at least 2 characters, cut to 200, at most 50, only when any remain), each text cut at a word to 20,000, 200 and 200 characters. The posting text is read from the form frame (`job/postingText`) for the policy and attestation checks even with a job id, but sent only without one. `resumeId`: `jobs.tabJob().resume` (the person's Change, else TailorIT's suggestion; this application's own tailored resume stands for the one it was tailored from, so tailoring again never tailors a tailored resume), left out when there is none or it is no `[a-z0-9-]{1,64}` id (TailorIT then takes the profile's chosen or default resume); "Apply without resume" does not clear it. `coverLetter` true for "Generate Cover Letter" alone: every tailoring writes a resume. Never sent: the templates (absent means the profile's `documents`, per the contract, as the person chose them in Options, Settings or the profile editor), `postingUrl` or any address.
- **The poll.** GET /api/autofill/tailor/:id every `AUTOFILL_TAILOR_POLL_MS` (3 s; at once when `deduped`), a 429's hint waited out (at least 3 s), a failure on the way (time-out, no answer, 5xx, a bad answer) polled again, given up after `TAILOR_CAP_MS` (10 minutes of waits between polls, carried over a forced re-post; the requests' own time is not counted; `unavailable`). Each change of TailorIT's step or line tells every panel `panel/changed {what: 'tailor'}` (`PANEL_CHANGES` gains `tailor`); each panel reads its own tab's with `panel/tailor`. The panel has no timer.
- **Generate Cover Letter, deduped onto a resume-only tailoring.** A succeeded task with no `coverLetterId` for a letter asked for is posted once more with `force: true` (the contract's dedupe key names the job, the source resume and the templates, not the letter flag). It is not counted against `TAILOR_LIMIT`, but it is a second task against TailorIT's ten an hour.
- **Done.** The view says the tailored resume's title, the ATS score (and the one before) and whether a letter came. The worker makes the tailored resume the tab's choice, as Change would (`job-cards.ts` `setResume`: kept per application in `chrome.storage.session` `jobchoice:<tab>`, ids only; "Apply without resume" let go), while the tab still shows the same application: the card is read again (the panel reloads it when it sees running then done), the fill and a run going on attach it, and the letter row's key moves (its resume changed), so it is asked again. TailorIT linked both documents to the tracker entry (made Saved when there was none), so its letter route answers the tailored letter first for the next fill. The letter's id stays in the worker for its Preview and Edit (`previewLetterOf` prefers the tailored letter of the application the tab shows); the resume's Preview and Edit read the tab's choice (`attachedResumeOf`), and the kept source id only points a second tailoring back at its source.
- **Refusals.** 409 `needs_editable_copy` → `needs-copy` ("Make An Editable Copy In TailorIT", a plain link to TailorIT's `/documents`, opened by the page, not the worker); 429 → `rate-limited`; 503 `ai_unavailable` → `ai-unavailable`; 409 `ai_busy` → `busy`; 403 → `not-allowed`; 401 → `not-connected`; a task that failed → `failed` (or its code's reason). Each a short line under the control.
- **Memory only**, per tab (`held`): the task, its view, the source resume id and the result's ids; gone with the tab, the token, and a start for another application. A worker that stops loses its poll (TailorIT goes on and links what it made to the tracker entry, which the next lookup reads; the panel then shows the line idle again).

### Preview and Edit (`ui/preview/PreviewPage.tsx`, `entrypoints/preview/`, `panel/openDocument`)
- **Preview** (the owner: "the resume will be shown as whole page dialog"): every Preview (the résumé line, the letter line, the review's résumé and cover-letter rows) is `panel/openDocument {tabId, kind, how: 'preview'}`; the worker opens `preview.html?tabId=N&kind=resume|coverLetter` in a tab of its own (`tabs.create`, no popup window): an extension page ("TailorIT Preview"), full window, never inside the job page, no web-accessible resource (wxt declares none, and check-build refuses the key). The worker checks nothing more for Preview and answers `{opened: true}`. The page has two tabs, Résumé and Cover Letter, `kind` choosing the first, an "Edit In TailorIT" button (Edit for the shown kind) and Close (`window.close()`); without a tab id it says so ("This preview names no tab. Open it again from the panel."). Each document is fetched when its tab is shown, over the existing `preview` port, which the worker accepts from extension pages alone (`receivePreview`: the worker's checked PDF from `GET /resumes/:id/file` or the letter's `/file`; the page checks the type, the size, the chunks' count, length and order, and the SHA-256 again) and drawn from the page's own `blob:` address in a frame filling the window. The address is revoked and a transfer still on its way ended when the other tab is shown (going back fetches it again); when the page closes Chrome releases its `blob:` addresses with the document. `ResumePreview.tsx` is removed, with the "Open It Larger" and "Close the preview" words and the sidebar's "opens in Chrome's side panel" link: the sidebar asks the worker to open the same page, so no file's bytes reach the job page's process (the S6/F10 rule holds; `SIDEBAR_PORTS` stays `['panel']`, never `preview`). `Report.tsx` keeps a dormant inline path (`renderPreview`) that nothing passes now.
- **Edit**: `panel/openDocument {how: 'edit'}` (refused `not_connected` while not connected); the worker reads the id itself and opens TailorIT's editor in a new tab (`editorUrlOf`): a built resume's `<origin>/resumes/<id>`, an upload's `<origin>/documents` (where its editable copy is made), a letter's `<origin>/cover-letter/<id>`. The resume is the one the tab attaches (the tailored one after tailoring); the letter the tailored one, else the run's ready letter, else the person's own (a check, `generate` false). None: `{opened: false}`, "There is nothing to open yet." An id is checked before it names a path. Preview and Edit share one limit, `OPEN_DOCUMENT_LIMIT` (10 a minute per tab; past it `rate_limited`).

### The templates (`ui/page/documents.tsx`, `profile-editor.ts` `documents`)
- `panel/documents` (scope `profile:read`): GET /profile and GET /api/autofill/templates: the profile's `documents.resumeTemplateId` and `documents.coverLetterTemplateId`, TailorIT's lists (at most 100 resume and 200 letter templates, names cut to 120 characters) and `canWrite` (`profile:write`). Reads and saves count against `PROFILE_EDIT_LIMIT` (20 a minute, its own key); a failed read is logged, a good one not.
- `panel/documents {save: {resumeTemplateId, coverLetterTemplateId}}` (`profile:write`): the worker reads the profile and the lists as TailorIT holds them now and PUTs the profile with its revision, those two alone changed: every other field as read, the email that stands for the account's address saved as '' again (as the editor saves it), the sensitive part left out (TailorIT keeps it). A template TailorIT does not list is refused before the PUT (`invalid`; the worker checks both ids, so a stale choice left selected refuses a save of the other too); a stale revision is `conflict`. The worker's profile cache is dropped after a save, as the editor's save drops it.
- Drawn in three places: Options' "Documents" card (after the modes, with its "?"), the side panel's Settings view (a "Documents" section with its own Save: the profile's, not this browser's settings), and the profile editor's "Documents" section after Preferences (bound to the editor's draft, saved with the profile through `panel/saveProfile`, where TailorIT checks the ids; read-only inside the editor's disabled fieldset without `profile:write`). Choices: "Resume's own template" (null) then TailorIT's resume templates; "No template" (null) then the letter templates, TailorIT's own marked "(TailorIT)". A stored template TailorIT no longer lists stays a choice ("A template TailorIT no longer has"), so nothing changes unasked. Not connected: Options and Settings ask nothing; the editor's section asks, and the worker refuses it locally. No `profile:write`: read-only.
- The pulled contract carries the two fields (`.default(null)`), so every save of the profile (the editor's included) keeps them; TailorIT also keeps the stored ones when a save leaves them out.

### The sidebar's channel, messages and logs
- `SIDEBAR_PAGE_MESSAGES` gains `panel/tailor`, `panel/openDocument` and `panel/documents`, and nothing else; each from the tab's top frame alone, while the panel shows on the page (the existing rules); the first two name their own tab, and `panel/documents` names none (`sidebarMayAsk` lets a message without one through). None of the three raises the extension's autonomy or carries the sensitive part. The finished view the panels receive carries the tailored resume's title ("<Company> - <Job title>", at most 200 characters), the ATS scores and TailorIT's line, as the job card already carries the resume's title: in the sidebar these are in the page's process, in its closed shadow root; never a document's body or bytes.

| Message | Answer | Note |
|---|---|---|
| `panel/tailor {tabId, start?: 'resume'\|'coverLetter'}` | `TailorView`: idle; running {kind, step, message}; done {kind, title, letter, score, scoreBefore}; failed {kind, reason} | v1.4 F; sidebar too |
| `panel/openDocument {tabId, kind, how: 'preview'\|'edit'}` | `{opened}`, or a refusal (`rate_limited`; Edit `not_connected`) | v1.4 F; sidebar too |
| `panel/documents {save?}` | `{outcome: 'ready', resumeTemplateId, coverLetterTemplateId, resumeTemplates, coverLetterTemplates, canWrite, saved?}` or `{outcome: refusal}` | v1.4 F; sidebar too |
| `panel/changed {what: 'tailor'}` (panel port, worker → page) | | v1.4 F |

- **Log codes** (value-free): `tailor.started` (job, deduped, letter: flags), `tailor.refused` (reason: a number), `tailor.step` (a number), `tailor.done` (letter: a flag; the score, -1 without one), `tailor.failed` (reason: a number), `tailor.opened` (preview, letter: flags), `profile.documents` (status; saved on a save).
- **Copy** (`COPY.tailor`, `COPY.previewPage`, `COPY.documents`): drafts for the owner.
- **The mock:** docs/CONTRACT.md "The mock in v1.3 and v1.4". The golden profile fixture (`fixtures/profiles/alex-rivera.profile.json`) gains the two null template fields, as the contract's parse now gives them.

### Disclosure version 12 (`shared/stored.ts` `DISCLOSURE_VERSION`, `COPY.options.connection.disclosure`)
docs/PRIVACY.md holds it word for word (`src/shared/copy.test.ts`). New: the auto-apply agent's submitted job, as any submitted application, sends its questions and the person's answers; a fourth paragraph, after the one on what this browser keeps: once a site confirms an application the person submitted, its questions go to their TailorIT, and their answers unless "Keep my submitted answers in TailorIT" is off; the questions kept for everyone on that TailorIT and never shown on Jobs, the answers for the person alone, in the Tracker and filling the same questions later where the profile does not, before any AI; the job's known questions and earlier answers asked for as a page loads and at a fill, the open questions named by their wording and a fingerprint of their options alone; a job TailorIT lacks added for everyone, on some sites with the posting's visible text and title; never a password, a human check, a hidden field or a file's content (a file's name alone); self-identification, legal and pay answers sent to TailorIT and never to the AI; none of it kept by this browser. In the AI paragraph: a job's known open questions asked of the AI together as a fill starts, and tailoring (a resume, and with Generate Cover Letter a letter, from the resume it would attach and the posting, on the chosen templates, kept by TailorIT and linked to the job, attached from then on), only when the person asks and while AI-written answers are on. In the second paragraph: the templates among what its panel shows and changes, the previews in a full window of the extension's own, never in the job page, Edit in TailorIT, and the files held while previewed. A connected browser shows it again with I understand (`auth.ts` `disclosed` reads the stored version against `DISCLOSURE_VERSION`), and until then what it governs stays off as before (no submitted application is recorded, so no form is sent either).

Disclosure 12 adds about 2.4 KB of words (2,384 bytes) to every bundle that holds `COPY`; not measured in a build here (no e2e or build was run for the docs).
- **Tests (v1.4 F):** `src/background/tailor.test.ts`, `src/ui/panel/tailor.browser.test.tsx`, `src/ui/preview/preview.browser.test.tsx`, `src/ui/options/options.browser.test.tsx` (the Documents card), `src/ui/panel/job-card.browser.test.tsx`, `src/content/sidebar.browser.test.ts` and `src/ui/sidebar/sidebar.browser.test.tsx` (the new messages and the held buttons), `test/mock-server.test.ts` (the tailor routes and their scenarios); e2e `64-tailor` (Tailor Resume For This Job on a posting TailorIT has: the request exactly `{jobId, resumeId, coverLetter: false}`, the tailored resume attached, the needs-copy refusal, Preview from the sidebar opening the extension's page and no frame in the job page), and `36-panel-3b` and `41-cover-letter` changed (the preview page's address, a `blob:` frame, the SHA-256 of the golden file; switching to the letter's tab removes the résumé's frame). Builder F's `npm run verify` on `b8f75fa` passed (typecheck, lint, test, test:dom, the Edge build, check-build of the three builds, and Playwright: 203 passed); docs/SPEC.md, the trap specs 20, 21 and 29 and `fixtures/traps` unchanged.

## v1.5 (TailorIT v1.5): the preview over the page, Edit, the folders and the draft answers (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §17)
What follows holds over every note above and below where they differ. It supersedes "v1.4"'s Preview for the résumé line, the letter line and the review's rows: the full-window page stays, as the fallback and as the dialog's Open In A New Tab. The owner's words of 2026-11-09 on v1.5 ("if user click `preview` on resume bullet, resume should be shown as dialog of current active page", "`show` right of preview should be `edit`", Prebuild's "job application draft", "list of resume will be shown as folder directory style"; all of them in §17), under "I approved all"; the coordinator's decisions 2, 3, 4 (the drafts' use) and 6 (the extension's folders), built by builder X as `9c4d110` and `14afa2c` (branch `v15-ui`, from main `13abe2b`), against TailorIT's `v1.5-contract` `32194ad` (P's drafts and W's `madeBy`/`createdAt`, API 6, additive). The copy is now pinned to TailorIT `v1.5` `2ad0fbf`, whose contract bytes equal `32194ad`'s (docs/CONTRACT.md "v1.5"). Everything new is marked `v1.5 X` in the code. Decisions taken under "I approved all": docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md §18.

### The preview over the page (PLAN decision 2; `content/ui/document-dialog.ts`, `background/document-dialog.ts`)
- **What the person sees** (the owner's screenshot of Jobright's "Your Autofill information", read for its look alone, in TailorIT's teal): Preview on the résumé line, the letter line or a review row shows a dialog over the tab's own page. A dimmed backdrop; a white window with 20 px corners, centred, at most 1,056 px wide (`DIALOG_MAX_WIDTH_PX`) and 85 % of the window high; "Your Resume" and the attached resume's file name (or "Your Cover Letter", with no file name); a close ×; a grey strip with the Résumé / Cover Letter tabs (only when the panel previews both) and "Open In A New Tab"; then the document. Escape closes it; Tab and Shift+Tab cycle its controls and the document's frame; the focus starts on the × and goes back where it was when it closes (the dialog's own restoring). One at a time per document (a new one replaces the one open); none in a hidden tab; it goes with the content script (teardown) and with the page (`pagehide`).
- **How:** `panel/openDocument {how: 'preview'}` gains `kinds`, the documents the panel previews for the tab (`Panel.tsx` `previewKinds`: the résumé when the attached resume is a PDF and "Apply without resume" is not chosen; the letter when a review row, the letter line's state or a finished tailoring has one; `kind` always among them). The worker (`index.ts` `openDocument`) mints a preview grant (`createDocumentDialogs`: 128 random bits as hex, in its memory alone, `PREVIEW_GRANT_TTL_MS` 30 minutes, for the documents it names, replaced at each Preview of the tab, gone with the tab) and sends `preview/show {tabId, kind, kinds, grant, names, page}` to the tab's top frame alone (`frameId: 0`), answered `{shown}` within `DIALOG_ASK_MS` (3 s). `names.resume` is the attached resume's `fileName` from GET /resumes (cut to 255; none when empty); `page` is `runtime.getURL('/preview.html')`, the preview page's dynamic address of this browser session. No frame of ours there (a browser page, the Web Store, a page our script does not reach), a refusal (a hidden tab, a modal the document refuses, a stopped script, a frame that is not the top one) or no answer in time: the grant is dropped and the full-window page opens at the extension's fixed address (`runtime.getURL('/')` + `preview.html?tabId=N&kind=K`), as v1.4, answering `{opened: true}`. Shown: `{opened: true, onPage: true}`. The same path serves the sidebar on the page and Chrome's side panel: the worker asks the tab's top frame either way.
- **The dialog** is a native modal `<dialog>` (`showModal`: the top layer, the page behind it inert) in a closed shadow root of its own (`shadow.ts` `createUiHost`: registered as our UI, events kept from the page, put back once if the page removes it, its style put back if the page restyles it), as the applied window is. Its body is an `<iframe>` (`referrerpolicy="no-referrer"`) of `<page>?tabId=N&kind=K&grant=G`; another tab replaces the frame, and the document and its PDF go with the old one. Every control (×, the tabs, Open In A New Tab; the confirm's box, Cancel and Continue) takes trusted clicks only, each once that control was seen without a break for `CONTROL_SEEN_MS` (600 ms, `visible.ts`), the sidebar's rule; a restyled host starts every control's time again; a refused click does nothing (a box keeps its state; `content.ui-click-refused` gains the flag `document`). Escape is the dialog's own trusted `cancel`; a page's untrusted one does nothing.
- **The embedded preview page** (`PreviewPage.tsx` `EmbeddedPreview`, which `entrypoints/preview/main.tsx` chooses when `window.top !== window`): the one document its address names, filling the frame, with no tabs and no buttons (a page that frames it has nothing to press), asked for over the preview port with the grant (`preview/request` gains `grant`, `DOCUMENT_GRANT`: 32 lower-case hex digits); without a well-formed grant or a tab id it asks nothing and says so ("This preview names no tab. Open it again from the panel.").
- **The worker's side:** an extension page inside a subframe of a tab (`frameId` not 0) is a new channel kind, `framed` (`senders.ts` `channelOf`; one in a tab's top frame is still a `page`). It sends no message at all (`router.ts`: `bad_sender`, `router.bad-sender`) and opens no `panel` port; its `preview` port is served (`resume-transfer.ts` `acceptPreview`'s new `admits`) only for its own tab, with a live preview grant of that tab naming the document asked (`document-dialog.ts` `admits`; else `resume-unavailable`, and the logs `document.refused {preview, granted}` and `resume.preview {sent: false, refused: true}`). A page that learns the dynamic address in a session and frames the page itself gets nothing. The full-window page (a `page`) is served as in v1.4, without a grant.
- **Open In A New Tab:** `preview/openTab {kind}` (content → worker, the tab's top frame alone, while a live preview grant of that tab names the document, under `OPEN_DOCUMENT_LIMIT`; else `bad_sender` and `document.refused {tab}`): the full-window page on that document, then the dialog closes.
- **Strict CSP:** e2e `65-document-dialog` serves the form with `Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'self'; frame-src 'none'`: the dialog shows, the extension's frame loads (a page's policy does not govern the extension's own pages), its scripts and styles load from the extension's own origin (its sheet applies), the PDF draws with the golden SHA-256, and no violation of ours is reported (the page's own inline style and script are refused by its own policy, as before). Builder X measured first, in a bare experiment on the e2e Chromium (156.0.8078.4): `runtime.getURL` gives the dynamic address in the worker and in the content script; the frame commits at the extension's fixed origin (the worker's `sender.url`); its sub-resources and its `blob:` PDF frame load; a web page's fetch or frame of the fixed address is refused ("blocked by Chromium"); and no extension address shows in the page's Resource Timing.

### Edit (PLAN decision 3; `Report.tsx`, `Panel.tsx`, `Modals.tsx`, `index.ts` `openDocument`, `tailor.ts` `editorUrlOf`)
- The review's résumé row shows **Edit** in Show's place (`ReportActions.openEdit`, `data-action="edit-file"`, "Edit "<label>" in TailorIT") while a resume is attached and the application is not submitted (as the row's Change); every other row, and the résumé row then, keeps Show. The job card's résumé line already had Edit (v1.4); both go through `panel/openDocument {how: 'edit'}`.
- A built resume opens `<origin>/resumes/<id>` at once; a letter `<origin>/cover-letter/<id>`, as v1.4. An uploaded one opens `<origin>/resumes/<id>/edit` (TailorIT's route, from builder W, which makes or reuses the editable copy with progress and then opens the editor), after the confirm unless the person chose "Don't show this again": the worker mints an edit grant (in its memory alone, `EDIT_GRANT_TTL_MS` 10 minutes, used once, naming the resume id, replaced at each ask, gone with the tab) and sends `edit/confirm {grant}` to the tab's top frame; shown, it answers the panel `{opened: false, confirm: 'page'}` and the panel does nothing more. The confirm (the preview's look, `role="alertdialog"`, the focus on Cancel): "Edit An Uploaded Resume", "Editing an uploaded resume needs an editable copy. TailorIT's AI reads your file to make it, which can take a little while. Continue?", "Don't show this again", Cancel and Continue. Continue sends `edit/confirmed {grant, skip}` (the top frame alone); the worker takes the grant (once; a stale, used or other tab's grant is `expired` and `document.refused {edit}`), checks the connection (`not_connected`), keeps `skip`, and opens the route (`document.edit {confirmed, page, skip}`). The id never reaches the page.
- Where the page does not show it (Chrome's side panel on a page with no frame of ours, a hidden tab, no answer in 3 s): `{opened: false, confirm: 'panel'}`, and the panel shows the same words in its own modal (`PanelModal` kind `edit-upload`, its Continue held until seen `CONTROL_SEEN_MS`), whose Continue sends `panel/openDocument {how: 'edit', confirmed: true, skipConfirm}`; the worker keeps `skipConfirm` and opens the route at once (`document.edit {confirmed: true}`). `confirmed` and `skipConfirm` are refused on a Preview (the schema's refinement).
- **Settings version 10:** `ui.skipUploadEditConfirm` (false by default: the confirm shows). `migrateSettingsV9` gives every version 9 value the default; versions 1 to 8 go through it (`SettingsV9Schema` reads version 9 to migrate it). Options' Documents card shows it the other way round, "Ask before editing an uploaded resume" (`data-setting="ui.askBeforeUploadEdit"`, with its "?"), which turns the confirm back on; the panel's Settings does not show it. Read and saved by the worker alone (`settings.get`, `settings.save` on the settings as stored now); content scripts never receive it.
- `editorUrlOf(origin, 'resume', id, false)` is now `/resumes/<id>/edit` (it was `/documents`); `Make An Editable Copy In TailorIT`, the tailoring's `needs-copy` link, still opens `/documents`.

### The folders (PLAN decision 6, the extension's part; `ui/panel/resume-folders.ts`, `JobCard.tsx` `Folders`)
- Change's list shows three folding groups: Uploaded Resumes (`madeBy: 'upload'`), Edited Resumes (`editor` and `copy`), and Tailored Resumes (`tailor`), the last under one heading per local day of `createdAt` ("Nov 9, 2026"), newest first by day and within a day, an undated group ("No date") last. TailorIT before v1.5 names neither: an uploaded resume is Uploaded, a built one Edited. Each heading shows its count (its label "Tailored Resumes, 3 resumes"). The folder holding the attached resume opens (none attached: the first that holds any); an empty one shows its 0 and cannot open. The extension has no Total / By date switch: Tailored is always by day.
- `panel/resumes` items gain `madeBy` and `createdAt` (`job-cards.ts` `madeOf`: each only when it reads; `createdAt` an ISO 8601 instant of at most 40 characters that parses).

### Draft answers (PLAN decision 4, their use; `background/submitted-forms.ts` `draftAnswersOf`, `known-forms.ts`; `core/resolver/submitted.ts` `findDraftAnswer`, `resolve.ts`)
- POST /forms/known's `drafts` (P's contract: an array apart from `answers`, each `match: 'draft'`, `questionId`, `status` `ready | not_ready`, `answer` (null when not ready, never a file), `provenance` `server-ai | mcp`, `preparedAt`): `draftAnswersOf` turns each **ready** one for one of the job's own questions into this job's answer marked `draft` (`SubmittedAnswer.draft`, `sameJob: true`, its `preparedAt` as its time); a `not_ready` one, a file's, a kind the extension does not fill, and one naming no question it returned are left out (the fill's own AI path answers that question). A Greenhouse board-API question's key and option set are computed from its label and options, as for earlier answers. They join the run's known answers (`known-forms.ts`, log `forms.known {questions, answers, drafts, notReady}`), so every plan reads them: the run-start AI batch's and the auto-apply agent's included.
- **The order** (`resolveField`, only where a saved answer, the profile and the policy leave a gap, `SUBMITTED_GAPS`, as v1.3's earlier answers): this job's own earlier answer; then the draft; then another job's earlier answer; then the run-start AI batch and the AI. New provenance `prebuilt` (`{draftId}`, not trusted), the note `prebuilt-answer` (written, not flagged), classifier `prebuilt` (so no AI classifies, answers or drafts it: `core/plan/answers.ts`, `drafts.ts`), confidence `CLASSIFY_PREBUILT` 0.9, rule `prebuilt.draft`.
- **Never** a sensitive, EEO, legal, consent, attention-check or bot-check question, one the classify screen withholds, a profile category the AI may not answer (`ANSWERABLE_PROFILE_CATEGORIES`), or a file (`DRAFT_CLASSES` profile, unknown and free text; the shared finder asked with `sensitiveReady: false`: two layers); never in place of the EEO decline default. This job's own earlier answer whose option is gone stays flagged (`submitted-option-gone`; no draft in its place). A draft whose option the field no longer offers is left out, unflagged: the gap goes on to another job's earlier answer, then the AI (the contract's comment says to flag it; docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md item 343). A list not read yet is matched by its label, and its options checked once read. A lone box takes a draft's tick only on an affirmative statement, as any AI answer.
- **The Guard:** `checkProvenanceShape` (a `draftId`; never a file or a date), `checkProvenance` (a draft the run holds, for this field's label; an earlier answer's provenance never names a draft), the category policy (`prebuilt` for the profile, free-text and unknown classes alone, the profile's only in the categories an AI answer may fill).
- /submissions names a filled draft answer `prebuilt` (P's contract has it; a contract without it would take `llm-answer`).

| Message | Answer | Note |
|---|---|---|
| `panel/openDocument {tabId, kind, how, kinds?, confirmed?, skipConfirm?}` | `{opened, onPage?, confirm?: 'page'\|'panel'}`, or a refusal (`rate_limited`; Edit `not_connected`) | v1.5 X; sidebar too (no new sidebar message) |
| `preview/show {tabId, kind, kinds, grant, names, page}` (worker → top frame) | `{shown}` | v1.5 X; the worker's script alone (`fromWorker`) |
| `edit/confirm {grant}` (worker → top frame) | `{shown}` | v1.5 X; the worker's script alone |
| `preview/openTab {kind}` (content → worker) | `{}`, or `bad_sender`, `rate_limited` | v1.5 X; frame 0, a live preview grant of the tab |
| `edit/confirmed {grant, skip}` (content → worker) | `{}`, or `bad_sender`, `expired`, `not_connected` | v1.5 X; frame 0, the grant once |
| preview port `preview/request {tabId, kind?, grant?}` | the transfer | a framed page needs a live grant of its own tab |

- `SIDEBAR_PAGE_MESSAGES` and `SIDEBAR_PORTS` (`panel`) are unchanged. A sidebar's `panel/openDocument {confirmed: true}` skips the confirm: a page process taken over by an exploit could send it, but the confirm guards the person's time and TailorIT's AI use, no data (docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md item 344).
- **Log codes** (flags only): `content.document-dialog {preview, shown}`, `document.refused {preview, granted} | {tab} | {edit}`, `document.edit {confirmed, page?, skip?}`; `tailor.opened` gains `page` and `fromPage`; `forms.known` gains `drafts` and `notReady`; `resume.preview` gains `refused`; `content.ui-click-refused` gains `document`.
- **Copy:** `PAGE_COPY.documentDialog` (the titles, tabs, frames' titles, "Open In A New Tab", "Close", the confirm's title, text, box, Continue and Cancel), `COPY.report.edit` and `editLabel`, `COPY.job.picker.folders`, `folderLabel` and `undated`, `COPY.documents.askBeforeUploadEdit` and its help, the reason `prebuilt-answer` ("Filled with the answer TailorIT prepared for this job ahead of time."), the provenance `prebuilt` ("Prepared by TailorIT"): drafts for the owner.
- **The mock:** docs/CONTRACT.md "The mock in v1.5".

### check-build and the manifest
- `wxt.config.ts`: `web_accessible_resources: [{resources: ['preview.html'], matches: ['https://*/*', 'http://*/*'], use_dynamic_url: true}]` (`hosts.ts` `WEB_ACCESSIBLE_PAGE` and `ALL_SITES_HOST_PERMISSIONS`). The entry is built in `wxt.config.ts` because `WEB_ACCESSIBLE_PAGE` must stay a bare string in `hosts.ts`, which the page-world bridge imports: an object there cost the bridge 40 bytes and broke its budget.
- `scripts/check-build.mjs` `checkWebAccessible`: exactly that one entry (keys `resources`, `matches` and `use_dynamic_url` alone: `extension_ids` would let other extensions in; the matches as a set), the reason written beside the rule; `preview.html` must be in the build. `web_accessible_resources` leaves the list of keys that must be absent. `test/check-build.test.ts`: a second entry, a second resource, the fixed address (no `use_dynamic_url`), every scheme, other extensions and none each fail; the matches' order does not matter.

### Disclosure version 13 (`shared/stored.ts` `DISCLOSURE_VERSION`, `COPY.options.connection.disclosure`)
docs/PRIVACY.md holds it word for word (`src/shared/copy.test.ts`). In the second paragraph, in place of version 12's "preview the resume and the cover letter it attaches in a full window of the extension's own, never in the job page": the previews over the page the person is on, inside a frame of the extension's own that the page's scripts cannot read, from Chrome's side panel too, in a full window on Open In A New Tab or where the page cannot show it; Edit of an uploaded resume opening an editable copy TailorIT's AI makes from the file (or one made before), asked first unless the person chose Don't show this again; the extension's one page that web pages could load, at an address that changes each browser session, which shows none of the person's files to any other page; and Chrome's side panel keeping the panel from the site, though a preview and that question still open over the page. In the third paragraph: the answers TailorIT prepared for the job ahead of time with Prebuild (by its AI or the person's own through TailorIT), fetched with the job's questions, filling a question where the profile and the person's earlier answer to that job do not, before other jobs' answers and any AI, never a self-identification, legal, pay, consent or attention-check question or a file. In the AI paragraph: the run-start batch asks what the profile, earlier answers and prepared answers leave open. A connected browser shows it again with I understand, and until then what it governs stays off as before.
