## v1.5 part 2 (TailorIT v1.5): sequential auto-apply, the panel's tabs and settings version 11 (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §18)
What follows holds over every note above and below where they differ. The owner's words of 2026-11-10 ("Sequential Auto-apply feature: open new tab with job link on auto-apply queue one by one … new tab will be open in `duration` time after previous tab is open. and accelation is also applied. e.g. 8s, 8.5s, 9s, 9.5s", "UI/UX: there will be tab bar at bottom of topbar … there is 2 tabs(Autofill, Queue) … on settings, radio buttons should be located as horizontal(not vertical) … settings order: …"; all of them in §18), under "I approved all"; the coordinator's decisions 7 to 10, built by builder QX as `78b0772` (branch `v15-queue`, from main `b9d0b9c`), against TailorIT v1.5 Q2's Queue routes (`eafe54f`, pulled as `3549c6d`; API 6, additive: no new scope, nobody connects again); decision 11 is disclosure version 14 (below). The copy is now pinned to TailorIT `v1.5-int2` `7d8af20`, whose contract bytes equal `eafe54f`'s (docs/CONTRACT.md "v1.5 part 2"). Everything new is marked `v1.5 QX` in the code. Decisions taken under "I approved all": docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md §19. No permission was added, and the manifest is unchanged.

### The runner (PLAN decisions 7 and 8; `background/queue-runner.ts` `createQueueRunner`, `queue-source.ts`)
- **A state machine in the worker,** one operation at a time (a keyed queue), its run in `storage.session` `queue:run` (gone at a browser restart, with the tab ids it names; read through `RunSchema`, a strict schema): the phase (running, paused, finished, stopped and why), the tabs opened (the schedule's k is one less), the next tab's time or the ms kept, why the timer waits (`cap`, `offline`, `server`), whether Start turned Run auto-apply off, and each job (at most `QUEUE_RUN_MAX`, 3,000): its queue id, job id, title and company as TailorIT named them, its application key once opened (a hash, never an address), the resume TailorIT names, its status and reason, its tab, when it opened, was first heard from and its fill started, and a result not yet told. Written only when something moved (a signature of the phases and the jobs), so a run of thousands is not rewritten at each tick.
- **Start** (`queue/start`): a run already running or paused answers its status; while not connected, without `agent:run` or before the disclosure as it reads now is acknowledged it starts nothing and answers the status with its `blocked` (`not-connected`, `no-scope`, `disclosure`); it reads every page of TailorIT's queued rows (`GET /api/autofill/agent/queue?state=queued&limit=100&cursor=`, until 3,000; a failed read is `unavailable`); turns Run auto-apply off if it is on (`agent.set(false)`: its running job left for the person); keeps an earlier run's jobs whose tabs are still open and not done (they hold their places in the cap) or whose result waits to be told; and the first tab opens at once (`nextAt` now, then a tick).
- **The schedule:** after the k-th opening (k from 0) the wait is `durationSec + k × accelerationSec` (`core/types/settings.ts` `queueWaitMs(queue, opened)`, k = opened − 1), read from the settings as they are then. While `maxOpenTabs` queued tabs are open and not done (opening, opened, filling, ready), the timer is held with the ms it had left (`hold: 'cap'`, also when the tab just opened reached the cap), and goes on from them when one is done or closed. Pause keeps the ms left; Resume goes on from them; Stop opens nothing more and leaves the open tabs to the person (their results are still told). Offline (`navigator.onLine` false): nothing opens (`hold: 'offline'`). TailorIT not answering an opening: `hold: 'server'`, tried again in `QUEUE_RETRY_MS` (30 s), the schedule unchanged.
- **Opening** (`openNext`): `POST /api/autofill/agent/:id/opened` first (TailorIT takes the row for this browser's tab run, so the background agent never takes it). `item` null: a row TailorIT finished at the claim, shown with its state and reason, nothing told. A refusal of `NOT_QUEUED` (404, 409, 400, 403, a path refused: the agent, another browser, an end, removed): skipped, never opened, nothing told. Any other failure: `retry`. The task's `url` is read the agent's way (`agentAddressOf`): none is failed `unsupported_site` (told), never opened. Then a background tab (`QueueTabs.open`: `tabs.create({url: 'about:blank', active: false})`, in the last focused window), the job's status `opening` saved, then the canonical address (`tabs.update`); the e2e build rests `AGENT_E2E_SETTLE_MS` (750 ms) between, as the agent's window does. Chrome opening no tab: that job skipped and told `needs-you` `cancelled`, and the run stops (`no-tab`). The tab gone before its address opened: skipped, told `cancelled`.
- **Filling** (`hello`, `wall`, from `index.ts`'s `frame/hello` and `frame/wall` for a tab the queue owns): a frame that acts for the tab (its top frame, or the frame that claims its application: `claimFrameOf`) at the job's own application key names the job and resume (`JobCards.assign`: the tab's choice for that application, kept in `jobchoice:<tab>` as Change's). With "Autofill when a queued tab opens" Automatically, that frame's first form gets `run/start {dryRun: false}` (the panel's Autofill's message), sent once the hello is answered; or, at a page with no form whose application frame (`wallFrameOf`) shows the posting's own Apply, an entry choice or an account wall, the panel's Autofill itself (`panel.ts` fill: its entry pressed, the next form started by the grant; a wall rests as Ready for you). Once per job (`startedAt`): the run's own "Autofill after page turn" takes later pages. Manually: nothing until the person's Autofill. The tab's settings are the person's (`contentSettingsFor`: never the agent's), so Submit follows Submit mode and every guard holds.
- **Status** (from the tab): opening → opened (a hello) → filling (a report at work, `statusOfReports`) ⇄ ready (a report at rest) → submitted (`submissions.ts`'s sign for the job's own application, through the `agentSubmitted` hook after the agent's own: `POST …/result {submitted}` with the reports' AI-guess count, never POST /applied; recorded, the form's questions and answers then go to POST /submissions as the agent's; the tab closes `QUEUE_CLOSE_SUBMITTED_MS`, 5 s, later, by a timer in the worker's memory, unless `QueueTabs.state` reads it as its window's active tab) / skipped (the person closed the tab: `needs-you` `cancelled` told) / failed (no hello within `QUEUE_FORM_WAIT_MS`, 60 s: `page_error`, or `posting_closed` where `tabs.atsPageOf` reads the tab's address as the platform's page that names no posting; the tab stays). A failed job's tab that later submits is still told submitted. A result TailorIT could not take is sent again at the next tick: a submitted one for every refusal but 404, 400 and a path refused (`DROPS_SUBMITTED`), another only after a passing failure (offline, a time-out, 5xx, 429, not connected, a version mismatch).
- **Wake-ups:** the `queue-tick` alarm (`QUEUE_TICK_MINUTES`, 0.5, Chrome's minimum) while the run goes, a tab waits for its page or a result waits; timers between (the next tab, a page's 60 s). A tick sweeps the open jobs' tabs (gone: skipped; silent past 60 s: failed; their reports read again), stops a running queue that lost its connection or `agent:run` (`connection`) or its acknowledged disclosure (`disclosure`), advances the schedule, then tells every result still waiting. `boot()` at start-up picks the run up after a worker restart. Chrome stops an idle worker after about 30 s, so a wait that finds the worker asleep opens its tab at the next alarm (docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md item 367).
- **Never beside the agent:** Start turns Run auto-apply off; `agent/set {on: true}` stops a running or paused queue first (`stoppedBy: 'agent'`); TailorIT never hands a tab run's row to `agent/next`. A changed or dropped connection stops the queue (`tokenChanged`: `connection`).

### Platform, messages, logs
- **Platform:** `WorkerPlatform.queueTabs` (`platform/types.ts` `QueueTabs`: `open`, `navigate`, `focus` (the tab active and its window focused), `state` (`gone`, `active`, `background`), `close`; `chrome.tabs` and `chrome.windows`, no permission). The runner reads no tab's address but through `tabs.atsPageOf` for its own silent tab.
- **Server client** (`server-client.ts` "v1.5 QX" blocks): `queuePage(cursor)` and `queueOpened(id)`; a cursor `AutofillAgentQueueQuerySchema` would refuse, or an id that is no queue id, is refused before any request (`server.path-refused`).
- **Page messages** (`messaging/page-schemas.ts`, each in `SIDEBAR_PAGE_MESSAGES`):

| Message | Answer | Note |
|---|---|---|
| `queue/status` | `QueueStatus`: phase (`QUEUE_PHASES`: idle, running, paused, finished, stopped), hold, `nextInMs`, total, opened, counts per status, open tabs, `maxOpenTabs`, `stoppedBy` (`QUEUE_STOPS`: person, agent, connection, disclosure, no-tab), `agentStopped`, `agentOn`, `blocked` (`QUEUE_BLOCKS`), `pending`, `autofillOnOpen`, `submit` (Manual until the disclosure is acknowledged) | v1.5 QX; value-free; sidebar too |
| `queue/list {offset}` | `QueueListAnswer {source: 'run' \| 'queue', items (≤ 50: id, title, company, status, reason, tab), offset, total, more}`: the run's jobs, or TailorIT's queued ones before a run | v1.5 QX; sidebar too |
| `queue/start`, `queue/stop`, `queue/pause`, `queue/resume` | `QueueStatus` (`queue/start` refused `unavailable` when the queue cannot be read) | v1.5 QX; sidebar too |
| `queue/focus {id}` | `{focused}` | v1.5 QX; the job's tab while it is open; sidebar too |
| `panel/changed {what: 'queue'}` (panel port, worker → page) | | v1.5 QX; `PANEL_CHANGES` gains `queue` |

- **The controls:** Start, Stop, Pause and Resume (`QueueView.tsx` `QueueButton`) each take a click only once their label was drawn `CONTROL_SEEN_MS` (600 ms, `useShownFor`), and only while they apply. In the sidebar the content script's gate takes trusted clicks alone, on a sidebar seen 600 ms; the queue's controls are not among its `HELD_BUTTONS` (`[data-step-action], [data-main-action], [data-tailor-action]`), whose own visibility it also checks (docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md item 369).
- **Logs** (`shared/log.ts`; counts, phases and places in closed lists only, never an id, an address or a title): `queue.started {jobs, agent}`, `queue.opened {k}`, `queue.open-failed`, `queue.fill-started {form}`, `queue.finished {status, reason}`, `queue.report-failed`, `queue.read-failed`, `queue.paused`, `queue.resumed`, `queue.stopped {by}`, `queue.done {jobs}`.

### The views (PLAN decisions 9 and 10)
- `ui/panel/TabBar.tsx`: a WAI-ARIA tablist under the main view's head (Autofill, a pencil; Queue, its own icon, `shared/icons.ts`): arrow keys, Home and End choose and focus; the chosen tab alone in the tab order; each tab names its panel. In the sidebar and Chrome's side panel alike; the Settings view and the profile editor have none.
- `ui/panel/QueueView.tsx`: the progress bar with "x of y" (the run's jobs no longer waiting of all of them; before a run "0 of" TailorIT's queued ones), the line under it counting down on animation frames ("Next tab in 8.5s"; or why the timer waits, paused, done, stopped and why), the counts past Opening, Start/Stop and Pause/Resume, the notes for Run auto-apply, a block and a result not yet told, every job with its status chip and why, a row with an open tab a button to it (`queue/focus`), Show More a page at a time; empty, not connected and error states. A live region says the phase and "x of y" (never the seconds). It reads again on `panel/changed` `queue`, `connection`, `settings` and `agent`.
- `Panel.tsx`: the tab bar under the head of the main view; the Queue tab in place of the stage.
- `SettingsView.tsx`: the owner's order (All Settings at the head's right, the connection or "Not connected to TailorIT", Autofill Information, Documents, Queue Settings, Autofill Settings); the queue's and the modes' choices are one draft, saved together with Save.
- `ui/page/modes.tsx` `QueueFields`: the seconds typed, kept as typed while focused, refused out of bounds (in tenths); Autofill when a queued tab opens; queued tabs at once; the waits they make ("Waits: 8 s, 8.5 s, 9 s, 9.5 s …").
- `Options.tsx`: Connection, then Documents, Queue and Modes (the panel's order), the rest as before.
- Styles in `v5-styles.ts` and `page/styles.ts`: radio cards two to a row (2 × 2 for more than two choices), the skip rules one per line.

### Settings version 11
`queue` (`QueueSettings`; `DEFAULT_QUEUE {durationSec: 8, accelerationSec: 0.5, autofillOnOpen: 'auto', maxOpenTabs: 5}`; `QUEUE_LIMITS`: duration 1 to 600 s and acceleration 0 to 60 s, in tenths, and 1 to 10 tabs; `shared/settings.ts` `QueueSettingsSchema`). `migrateSettingsV10` gives every version 10 value the defaults; every older migration goes through it (`SettingsV10Schema` reads version 10 to migrate it). Not in the content settings. Not read by `raisesAutonomy`: the panel on the page may save it.

### The mock
docs/CONTRACT.md "The mock in v1.5 part 2": GET `/agent/queue` with a query pages the rows it holds (running, queued, finished; `nextCursor`), POST `/agent/:id/opened` takes a queued row for the tab run, `agent/next` never takes or gives back a tab run's row.

### Disclosure version 14 (`shared/stored.ts` `DISCLOSURE_VERSION`, `COPY.options.connection.disclosure`)
docs/PRIVACY.md holds it word for word (`src/shared/copy.test.ts`). In the first paragraph, after Run auto-apply's sentence (which stays true: the background agent still opens each job in a window of its own): Start in the panel's Queue tab opens the jobs queued in TailorIT one by one in new tabs of this browser, on the person's timer (the seconds between tabs, growing by the seconds added each time), with no more unfinished queued tabs open at once than they allow; each fills by itself unless Autofill when a queued tab opens is Manually, and submits only with Submit on Auto, the rules under Modes still leaving a job for them; the list comes from their TailorIT, which is told as each job opens and how each went; a submitted job's tab closes a few seconds after the site confirms it, unless it is the tab its window shows; Run auto-apply and the queue never run at once. QX's draft said "no more queued tabs open at once than you allow" and "unless you are looking at it"; the code caps unfinished tabs alone (a failed tab stays and does not count) and keeps the tab its window shows, so the words follow it. A connected browser shows it again with I understand, and until then what it governs stays off as before, the Queue tab's Start among it.

### Sizes and checks
- **Sizes:** `sidebar-app.js` 788,030 bytes at `b9d0b9c`; builder QX's 815,763 (production; 815,865 e2e); with disclosure 14 (the docs' production Chrome build) 816,598, under the 819,200-byte budget (800 KiB), 2,602 bytes to spare. The Queue view is in the bundle, not lazy-loaded: a second injected chunk would need React shared across two injected scripts. The sidebar shows the disclosure in full (the set-up view's Connect, and the disclosure shown again when it changed), so its words stay in the bundle. The next view or disclosure added to the sidebar will need a lazy chunk, the disclosure's words moved out of the sidebar (a link to Chrome's side panel or the options page, where I understand already is), or a raised budget. `content-script.js` 863,503 bytes (production; budget 1 MiB), which the disclosure does not reach.
- **Tests (v1.5 QX):** `src/background/queue-runner.test.ts` (the schedule 8, 8.5, 9, 9.5 …; the cap held and released with its ms; pause and resume; stop; a worker restart; Run auto-apply off at Start; each status and what is told; TailorIT's finished, taken and removed rows; retries; nothing opened while blocked or offline; Automatically and Manually; no address in storage), `src/background/queue-worker.test.ts` (the real worker: messages and senders, Start to submitted, the agent both ways, a disconnect), `src/ui/panel/queue.browser.test.tsx` (the tab bar's keyboard and ARIA, the Queue tab's states and controls, the seen-click gate, paging, the settings' order and side-by-side radios, the queue fields, Options' card), `src/core/types/settings.test.ts` and `src/background/settings.test.ts` (version 11), `test/mock-server.test.ts` (the Queue routes), e2e `67-queue` (the schedule in a real browser, a tab filling, the statuses, Pause and Resume, Stop, the Queue tab and its accessibility), `e2e/visual/shots.spec.ts` (opt-in pictures).
- **The docs' checks** (`v15b-docs`, on `78b0772` with the pull and disclosure 14): `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm test`, `npm run test:dom`, `npx wxt build` and `node scripts/check-build.mjs` (no e2e); docs/SPEC.md, the trap specs 20, 21 and 29 and `fixtures/traps` unchanged.

## jr6: forms like Jobright's on eight platforms, the job-site password in this browser, and Autofill presses the site's Apply (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §19)
The owner's words of 2026-11-10 asked three things: autofill as good as Jobright's on Workday, iCIMS, ADP Workforce Now, SmartRecruiters, UltiPro, Oracle, Jobvite and JazzHR, from their snapshots; a job-site password kept in the browser and managed in the extension's settings; and Autofill pressing the site's own Apply, the word kept. They were built in three lines and merged on `jr6-integration`: line W (the forms: TOOLING, ENGINE E0 to E3, RULES, the eight platform packages, merged on `jr6-forms`), the job-site password (`jr6-password`, the base of line P), line P (A0, F1, F2, F3a, F4a, merged on `jr6-apply`), then SHELL (the sign-in shell and the email gates), the chain facts and these docs. What follows is the code as built on `jr6-integration`; where it and an earlier note differ, this is the newer word. docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §19 has the decisions; docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md §20 the questions.

### Sign-up Information: the job-site password in this browser (jr6-password)
It replaces the earlier notes on where Sign-in Auto's password comes from (v3 Phase 5's GET /credentials?host=, v4's `signin/credential` refusals, the API 6 notes on `&path=`).
- **The policy** (`core/signup-password.ts`, pure): 16 to 64 characters; at least one uppercase letter, one lowercase letter, one digit and one special of `! @ # $ % ^ & * ( ) - _ = + ?`; nothing but ASCII letters, digits and those specials. `signupPasswordChecks` (the checklist, rule by rule: `length`, `upper`, `lower`, `digit`, `special`, `allowed`), `signupPasswordValid`, `generateSignupPassword(random)` (20 characters: one of each required kind, the rest uniform over the allowed alphabet, placed by a Fisher-Yates shuffle, every index drawn by rejection sampling; the one required special is never `_`, which a site testing `\W` does not count). Workday's create-account rules on the captured tenants ("A minimum of 8 characters", an uppercase, a lowercase, a numeric, an alphabetic and a special) are met by every password the policy takes. Jobright (`~utils/workday-signup-password`, read for behaviour) takes 12 or more and any ASCII punctuation: ours is stricter.
- **The vault** (`background/signup-vault.ts` `createSignupVault`): one email and one password for every job site. The key: AES-GCM 256, made once by the worker with `crypto.subtle.generateKey(..., extractable false)` at the first save, kept in the extension origin's IndexedDB (`platform/indexed-db.ts`: database `tailorit`, store `secrets`, key `signup-key`, beside the token's `auth`; `WorkerPlatform.signupKey`), which no content script can open; a key found that is not such a key is never used, a new one is made. The record: `chrome.storage.local` `signup:v1` `{v: 1, iv, data, email, updatedAt}`, a fresh 96-bit nonce per save, the ciphertext with its tag (its additional data the constant `tailorit-signup:v1` alone: the email is not bound to it, owner question 387), the email in the clear. `status()` (set, email, updatedAt), `has()`, `save({email, password?})` (an email alone rewrites the record under the new email, nothing decrypted), `remove()` (the record and the key), `reveal()` (decrypts for the one answer about to be typed, or says why not: `none`; `gone`, a record whose key went or whose ciphertext or nonce changed, removed at once; `unreadable`, IndexedDB not answering, kept). Writes and reveals go one at a time; plaintext buffers are zeroed after use. **The jr6 integration fixes (SEC-1):** `reveal(rules?)` first checks the ask's page rules (`refusalOf`) and decrypts nothing where they refuse (`{ok: false, why: 'refused', refused}`). It reads no plaintext for it: the saved password's length is the ciphertext's less its 16-byte tag (`TAG_BYTES`; a saved password is ASCII, one byte a character); every class a page may require holds for any saved password (`EVERY_SAVED_HOLDS`: the save's policy requires each); "not the login" is refused only where the email could itself be a saved password (its length, and each character one a password may hold), which no email the vault takes is (its domain holds a dot). The check runs in `reveal`'s own queue, so no save lands between it and the decryption. What it protects and what it does not (the review's sec-7) is docs/PRIVACY.md's "At rest".
- **The pages' messages** (`messaging/page-schemas.ts`; `background/signup.ts` `createSignup`): `signup/status` → `SignupStatus {set, email, updatedAt, suggestedEmail, importable, notice?}` (the email box's first value when none is saved: the TailorIT profile's, read with `profileCache.get()`, else the account's; `notice`, `SIGNUP_NOTICES`, what the worker did by itself since the last save, import or delete: `'gone'` or `'reverted'`, kept in `storage.local` `signup:notice`); `signup/save {email, password?}`; `signup/delete` → `{status, reverted}`; `signup/import` → `{outcome: 'imported' | 'none' | 'weak', status}`, once, while none is saved and the connection holds `credentials:read` (`GET /api/autofill/credentials?host=import.myworkdayjobs.com`, `SIGNUP_IMPORT_HOST`: taken only for host `*` and a password the policy takes). None is in `SIDEBAR_PAGE_MESSAGES`: from the sidebar each is `bad_sender`, from TailorIT's pages refused too. After a save, a delete or an import the worker pushes every frame its settings (`typeSignIn`) and tells every panel `panel/changed {what: 'signup'}` (new in `PANEL_CHANGES`). `AuthStatus.signUp: 'set' | 'not-set'` is a word, never the email.
- **Sign-in mode's guard** (coordinator decision 1): `ui/page/modes.tsx` `signInAvailability`: `'connection'`, then `'password'` (no password saved; the old `'scope'` block, `credentials:read`, is gone), then `'disclosure'`. `settings/save` refuses a result holding Sign-in on Auto with none saved (`unavailable`), and looks at the stored result again after the write (sec-9). `signup.keepModeHonest(why)` sets Sign-in back to Manual with no password saved (after a delete, a sign-in's `reveal()` finding `gone` or `none`, a save of Auto that met a delete, and once at the worker's start-up), saved with the settings it read as `base` (R13), said on the card (`notice: 'reverted'`). Settings stay version 11. The content settings: `ContentSettings.typeSignIn` = `vault.has() && disclosed`, never in the auto-apply agent's window (`contentSettingsOf`'s `agent`, sec-3).
- **The source switch** (`background/sign-in.ts`): `signin/credential` never asks TailorIT. `SignInDeps {vault, session, now, signInAct(tabId, origin), claimFrame?, missing?, profileEmail?}`; `signInAct` (index.ts): `null` before the disclosure as it reads now was acknowledged; `'auto'` for Sign-in Auto or the tab's Sign up & Autofill grant for the sender's origin; `'type'` on Manual; in the agent's tab `null` on Manual and `'agent'` on Auto. The frame rules stand (a top frame, or the tab's claim frame; no private tab; https; Chrome's sender address); the page must be one `signInHostCovered(host, path)` covers, never a company's own page. None saved, or one whose key is gone: `not_found` under `'auto'` or `'agent'`, `unavailable` on Manual; `unreadable` (IndexedDB did not answer): `unavailable` in every mode (`sign-in.ts` `none`). The answer `{host: '*', email, password, press}` (`SignInCredentialAnswerSchema.press`, required); at an email gate `{host: '*', email, press}` from `vault.status()`, nothing decrypted (R8). `typing: true` (optional) marks an ask the frame never presses: answered `press: false`, the press limit neither read nor written (R1). A press is answered once per tab, wall and host within `SIGN_IN_ONCE_MS`; an Auto ask after it is typed for the person (`press: false`), never refused but in the agent's tab (`expired`). `server-client.ts` `credential()` stays, for the import alone. **The jr6 integration fixes (SEC-1):** the ask may carry `rules` (`SignInRulesSchema`, built by the frame with `messaging/sign-in-rules.ts` `signInRulesOf`: on a create-account wall the adapter's `passwordRules`, its `minLength`, `requires` and `notLogin`; on a sign-in or create-account wall the smallest `maxlength` of its password boxes, `maxLength`; none at an email gate; numbers held to `AUTOFILL_SIGN_IN_LIMITS.password`, 256, so a page minimum over 256 reads 256 in the toast). Where `reveal(rules)` refuses, the answer is `SignInRefusedSchema` (`{refused: 'fits'}` or `{refused: 'rules', rule}`, `rule` from `SIGN_IN_RULE_KINDS`): no password, no email, no press, logged `signin.refused {rules: <index>}` or `{fits: false}`, and before the `signin-tried:` key is written, so a refused ask is no press: a password saved after the toast is pressed as a first ask is, and the agent's tab is not refused `expired` for a wall it never pressed.
- **The content script** (`content/sign-in.ts`, `orchestrator.ts`, `runtime.ts`): `SignInContext.typeOnly` (Manual with a password saved) and the worker's `press`: the form's button is pressed only when the worker says `press` and the run is not `typeOnly`; else the email, the password, its confirmation and the account's terms are typed with every Sign-in Auto guard held, and the outcome is `'typed'` (new `SignInOutcome`): nothing pressed, the boxes kept for the person's press (`keepForPerson`) until the run stops or ends or the document goes, with no timer (R11). `guardSubmission` lasts as long as a typed password is kept (sec-1); over a form whose press is the person's (`personPresses`: an account section, SuccessFactors' account page) until the document goes. A press the site refused, or whose button never enabled, keeps what was typed `SIGN_IN_KEPT_MS` (180 s) at most (R1). A password longer than its box's `maxlength` is never typed (`'unavailable'`, R2). A run on Manual asks only once its document is shown (`shownToPerson`, sec-3); since the jr6 integration fixes (SEC-4) it presses a prelude that only shows the form (Workday's "Sign in with email", `formAfterPrelude`) only then too, and reads the page again once shown. Every box the password is typed into is marked (`dom/secret-boxes.ts`, a WeakSet) and `dom/kinds.ts kindOf` answers it an auth field whatever its type later (sec-5). `dropKeptSignIns(doc)` ends every keep at once when `settings/changed` says `typeSignIn` false (sec-8), and (jr6 INT-2) when the answer to the frame's `frame/hello` says so, as a page the back/forward cache kept comes back after the password was deleted meanwhile. A person-presses wall is typed once per document (`signInHandedOver`). The orchestrator: on Manual with `typeSignIn` a sign-in or create-account wall (`typeableWallShown`, which never names an email gate: on Manual a declared gate is typed through `paused()`'s own rule, `gateDeclared`, saved password or not: "The email-gate step") is typed (`signInTyping`), its toast `PAGE_COPY.toasts.signInTyped`, and the pause watch carries the run on once the person's press takes the wall away; a form's account section is typed on Manual too; a sign-in container (ADP's guest Continue, UltiPro's Create account) stays Auto's. `runtime.ts atWall`'s wall branch types on Manual (`showWallToast('typed')`, `WALL_WAITS` `sign-in-typed`). The no-sign-in toast opens the options page at `#settings-signup` through the worker (`sidebar/options {section: 'settings-signup'}`, also from the tab's claim frame).
- **The views:** `ui/page/signup.tsx` `SignUpFields` (words `ui/page/signup-copy.ts` `SIGNUP_COPY`, CSS `SIGNUP_CSS`): the status line; the email box; the password box (`type=password`, `autocomplete=new-password`, never filled back); Show/Hide (masked again after `REMASK_MS`, 30 s, and at once when the page is hidden or loses the focus, sec-8); "Generate strong password"; the live checklist (`li[data-rule][data-met]`); Save; Delete (asks first); "Import from TailorIT" where offered; the `notice` line; "Once saved, the password is not shown here again: note it somewhere safe first." beside Save (R6). The options page's card `signup` (`#settings-signup`) right after the connection; the panel's Settings section `signup` right after Autofill Information: in Chrome's side panel the fields (given by `entrypoints/sidepanel/main.tsx` through `ui/page/signup-slot.ts` `SignUpSlot`), in the sidebar a status line and a link to `options.html#settings-signup`. The fields live in a chunk the options page and the side panel load alone; `scripts/check-build.mjs` (`checkSidebarApp`, `checkNoSignupFields`) fails a build whose sidebar bundle or any content script holds `data-signup-password` or "Generate strong password" (R16). Sign Up & Autofill's "?" says to save a password first when `AuthStatus.signUp` is `not-set` (`COPY.mainView.signUpNeedsPassword`, R5).
- **How it relates to Jobright's account flow** (read for behaviour only: `~contents/sites/myworkday/account-preflow`, `~contents/pre-autofill-flow/account-flow`, `~utils/workday-signup-password`): Jobright keeps its password in `chrome.storage.local` in plain text and the email in its server profile, picks Create Account or Sign In from the view, fills email, password, Verify New Password and the privacy box, and waits for the person's own press. Ours keeps both in this browser, the password encrypted; on Manual it matches Jobright (typed, never pressed); on Auto (and Sign up & Autofill) it presses, as v3's Sign-in Auto did; the switch from Sign In to Create Account stays the person's (owner question 403).

### Autofill on a posting: press, follow, fill (A0, F1, F2, F3a, F4a)
The owner's words of 2026-11-10: "extension should also click `apply` to find and run the form ... and `Autofill` word will be kept". It replaces P2-fill's posting button ("v5 Phase 2" above) where they differ.
- **The contract (A0):** `entry/pressed {v, type, nextKey?}` (content → worker: the frame that pressed an entry; `nextKey`, the destination's application key, same origin only; answered `{}`, `DoneAnswerSchema`; content channel only); `frame/wall` and `PageState` gain `entering` (a flag) and `refusal` (`ENTRY_REFUSALS`: `none`, `ambiguous`, `blocked`, `new-tab`, `elsewhere`, `nothing-followed`; a refusal rides only beside `entryRefused`). The log codes `content.entry`, `content.entry-step`, `grant.armed`, `grant.step`, `grant.ended`, `queue.entry-timeout`.
- **The panel (F1, `ui/panel/Panel.tsx`):** `MainButton`'s label is `COPY.panel.fill` ("Autofill") for every meaning but sign-up ("Sign Up & Autofill", APPLYPLAN OQ1 kept); the seen key shares 'open' and 'fill', so a late Apply that flips the meaning never drops a click; `aria-busy` while it spins. `offersFill`: every page of ours with no form but the platform's page after submitting and a submitted one (D2); both meanings send a plain `panel/fill`. `PageState.entering`: the button disabled with its spinner, word unchanged, and a Cancel beside it (`panel/cancel`, which takes the focus the button had). `refusal`: one line, `p.tia-strip[data-strip="entry-refused"][data-reason]`, `COPY.mainView.entryRefused[reason]`, on any page without a form; a `'none'` that answers the panel's own click within `ENTRY_MODAL_MS` (30 s) opens the "no form" modal (`MODALS.errors['no-form']` = `entryRefused.none` = `NO_FORM`). No run line under the button on a page with no form; the no-form strip once (`COPY.strip.posting` on a posting, `COPY.strip.noForm` elsewhere) and only until a refusal; the status a screen reader hears (`p[data-entry-status]`: `COPY.mainView.entering`, then the refusal's words). The "?" is `COPY.mainView.entryHelp`.
- **The gate (F2, D6):** `content/runtime.ts entryOffered` offers on a page with no form whose address reads a posting or an application (never a confirmation) when an own (non-generic) adapter answers a visible entry control; `atWall` no longer needs a told entry. A page of ours with no form gets `run/start` on the panel's Autofill whatever it told (F3a, below).
- **`enter(source)` (F2):** one at a time (`entering`, `frame/wall {entering: true}` while it presses and watches); the page read again first (`lookAgain`: a form now is filled at once, the run started here, a dry run's too; the own adapter first, the generic one only where no adapter declares `formLateMs`, `formAtPosting` or `formInPlace`); with no Apply declared, a form an own adapter draws late is waited for, else `'none'`; then `press.ts pressEntry` (waited for `entryWaitOf`: 8 s, Workday and Oracle 15 s, at most 20 s), with the step the site shows after it (the adapter's declared choice, waited 8 s, `ENTRY_FOLLOW_MS`; or the generic second step), through `not-submit.ts`'s doors (`clickAdapterEntry`, `clickSecondStep`, lint-held to `press.ts`; their wrappers to `runtime.ts`); `ENTRY_MAX_PRESSES` stays 2. A run's own press at a declared choice (`RunEnv.enterChoice`, `press.ts pressEntryChoice(doc, adapter, address)`) passes the frame's address too since the jr6 integration fixes (SEC-2), so the door's another-site refusal holds there as at the entry. At each press the person's entry sends `entry/pressed` (with `nextKey` only for a same-origin `<a href>` an adapter reads as another application or posting); the agent's sends `agent/entered` once. Then `afterPress` watches the document for `ENTRY_FORM_WAIT_MS` (20 s, or the address's longer `formLateMs`): a form drawn in place is claimed by the address's own adapters, a posting address included, and the worker's grant starts its run; a wall goes to the sign-in step (`wallStep`, jr6-password's `typeOnly` kept), never a refusal; a check or a code is `'blocked'`; nothing is `'nothing-followed'`. Since the jr6 integration fixes (B2) a look that presses the generic second step, the cap's last look included, watches again for the same window, within `ENTRY_MAX_PRESSES`: what that press brings is followed, never refused `'nothing-followed'` in the same moment. A new document is the worker's (the grant). `run/cancel` ends the waits: nothing pressed or refused after it. So does (B1) a `pagehide` that keeps the page in the back/forward cache (`runtime.ts cachedAway`): an entry or a wall's own sign-in step under way ends quietly, as at `run/cancel`, its toast is cleared and a wait for a password to be saved is dropped; on the return (a persisted `pageshow`, `restored`) the frame says hello, then tells its wall afresh, so the panel's Autofill and Sign Up & Autofill act at once again. A hello whose answer says a password may now be typed wakes a wall step that waits for one, as `settings/changed` does.
- **The door's refusals (F2, D4):** `clickAdapterEntry(adapter, doc, address?)` answers `HTMLElement | EntryDoorRefusal | null`; `entryWhereRefusal`: a link with `download` (`'download'`, said as `'none'`), a link or a form's submitter naming another browsing context (its target, `formtarget`, its form's target or the document's `<base target>`, compared as written: anything but empty, `_self`, `_top`, `_parent`) (`'new-tab'`, until OQ3), a link to another http(s) origin given the frame's address (`'elsewhere'`, until OQ4). Nothing is pressed; the agent hears `blocked 'other'`.
- **The second step (F2, D7):** `adapters/second-step.ts` `secondStepControl` and `SECOND_STEP_WORDS` ("Apply manually", "Apply without (an) account", "Apply as (a) guest", "Continue as (a) guest", "Start (your) application", "Continue to application") and `NEVER_WORDS` (Autofill with Resume, Use My Last Application, another site's profile, a sign-in, an account's creation); `clickSecondStep(adapters, doc, before, address?)`: the one control a dialog shown since our press offers, never one that submits a form, one an adapter never presses (Taleo's "Apply as Guest" stays never pressed), or one whose press would go where the entry door refuses. Only where no adapter choice shows.
- **The quirks (F2, D5):** `formAtPosting` (Workday: its flow can sit at `…/apply`, which its parser reads as the posting; that adapter alone claims there, `noFormYet`, `watchLateForm`), `formInPlace` (ADP's guest view drawn at the details' address: a document-long cheap watch; the generic adapter never claims that address); SmartRecruiters and UltiPro declare `formLateMs` 30 s, ADP 20 s. The late watches hear `class`, `style`, `hidden` and `open` where they touch a control (`PROMPT_PARTS`) or a form part (`FORM_PARTS`, company pages), 50 records read at most. A posting's text is read for policy text on an application address with no form too, where an adapter's `postingText` names it (ADP-10's runtime part, BUILD D-e).
- **The line P review's rules (F2's fixer):** an entry belongs to the application its address read when it began: a route of the page's own to another one (or to a page of none) ends it as `run/cancel` does (`onAddress`; each look and press: `pressEntry`'s `here`); the entry's sign-in step ends with the entry (the panel's Cancel leaves nothing typed or pressed); on an application page that offers no Apply, a form drawn late is waited for the rest of the address's late window, then filled, `none` only after it, and only an own adapter's form starts the run so; never on the platform's page after submitting; the card of an entry page (ADP's details) loads through the panes drawn at its address (the frame relays `job/refresh` while its document told `entry` there). Log flags: `content.entry` gains `moved`, `content.sign-up` `cancelled`, `content.agent-entry` `wall`.
- **The worker's grant (F3a, `background/sign-up.ts`):** the panel's Autofill at a page with no form (`panel.ts fill`) sends `run/start` to the tab's application frame (`frames.ts wallFrameOf`: its claim frame, else its top frame) whatever that frame told (D2). Where it shows a wall or a choice, the grant `{signIn, fill}` is given at once (v4). Anywhere else it is a pending entry grant (`Grant.entry`); never for a dry run. A grant holds `documents` (each it sent `run/start` to or started) and, for an entry, `entry: {pending, armBy, pressedAt?, lastAt?, keys, steps, taken}`.
  - **Pending:** `takeFill` starts nothing; it ends at `ENTRY_ARM_MS` (30 s: the frame's 20 s cap and 10 s), at a refusal from a document it reached, or at the panel's Cancel (`endEntry`).
  - **Armed** by `entry/pressed` (`pressed`): only from a document in `documents`, on the grant's origin; never a fill grant with no key (a pending grant whose frame named no application and whose press named no `nextKey` ends there). A press re-arms; its `nextKey`s accumulate in `entry.keys`.
  - **`nextKey`:** `takeFill` and `takeStep` take a key in `entry.keys` within `ENTRY_NEXT_MS` (60 s) of the latest press, on the grant's origin (SmartRecruiters' one-click page without the referrer chain, C4). The button rule (a company page's grant whose presses named no key starts the first form of its origin within 60 s, once) is dormant until F5 gives the `site` adapter an entry.
  - **Key rule (`fits`):** the grant's key where both name one (v4 M1); a v4 wall or choice grant given where the frame named no key takes any form of its origin; an entry grant, and every step, never take the keyless "any", except (the line P review, revA-1) a frame naming no application (Workday's account page) within 60 s of the grant's latest press, step or kept re-press (`entry.lastAt`).
  - **`takeStep` (D3):** a `frame/wall` of the tab's application frame, in its registered document, showing a wall or a choice and no form, after a press of this grant, in a document not in `documents`, of the grant's origin and key: one `run/start` (with `signIn` where the grant holds Sign up & Autofill), at most `ENTRY_MAX_STEPS` (3) per grant, within the grant's time and 60 s of its latest press, step or kept re-press: a step continues the press's own chain, never a page the person opens later. A gate that is a form root, started by `takeFill`, is never started again as a step; the pressed document itself is never stepped (the frame answers its own wall or choice).
  - **A press again (`keep`):** a plain Autofill at a document of the grant's origin and key while an armed grant still holds its fill keeps the grant (that document joins `documents`); Sign-in Auto ends with it. Sign up & Autofill there is a new grant.
  - **Waiting:** `PageState.entering` from the worker while armed and nothing taken since the press, at most `ENTRY_WAITING_MS` (30 s); it also ends (`settled`, P-B1) when the application frame, in a document the grant reached, says after the press a `frame/wall` without `entering`, so the wall's Sign Up & Autofill, or Autofill at the choice, is pressable at once.
  - **Elsewhere:** `moved` (OQ4 not answered) still ends a grant on another origin; within 60 s of its latest press the tab says `entryRefused: true, refusal: 'elsewhere'` for `ENTRY_ELSEWHERE_MS` (60 s), until the next grant or press; never on a page with a form.
  - **Cancel** (`panel.ts cancel`): `run/cancel` also reaches the application frame while its registry says `entering` (beside a wall or a choice its press drew too); a pending or armed entry grant of the tab ends (Sign-in Auto told gone where it held it).
  - **`entry/pressed`'s handler** (index.ts): counted first (`ENTRY_PRESSED_LIMIT`, 6 a minute per tab, `rate_limited`); `bad_sender` unless the sender is the tab's application frame, in the document registered for it, with Chrome's address on that frame's origin; then `pressed`: `{}` when armed, else `expired`.
  - **The card (apply-b-new-2):** `FrameEntry.entrySaid` (the document said `entry` once); `cardFrameOf` takes, after the form frame, the posting and the confirmation, a top frame with no form, `entrySaid` and an application key (ADP's details at an application address); its first `entrySaid` sets the tab's `jobs.applicationShown` and loads the card once.
- **The queue (F4a, `background/queue-runner.ts`):** a job whose Autofill the queue gave at a page with no form (the posting's own Apply, an entry choice, an account wall) and whose tab no run has reported from since is Ready for you `QUEUE_ENTRY_WAIT_MS` (90 s) after that start (`entryAt`, saved with the run in `storage.session`, so it survives a worker restart; its own timer, paused and stopped runs too); it keeps its place among the open tabs (`maxOpenTabs`), nothing is told TailorIT, and `queue.entry-timeout {from}` logs the status it left. The queue never continues the press itself: its Autofill is the panel's, whose grant takes the next documents' walls and choices; its start stays once per job. Since SHELL-B (item 3), a queued job Filling whose tab no fill report shows for 90 s (its page turned to a document where no run starts: Autofill after page turn on Manually, a wall the grant does not take) is Ready for you too: `quietAt` (a time, saved with the run; null for a run saved before) is set at the first sweep that finds no report and cleared by a report, `queue.entry-timeout {from, quiet: true}` logs it, its place among the open tabs is kept, nothing is told or pressed, and the alarm stays while any open job is Filling (paused and stopped runs too). 'opened' (Manually) and 'ready' jobs are never timed.
- **The email-gate step (SHELL-A, BUILD D-d):** at an `'email-gate'` pause whose adapter declares `signInForm(doc, 'email-gate')` (iCIMS's `form#enterEmailForm`, Oracle's gate; in the top frame or the claim frame), in every Sign-in mode, the orchestrator first fills the gate as a step (`gateFilling`, `gateFillable`, `gateDeclared`: `pauseNow()` hides that gate during the scan and the fill; `validateStep` and `end()` clear it), so the plan writes the profile's email, iCIMS's phone and country code (OQ8's default, yes) and the gate's consent boxes as the Consents mode says; then the AUTH pause, where `paused()` makes the sign-in step due in every mode (`typing` on Manual). At a wall with no form, runtime `wallStep` does the same for a declared gate. `content/sign-in.ts`: at a gate the sign-in step ticks only the terms the page marks required (`markedRequired`: required or aria-required, in either Consents mode); a `personPresses` gate ends `'typed'` (its Next the person's); Next is pressed only when the worker says `press` and the run is not `typeOnly`. `background/sign-in.ts` answers an email gate `{host: '*', email, press}`: the Sign-up Information email, else the profile's contact email (`gateEmail`, `deps.profileEmail()`: `index.ts` reads `profileCache.get()` as any Autofill run reads the profile, so the profile the worker holds, else fetched from TailorIT (GET /profile with its saved answers, as every profile fetch) once the cache dropped it after `PROFILE_IDLE_MS` (5 min unused) or the worker restarted, as at a gate the person comes back to later; not connected, nothing is fetched and the gate is the person's (the background fixes' `signup-worker.test.ts` "jr6 ifix SEC-3" pins it through `index.ts`'s wiring; the jr6 integration review, SEC-3: kept, the notice's "it fetches your profile" covers it); trimmed, at most `AUTOFILL_SIGN_IN_LIMITS.email` characters), never a password (`vault.reveal` is never called for it); with none saved, `missing('none')` is still called. Logs `signin.credential {found, press, again, gate, profile}`, never an email. Toasts `PAGE_COPY.toasts.gateTyped` (typed; the person's Next) and `gate`. The consent-scope select (aon's), codes and CAPTCHAs are never touched.
- **The sign-in shell (SHELL-A; FIXPLAN §6.12 items 1, 3 and 4, WD-02, ICIMS-07, WD-05, WD-04):**
  - **The page's own password rules:** `content/sign-in.ts brokenRule`, on a `create-account` wall or account section, before any write: `adapter.passwordRules?.(form)` (Workday's "Password Requirements", iCIMS's create-login rules; a throw or null fails open, a shape it does not know is no rule). Refused when the saved password is shorter than the page's minimum, misses a class it lists (`upper`, `lower`, `digit`, `special`, `alpha`) or, where the page's rules say so (`notLogin`: iCIMS's create-login rules), equals the login (the email, any case): nothing is typed there, not even the email; outcome `'unavailable'`, its rule in a per-document WeakMap (`signInDetailOf(doc)`), log `content.sign-in-refused {rules: <index in PASSWORD_RULE_KINDS>}`, never the password or its length; toast `PAGE_COPY.toasts.passwordRule(kind, minLength)`. Since the jr6 integration fixes (SEC-1) the same rules, and the boxes' `maxlength`, go with the ask and the worker refuses first ("The source switch"); a refusal it answers is said as `fill()` says its own (`'unavailable'`; a rule kept in `signInDetailOf(doc)` with the ask's `minLength`; `content.sign-in-refused`), and `brokenRule` and R2's `maxlength` check stay in `fill()` on what is answered.
  - **Account errors:** `accountErrorShown(ctx, form)` reads the adapter's `errors()` a person can see through `accountErrorKind` (`ACCOUNT_ERROR_KINDS`: verify-email, reset-password, wrong-credentials), the first kind. On arrival a verify-email alert means nothing is asked or typed (`'unavailable'`); after a press that ended `'failed'` the kind is kept (log `content.sign-in {account: n}`) and the outcome stays `'failed'` (kept 180 s). Toast `PAGE_COPY.toasts.account[kind]`. The page's words are never kept or logged.
  - **The wait for a password (WD-04):** orchestrator `waitForPassword()` (a run at its wall) and runtime `passwordSaved()` / `passwordWaiters` (a wall with no form): after `'none-saved'` under Auto or Sign up & Autofill, once per run or step, `PASSWORD_WAIT_MS` (5 minutes, as Jobright's 300 s; tests set `RunOptions.signIn.passwordWaitMs`) for a `settings/changed` with `typeSignIn: true`, ended by the run's stop, Cancel or the document's end; then, while the wall still shows, one more sign-in as the pause would decide it now. Toasts `noSignInWaiting`, then `noSignIn`. Never in the auto-apply agent's run: its window is never told a password may be typed (`background/settings.ts contentSettingsOf`), so the wait could never end in a second try.
  - **Cancel during a wall's own sign-in step:** runtime `ownWallStep()` runs `atWall`'s wall branch under the entry's one-at-a-time state (`entering`, `entryCancel`, `entryKey`; `sendWall` at its start and end, so the frame says `{wall, entering: true}`) and passes the cancel signal into `wallStep` and `signIn`: Cancel stops typing and pressing at once. The two windows measure different things: `ENTRY_NEXT_MS` (60 s, `sign-up.ts`) is how long after a grant's latest press a new document's wall or choice may still get a step; `ENTRY_WAITING_MS` (30 s) is how long the worker itself says `entering` for a press nothing was taken from yet. A step given later says `entering` itself, so no step is left without a spinner and a Cancel.
  - **The status a screen reader hears** (`Panel.tsx`, `p[data-entry-status]`): `COPY.mainView.signingIn` ("Autofill is filling in the site's sign-in.") while the frame does the sign-in step beside a wall, `COPY.mainView.entering` while it presses the site's Apply.
  - **An entry choice is pressed once:** orchestrator `enterChoiceNow` (Workday's Apply Manually, ADP's Consent & Continue, Jobvite's and Taleo's "I Accept") no longer carries the run on at once after its press; it waits for the choice to go (`watchPauseGone`). A choice drawn in place goes at once (Workday, ADP); a consent whose press posts its form (Jobvite) stays shown until the next page loads and is never pressed again (before: about 20 presses in 5 s, bounded only by `MAX_AUTO_CONTINUES`).
- **The chain facts (SHELL-B, ICIMS-17):** an iCIMS IForm leaf frame shows no step bar. The tab's claim frame (the portal page that frames it) reads its bar (`Adapter.chainStepFacts`: the step's id, whether it is the last, the packet counter "( n/m )", how many counters; `content/chain-frame.ts watchChainFacts`, after its hello and 300 ms after each page change, said only when changed) and says it to the worker (`chain/facts`, `ChainStepFactsSchema`). The worker (`background/chain.ts factsSaid`) keeps it for that frame's document (`storage.session` `chainfacts:<tab>:<frame>`, no address; only from the tab's claim frame, in its registered document, holding an application, on a portal whose pages frame chain pages) and hands it (`chain/leafFacts`) to the leaf frames chained from it in the same tab: at once, or to a leaf chained later once the portal document still answers a `frame/ping` itself (`leafChained`). The leaf holds it (`holdChainFacts`) only where its portal loaded it (its referrer a portal of its family framing this origin); a leaf that posted its own form holds none. Its step machine (`validateStep`'s `isFinalStep` / `nextButton`, `pressNext`), the doors (`not-submit.ts` `NotSubmitContext.facts`: `submitButton`, `nextButton`, `isFinalStep`, `stepSubmitNext.laterStep`; `leafFormSign`) and, since the integration, `content/press.ts` (`PressPage.facts`: `finalityOf`, `buttonOf`, `stepButtonsOf`, `pressStep`, so the panel's buttons and its press) ask the adapter with it: the leaf's own "Submit" is Next while a later form or step follows, and the final Submit, through the Submit gates alone, on the last. No facts, two counters or a counter out of its packet: unknown, nothing pressed. Only ids, a boolean and counts cross (schema-checked both ways; logs `content.chained {facts, held}`, `frame.chained` / `frame.chain-refused {facts}`); still no message between frames. `src/content/transition.ts` (a person's own Next click) and `not-submit.ts nearApplicationButtons` still ask without facts: on an IForm leaf both answer null (a missed person's Next on a page that posts anyway; no sign-in form there).
- **The frame registry's claim (SHELL-B item 2):** `frames.ts claimFrameOf`: of several claimed frames below a wrapper (a wrapper that reloads leaves its old claimed frame registered until the sweep), the one that holds a form is the claim; several or none holding one: none.
- **The chain end to end:** e2e `68-apply-in-place` (the panel's Autofill on a posting with no told Apply, `run/start` to the application frame, the late Apply waited for and pressed, the grant armed by `entry/pressed`, the form drawn in place filled; a dialog's "Apply manually" pressed, never "Sign in"); e2e `62-open-application` (Lever's posting; a posting whose Apply never comes; a list of jobs). e2e `69-apply-platforms` (CHAINS; the owner's captures at their own addresses, Sign-in on Manual, nothing submitted): Workday's resolvetech posting, its Apply drawn late, then Apply Manually and the account view, where the saved password is typed and Create Account left to the person; ADP's details, Apply, Consent & Continue and the guest view drawn in place, filled, its Continue the person's; iCIMS's careers-ars posting, Apply and iCIMS's redirect to the gate, the email typed on Manual and Next untouched; a posting with no Apply, the "no form" window. The dom chains (`src/content/jr6-chains.browser.test.ts`, the real runtime and the registry's adapters on the owner's captures): ADP's one-document chain; Jobvite's cross-document consent chain (the residence chosen, "I Accept" pressed once, the next document carrying on by itself); Workday verily's `/login?redirect=` page telling the sign-in wall and its steps at `/apply` claimed under the same key; Workday resolvetech's Apply Manually to the entry's sign-in step; UltiPro's late `#OpportunityApply` claimed.

### The engine: widgets, reading, doors and the loop (ENGINE E0 to E3)
- **The contract (E0, `adapters/types.ts`, `adapters/configured/types.ts`):** `WidgetDecl.input` (the widget's own text box outside it: its part, never a field; opened before typing), `minQueryLength`, `freeText` (a search whose typed text stands when no result matches), `knownOptions` (the whole list as the page publishes it, the scan-time reading, winning over `storedList`), `tags` (`WidgetTags {chips, add?, search?, cap?}`: a box that takes several answers one at a time, scanned as kind `'text'`); `WidgetResults.options(list)` and `pressTarget(option)`; `Adapter.optionTextFor` (a radio's or box's words from the text run after it, as a person sees them), `controlBox` (the element that draws a borderless control's box), `dependsOn` (the control whose write resets this one), `accountRows` (the account part as rows, beside `accountSection`, which stays one element: FIXPLAN's widened `accountSection` could not compile against jr6-password's iCIMS test), `passwordRules` (`PagePasswordRules`, read fail open), `accountErrorKind` (`'verify-email' | 'reset-password' | 'wrong-credentials'`), `createAccountSwitch` (Workday's "Create Account" link: never pressed), `chainStepFacts` (`ChainStepFacts {stepId, lastStep, packet, counters}`, a portal frame's step bar for its IForm leaf), the optional `facts` on `isFinalStep`, `nextButton`, `stepSubmitNext.laterStep` and `submitButton(root, facts?)`, `repeatables()[i].removeButton` (never pressed: gated), `quirks.resumeParseCapMs` (gated), `quirks.formAtPosting`, `quirks.formInPlace`; `UploadRule.reveal` (built into `Adapter.uploadReveal`) and `SuccessRule.underModal`.
- **The widgets (E1, `dom/widgets`):** declared rows read and chosen (`WidgetResults.options`, `pressTarget`, `chooseResult`/`pressTargetOf`), across shadow trees and slots; the declared input as the widget's part, opened before typing; `minQueryLength`; `freeText`; a declared combobox acts whatever its trigger's ARIA; the scanner reads every declaration before its loop (`declarationsOf`); `watchList`, `watchAnswer`; an undeclared grid, tree or listless combobox is refused (`refusedUndeclared`) and what an activation opened is put back (`closeUnlisted`).
- **Reading and doors (E2):** account rows in pause detection (`accountRowsIn`); `controlBox` in the camouflage check; option words from `optionTextFor` (the visible-words rule); a validation message's suffix dropped from an explicit label (ADP-5); `visibleBesideFocusTrap`; the camouflage walk's overflow cuts follow containing blocks (iCIMS's dropdown results); declared busy signs read beside the declared list; the upload reveal door (`not-submit.ts clickUploadReveal`, once per document, navigation cancelled, a 300 ms settle, `needs-your-click` when the input stays hidden; in the "never submit" door list and lint-held, `test/jr6-upload-reveal-lint.test.ts`); `SuccessRule.underModal` (`shownUnderRule`: a sign hidden only by a modal's focus trap counts while it shows); a form root that stays shown counts as left once `detect` answers no form root (Jobvite's confirmation).
- **The loop and fillers (E3):** the tags filler (`widgets/tags.ts fillTags`, a per-value guard `pass.ts tagGuard`, `TAGS_ADD_CAP_MS`, `TAGS_FIELD_BUDGET_MS`; rows incl. `max-selections-reached`; exact results only, no chip removed; read back by its chips; `press.ts requiredLeftOf` counts a tags box with a chip answered); date parts get an untrusted focus and focusin before each write (`FOCUS_EVENTS`, beside `ALLOWED_EVENTS`, `KEY_EVENTS` and `EDITING_EVENTS`) and one digits retry where the page's alert shows; `markedInvalid` reads a date's alert after the settle; `dependsOn` in the loop: `parentsFirst`, `awaitDependents` (`DEPENDENT_QUIET_MS`, `DEPENDENT_WAIT_CAP_MS`), `ScanInput.dependsOn` and `replaced`, `PENDING_CAUSES` `'parent-written'` and `'reshaped'` (`dependsAgain`); the scanner's `withAdapterFormat` takes an adapter's same-format pattern over a `type`-only format (UltiPro's phones, U-16).
- **The loop's order** (FORMS-MERGE, `content/loop.ts` `Loop.queue`): a signature box last (`signatureLast`), after the phone's country before its number and the current-job box before its end date, before `resumeFirst` / `filesLast`.
- **The sidebar's bundle** reads `SHOWN_CATEGORIES` from `core/steps/shown-categories.ts`, never `blocks.ts`, so it carries no resolver (`test/sidebar-no-resolver.test.ts`; 816,598 → about 760,000 bytes).
- **The fix round** (ENGINE, after the line W reviews): the reveal door's navigation guard is a window click listener in the capture phase, removed after the door's click (it runs before the page's handlers, so they see the click cancelled); the tags filler's field budget counts the values tried (`fillTags(…, budgetMs)`), and an Add the page takes nothing of stops the box (`needs-your-click`); a tags search never chooses a result its list marks chosen (`SearchOptions.keepChosen`); a search closes a select-only anchor its own activation opened (a second click); a write whose `dependsOn` parent waits for its options this pass (or is held itself) is held back, unwritten, with no row, still pending (`loop.ts heldDependents`), and the books' `holdBack` (`LoopState.heldBack`) keeps the scan after it from reading as a stall.
- **The upload reveal door's lint** (FORMS-MERGE): the door `clickUploadReveal` is banned outside `dom/fillers/upload.ts` (`UPLOAD_REVEAL_RULE`), and its wrapper `revealUpload` outside `content/pass.ts` (`UPLOAD_REVEAL_WRAPPER_RULE`: an import, an export or a property read), as `pressEntry` and `signIn` are outside `runtime.ts` (`test/jr6-upload-reveal-lint.test.ts`).
- **Not built** (owner-gated): the entries' removal (Q1), UltiPro's parse cap (Q3), smartrecruiters-new-1 (Q5). The chain facts (ICIMS-17) are SHELL-B's (above).

### The resolver's jr6 rules (RULES, items 1 to 79)
- **New categories and rule ids** (`core/resolver/categories.ts`, `labelRules.ts` `JR6_RULES` before §8.3's list, `jr6-labels.test.ts` holding each label with its category and rule id): among them `address.county` (`countyChoice` ready for a profile county), `location.policyResidence` (Jobvite's residence: the option naming the profile's country, else the only one, else Global English, flagged), `referral.employeeReferred`, `location.residesIn`, `location.inCountry` (from the address, flagged, never a relocation), `location.inTimeZone` (from the state, `usTimeZones.ts`, public facts), `experience.yearsIn` (yes when the entries' titles reach N, no when all experience falls short, else the AI), `skills.experienceWith` (yes from the skills or entries, else the AI), `skills.qualifications` (a checklist of qualification sentences ticks only the options naming a skill or a degree, flagged), `workAuth.status` (work status by its meaning, `workStatus.ts`: the options' countries decide the entry, citizenships give a citizen's status, non-citizen sentences from the authorization and sponsorship answers), `workAuth.opt`, `screening.militaryTraining`, `education.graduated`, `education.highSchool`, the pay basis, the résumé method, a former name, a prefix, an address's third line, a mailing address if different, Facebook.
- **The `all` scope** (`AI_OPENED_CATEGORIES`) adds `screening.restrictiveAgreement`, `screening.relatives`, `workAuth.opt` and `screening.militaryTraining` (the fix round: never `screening.essentialFunctions`, which asks about a disability or the accommodation it needs, a self-identification's topic: the profile's explicit answer only, else the person's, revD-01; and every answer to a restrictive agreement, relatives or military training is written as an AI guess whatever basis TailorIT gives, `GUESS_ONLY_CATEGORIES` in `plan/frame.ts planAnswer`, so a legal guess is never shown as a fact and the agent's legal-guess rule sees it, revD-04); the standard scope (`ANSWERABLE_PROFILE_CATEGORIES`) `experience.yearsIn`, `skills.experienceWith`, `location.inTimeZone`, `location.inCountry`, `education.graduated` and `education.highSchool`. Never asked of an AI: a former name, a prefix, an address's third line, a mailing address only if different, Facebook and Oracle's numbered links; a box whose own words name another person (references) is never drafted.
- **Other rules:** a job's or a school's own block never takes the home address (`values.ts HOME_PLACE_CATEGORIES`); an EEO decline box is ticked only for the person's explicit decline the list beside it lacks, and that list is then skipped (`plan/eeoDeclineBox.ts`); a follow-up whose answer above is its opposite takes its one "Not Applicable" (`conditional.not-applicable`, flagged); `declineInstead` declines only a list it read whole that names none of the person's answers; an "own work" statement attests only on a box or a choice; the state lived in with the list's "none of these"; which clearance is held (never inferred from citizenship); a referral list with its own "Other" never goes to an LLM tiebreak; a signature box ticked on the signature's terms, last; a secondary phone left empty beside the primary; a place too long for its box written "City, ST", never cut; a postal code searched by five digits then three, a search's address result chosen when it starts with the street and names the city or state; Oracle's numbered links in the order LinkedIn, GitHub, website; help text's runs of file types dropped before the rules read it; a held résumé behind a replace control (iCIMS) stays the person's under "Upload mine again".
- **The fix round** (RULES, after the stop): `address.city` carries its place parts, so a search's "City, State, Country" result is chosen by `matchPlace` while the typed query stays the city (`resolve.ts searchPlace`); the region option matcher reads "TX - Texas" or "Texas - TX" as the one region both halves name (`prepareOptions`, `pairedRegionHits`, as `canonicalRegion` reads a profile value); a companion or decline box beside a list still waiting for its options is left empty (skipped, `conditional-question`, never `waiting-for-options`), and a companion planned without its list follows what the list shows; the answer screen's identifier pattern sets aside a title-case "Id" starting a name (`PROPER_NAME_ID`: "Id Software"), identifiers still screened; the decline vocabulary gains "Decline self-identification"; `EXPERIENCE_WITH` reads past "using" after "with"; label refinements R4 (an outside-business capacity needs for, by, of or with before the vendor word), R14 (the school labels), R49 (a chosen or preferred first name drops the given-name autocomplete vote; §8.1's Nickname keeps its conflict) and R68 (the pay basis anchored at the label's end).
- **docs/design/core.md §8.3** keeps its list as written; its refinements and the jr6 rules are recorded beside it ("jr6's rules and refinements").
- **The answer vocabulary** was regenerated from the curated tables alone (the build's sandbox could not reach the vocabulary's sources): on the unchanged tables it reproduced the committed files byte for byte. Where the network allows, `npm run vocab:fetch && npm run vocab:build` should leave `src/core/vocab` and `vocab/reports` unchanged (RULES' open issue 1; not run yet: INT-1's open item 4).

### The eight platforms (line W's packages; each adapter's header holds its jr6 paragraph)
- **Workday** (`src/adapters/workday/`): the `/login?redirect=` reading (B6: a same-origin path naming this host's posting or application, and the same site as the login path, read as that application, page `application`; anything else none), `formAtPosting` (B5); the hooks `passwordRules`, `createAccountSwitch` (never pressed), `accountErrorKind` (verify-email from a capture; reset and wrong-credentials words synthetic), `removeButton` (never pressed), Skills as `tags` (cap 30); Education sections by name (WD-20); Self Identify's labels (WD-10: the legend stays the label unless the title says "disabilit…"); the 26 jr6 pages' plans pinned in `jr6-workday.browser.test.ts`. The fix round: the address parser is two expressions (the hosts; the path behind the host's mark, the locale decided once), so the page-world bridge holds it, B6 included, within its 26 KiB budget.
- **iCIMS** (`src/adapters/icims/`): dropdowns as widgets (the box beside, its results, `dependsOn` for a list a write resets, and since the integration fixes (ICIMS-24, RULES-R65-R24's optional trigger) for a companion text box: one whose own label says the list lacks it, "Enter school name if not in the list above", in the row right after a list of its block, is decided again once that list is written, `listAboveOf`, so a school the School search does not find is typed into the box after the list's "Other"), the date parts' labels, the IForms' captions, titles and option words (`optionTextFor`), Basic Information's account rows (`accountRows`, answered only inside `div.iCIMS_BasicProfilePane`; a "Create a login" group holding anything else pauses as before), the step facts (`chainStepFacts`, relayed by SHELL-B), the held résumé, the Assessments rule; the fix round's IForm signature date: a date part whose caption's own words are the bare "Date", in a form holding `input[name="icims_f_signature"]` and with no `label_<base id>`, reads "signature month|day|year" (`labelSuffix`, `bareSignatureDate`), so NYU's date is the signature date as edgewater's is. The posting's Apply unchanged (measured working).
- **ADP Workforce Now** (`src/adapters/adp/`): the jr6 states `consent` (`sdf-focus-pane#jdv_privacy_dailog`, `auth: 'entry-choice'`, its entry the primary custom button checked by its "Consent & Continue" aria-label; "Decline & Close" in `neverPress`) and `code-choice` (`#login_view_identifier_dialog`, the `email-code` pause); the posting rule (`posting.text` `div.job-description-data`; its entry the header's Apply, never the filter dialog's `button#filter-apply-handler`); quirks `{entryPresses: 2, formLateMs: 20_000, formInPlace: true}`; the uploads and the disconnected-input reading; its own lists as declared comboboxes (the State chosen from its whole read: its filter box declares no `aria-autocomplete`; a currency list longer than `ANSWER_MAX_OPTIONS` never asked of the AI); the errors and on-load marks; the success rule with `underModal`; the date layout; the pay basis label. The signed-in landing's "Complete Your Application" offers no entry (owner question).
- **Jobvite** (`src/adapters/jobvite/config.ts`): the résumé parse (quirk and busy sign), steps by `ng-form` alone, the success sign; the residence select's label ("Location of Residence and Language") for RULES' answer; the jr6 pages' plans pinned in `jr6-jobvite.browser.test.ts`.
- **JazzHR** (`src/adapters/jazzhr/config.ts`): both layouts (the classic question rule and the excluded paste box), Submit by id whatever its tag, the résumé's upload rule with its reveal (on the classic layout "Attach resume" pressed once per document by the reveal door; with no input then, the Resume row is the person's), the field errors read as their question, the classic posting text (`#resumator-job-description`), Cancel and "Apply with Indeed" never pressed.
- **SmartRecruiters** (`src/adapters/smartrecruiters/`): the lists declared on their box or button (the menu through the shadow roots, rows, the inner item pressed, the screening's published lists as `knownOptions`, the City's shortest query and manual row, the Country/Region of the manual mode, the phone's slotted rows, its label and shown answer); the drop zone's file rows (`uploadHeld`, `uploadHeldNames`, `uploadState`); `formLateMs` 30 s; the `/job/<id>` application address. A City search whose results add the region and country is left to the person (`no-match`) until RULES reads them as places.
- **UltiPro** (`src/adapters/ultipro/`): the review's repeatables (blocks, Add never offered, Delete as `removeButton`), the period labels (From boxes), School and Degree as typeahead.js declarations (`freeText`, the page's degree list), the Skills tags box, the date component as one date (`dateFor`, drawn only where its container shows a box, `controlBox`: since the fix round, REVC-5, the first wrapper from the component up to its datepicker container whose style draws an edge, a border side, an outline or a box shadow, else `ukg-item`, else the container; on the capture `ukg-item`'s inset shadow, so the start date reads fillable), the decline boxes' labels, the parser's and the documents' exclusions, the phones' `formatFor`, `formLateMs` 30 s.
- **Oracle Recruiting Cloud** (`src/adapters/oracle/`): the entry's state rule (C8: the `apply-now-button--apply-now` modifier or the words "Apply Now"); the gate's phone switch never pressed (O16); the cx-select declaration (grid rows by label part, "No results" left out, searches for the street and the ZIP, learned lists under the Country; a search with no toggle, Address Line 1, names no `expanded`, so its query is typed at once); the phone's "Country code" label and its row's control box (O5, O4); the address order (O3, `dependsOn`); the timeline's open editor as a repeatable block with no Add, read by its School / Employer and Title; a section's Next, finality and step signature (`oracleApplySection`); the upload slot's state.

### The converter (TOOLING; docs/FIXTURES.md has the rules)
`scripts/mhtml-to-fixture.mjs` gained `--replace-text <css>=<file>` (synthetic text for an element where a listed string would tell a value by elimination), the refusals of a listed string in a page's public text and of choice rows that still differ, AngularJS's state classes stripped, ADP's and SmartRecruiters' signed-in places, and in round 2 the list rows' words and identifiers (`LIST_ROWS`, `TYPED_LISTS`), respelled listed strings in attributes and addresses, and the header's `respelled` and `listRows`. jr6 cases stay out of the frozen lists and go through the per-file checks (`test/mhtml-to-fixture.test.ts`). Its fix round (TOOLING F and G): no chosen row survives as a roving tab stop, a reflected `checked` or `selected` on a custom element, an Angular state class on any element or a drawn mark (`CHOSEN_MARKS`, `CHOSEN_DRAWINGS`, more `CHOICE_ROWS`); a radio group's chosen value mirrored on its rows is blanked (`VALUE_MIRRORS`: ADP's `radio-group-value`); referral-source parameters (`REFERRAL_PARAMS`, `REFERRAL_PREFIXES`) read REDACTED in every address, link and text it writes; a listed string respelled in the page's text, or in an attribute that holds words (never an address), is redacted and noted for the elimination refusal, which now reads every list row; a sweep (`eliminatedListsIn`) holds every committed fixture to it. `scripts/fixture-leak-gate.mjs` (FORMS-MERGE, PRIV-5) counts the redaction list's entries in every fixture a line of work changed since a base, prints paths, entry numbers and counts only, and fails on a hit that no public verdict (kept outside the repository) covers (`test/fixture-leak-gate.test.ts`). PRIVACY-FIX (`jr6-privacy`): a posting's JSON copy in an attribute (`POSTING_COPY_ATTRIBUTES`, Breezy's `data-position`) is a posting place, given synthetic text by `--replace-text '<css>@<attribute>=<file>'`; a list row's id normalisation keeps the words of Workday's `data-automation-id` (`ROW_ID_WORDS_KEPT`); a referral value after `&amp;amp;` is redacted too. The converter test's tolerance maps are empty.

### Disclosure version 15 (`shared/stored.ts` `DISCLOSURE_VERSION`, `COPY.options.connection.disclosure`)
docs/PRIVACY.md holds it word for word (`src/shared/copy.test.ts`). New: the job-site email and password set under Sign-up Information, kept in this browser, the password encrypted, never sent to TailorIT, and what the encryption does not keep it from; typed only into a job site's own sign-in and new-account forms, pressed only on Auto or with Sign up & Autofill, on Manual typed with no further click for the person's press in a tab they see and never in the auto-apply window; Import from TailorIT; an email-first step's email typed in either Sign-in mode (iCIMS's phone and country code from the profile), its Next only on Auto or with Sign up & Autofill, never where the step asks for more; the page's path on ADP's and ClearCompany's job pages no longer sent; on a job page with no form, Autofill pressing the site's own Apply and the steps on the way (consents among them), filling in the same tab on that site, refusing a new tab, another site or a download, and never following another site by itself; and military training, a non-compete and relatives among the questions the AI may guess (never a job's essential functions: RULES' fix round took them out of the `all` scope, so the integration dropped them from DOCS' draft). It must reach a release in the same merge as jr6-password's code (`holds()` keeps Sign-in and `typeSignIn` off until it is acknowledged). A connected browser shows it again with I understand. The integration put it in `copy.ts` with `DISCLOSURE_VERSION` 15 in that merge (`jr6-integration`), and `test/no-open-application.test.ts` reads the disclosure too.
