## jr7: auto-apply on every platform (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §20)
The owner's words of 2026-11-11, with snapshot7 (captures of each platform's application from its posting to its page after submitting): "Please find all issues and fix them so that auto-apply can work on all platform correctly." Auto-apply is both Run auto-apply (the background agent, `background/agent.ts`) and the Queue tab (`background/queue-runner.ts`). FIX7PLAN gave its 212 kept findings to fifteen extension packages and to TailorIT's TAILORIT-7 (7a within API 6, 7b as API 7): wave 0 TOOLING-7 (the converter) and ENGINE-7 E0 (the shared contract); wave 1 ENGINE-7 E1 to E5, RULES-7 R1 to R5, AUTO-7 (a1, done, r2, r3), CHAIN-7 and ten platform packages (WORKDAY-7; ICIMS-7 with Jibe; ULTIPRO-7; ADP-7; ORACLE-7; DFPC-7 for Dayforce and Paycom; RBP-7 for Rippling, BambooHR and Pinpoint; BOARDS-7 for SmartRecruiters, Jobvite, JazzHR and Dice; UKGREADY-7; JDEF-7 for JobDiva and Eightfold), each in two parts (P1 the adapter, P2 the shared outcomes once ENGINE E2, RULES R2, AUTO and CHAIN were tagged). INT-7 merged every tag on `jr7-integration` (18 merges), ran the readiness verdict, cut the content script back under its budget, and in round 2 wrote disclosure 16 and pulled TailorIT's API 7. What follows is the code as built on `jr7-integration`; where it and an earlier note differ, this is the newer word. docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §20 has the decisions (FIX7PLAN's D-1 to D-10); docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md §21 the questions Q7-1 to Q7-15, each default in force.

### The five new platforms and their registration (ENGINE-7 E0)
- **Ids and names:** `core/enums.ts` `PLATFORMS` += `ukgready`, `paycom`, `jobdiva`, `eightfold`, `jibe`, appended in that order; `COPY.platforms` "UKG Ready", "Paycom", "JobDiva", "Eightfold", "Jibe"; `COPY.strip.none` names the four ATSs, never Jibe (iCIMS's candidate front). All five were in `PLATFORMS_AHEAD_OF_CONTRACT` until round 2 pulled API 7; it is `['site', 'jibe']` now, Jibe for good (a Jibe job is the iCIMS job its Apply leads to, D-6). A platform ahead of the contract is never classified by TailorIT's AI (`unknown-field`) and its saved answers keep no platform, as `site`'s.
- **Stubs and registration:** each folder's `index.ts` (`ukgReadyAdapter`, `paycomAdapter`, `jobDivaAdapter`, `eightfoldAdapter`, `jibeAdapter`) and `url.ts` (`parse…Url`, `canonicalApplicationUrl`) are registered in `adapters/index.ts` `PLATFORM_ADAPTERS` and `adapters/address.ts` `URL_PARSERS` (E0's marked hunks). A registered parser that reads nothing keeps its candidate pages dormant (no application, no launcher, no run), never the generic rule's origin-and-path application, which would have made every page of a host one application (UKGR-3); each package's parser then keys its own pages. UKG Ready, Paycom, JobDiva and Eightfold are configured adapters: each adds its config to `configured/registry.ts` `ATS_CONFIGS` in a marked hunk, so `content/submitted.ts` reads its success rule. Jibe declares none (its application is iCIMS's).
- **Hosts (`shared/hosts.ts`):** `ATS_MATCHES` += `https://*.saashr.com/ta/*.careers*`, `https://www.paycomonline.net/v4/ats/web.php/portal/*`, `https://*.jobdiva.com/portal/*`, `https://*.eightfold.ai/careers*`, `https://*.jibeapply.com/jobs/*` (host permissions unchanged: `https://*/*`). Their host entries form `UNBRIDGED_HOST_PLATFORMS` (named by `UNBRIDGED_PLATFORMS`), read by `platformOfUrl` after `HOST_PLATFORMS` (`platformAmong`): UKG Ready `secure<n>.saashr.com`, path `/ta/<n>.careers`; Paycom `www.paycomonline.net`, a portal's `/jobs/<n>` or `/applications`, never `/v4/ee/` or `/v4/cl/`; JobDiva `www<n>.jobdiva.com` under `/portal/`; Eightfold `<company>.eightfold.ai` under `/careers`, never `app.` or `www.`; Jibe `<customer>.jibeapply.com` under `/jobs/`.
- **The HR rule:** `saashr.com`, `jobdiva.com` and `eightfold.ai` join `HR_HOST_MATCHES` and `isHrHost`, so the sentinel and the company-page rule stay off their staff pages; Paycom's host was there already (`HR_HOSTS_EXACT`). Eightfold's rule is its whole domain, not the plan's two patterns (`app.eightfold.ai`, `/careerhub/`): `isHrHost` reads a host, never a path, so a `/careerhub/` page (employees' internal mobility) would otherwise read as a company page, and a route from `/careers` could make it a `site` application. Two side effects, both dormant: the static script also runs on `app.eightfold.ai/careers*` and `www.eightfold.ai/careers*`, and on a saashr.com page whose query holds ".careers" (Chrome matches a pattern's path against path and query; `platformOfUrl` reads the path alone). Jibe has no employee system: its pages off `/jobs/` stay company pages. `ORACLE_REFUSED_DOMAINS` (in `hosts.ts` and `oracle/url.ts`) gains `saashr.com`, `jobdiva.com`, `eightfold.ai` and `jibeapply.com` (`paycomonline.net` was there), so no lookup reads one of their hosts as Oracle's own domain.
- **The page-world scripts' own lookup:** `bridgePlatformOfUrl` reads `HOST_PLATFORMS` alone, so neither the bridge's nor the submit watcher's bundle holds the unbridged table. `adapters/bridge-address.ts` `bridgeInstallsAt`, `submitWatchInstallsAt` and `bridgeFramedByEmployerPage` read it (`entrypoints/page-bridge.content.ts`, `submit-watch.content.ts`); `framedByEmployerPage` keeps `platformOfUrl` for the content runtime. None of the five is in `SHARED_DOMAIN_PARSERS`: the bridge never installs on their hosts. A platform that needs the bridge later moves its entry to `HOST_PLATFORMS` and measures the bridge; `src/adapters/jr7-bridge-lookup.test.ts` fails until both are done.
- **check-build (`scripts/check-build.mjs`):** `BRIDGE_BUDGET` 26 → 28 KiB and `SENTINEL_BUDGET` 19 → 20 KiB, raised once for the five platforms, the reason beside each; no budget was raised after. Its own HR lock: `HR_EXCLUDED` += `https://*.saashr.com/*`, `https://*.jobdiva.com/*`, `https://*.eightfold.ai/*` (Paycom's was there), `HR_ADDRESSES` += made-up staff addresses of the four; `test/check-build.test.ts` holds `HR_EXCLUDED` to `hosts.ts` `HR_HOST_MATCHES` pattern for pattern, each pattern to an address it covers.

### The shared hooks (FIX7PLAN §3.1; `adapters/types.ts`, ENGINE-7 E0)
All optional. Each answers from the page's markup and its own address only, never a value the person typed and never a per-person id (D-10). A configured adapter declares them in its config's `code`, which `configured/index.ts` passes through as written.
- `chainChildOrigins(doc)`: on a claimed portal frame, the origins of the forms frames it shows, origins only (iCIMS, ICIMS7-01); read by CHAIN-7.
- `confirmationAfter(armed, here)`: whether `here`, on the same origin, is the page after submitting the application armed at `armed` (Workday WD7-05, UltiPro UP7-01, Oracle's my-profile); read by the runtime and the worker ("Auto-apply", below).
- `successSigns(doc, armedKey)`: the drawn signs of the application armed under `armedKey`, read only after an armed Submit, only the armed one where a page lists several (Oracle's tile, SmartRecruiters' heading, Workday's fourth sign, JazzHR's second).
- `requiredBlocksLeft(root)`: required blocks holding no control and no entry yet (Oracle's Skills, Paycom's Signature); read by the review's rows and the Submit facts (E1).
- `uploadPrompt(doc)`: `UploadPrompt {press, kind}`, `UploadPromptKind` `'dismiss' | 'consent'`: the dialog the site draws after the run's own file write and its one control (UKG Ready's "Upload Complete": Cancel; Eightfold's Data Privacy Agreement: I Agree); read by the upload-prompt door (E2).
- `editorOpener(root)`: controls that open a single-entry editor, each pressed at most once per document (UKG Ready's Address); read by the editor-opener door (E2).
- `notCodeBox(input)`: an `autocomplete="one-time-code"` box that is no code box (Eightfold's question boxes, EFA-3); read by `dom/kinds.ts`, the scan, the guard and `dom/pause-detect.ts` (E1).
- `identityHosts`: whole-host patterns of where the platform's sign-in leaves to (UltiPro's UKG sign-in, Dayforce's `dfid.dayforcehcm.com`, iCIMS's `login.icims.com`); read by the agent and the Queue tab (AUTO7-05).
- `handOver(doc)`: a `HandOverKind` of `HAND_OVER_KINDS` = `'assessment'` (iCIMS's Assessments step, ICIMS7-04), `'documents'` (UltiPro's documents dialog after Submit, UP7-07): a step that is the person's by design, nothing in it pressed.
- `tailoritAddress(doc, here)`: the posting address to give TailorIT instead of `here` (Jibe, D-6).
- `SignInForm.terms` widened to `HTMLInputElement | HTMLElement`: also a declared ARIA checkbox inside the declared container (UltiPro's Register privacy box, UP7-04).

### The engine: reading, doors and the loop (ENGINE-7 E1 to E5)
- **Reading (E1, E3, E4, and E2's two reading items):**
  - a bare `accept` token is that extension (`core/accept.ts`, DF7-V1: Dayforce's PDF is no longer refused at fill time);
  - labels of one control saying the same words count once, among its `label[for]` only (RBP7-21; ClearCompany's ferrilli-page2 now reads "Company Name" once);
  - a declared react-select's DummyInput is not read-only by its `aria-readonly` (Pinpoint's EEO questions, RBP7-20);
  - `kindOf` and the auth-field reading take the adapter (`CodeBoxReader`), so `notCodeBox` reaches the scan, the guard (`ScanResult.adapter`), the sole-field legend and `detectPause` (EFA-3);
  - an ARIA term (`role=checkbox`) inside a declared sign-in container is taken (`dom/sign-in-form.ts ariaTermRefusal`): `fill` clicks it while `aria-checked` is false, and the press reads it back true within `ARIA_TERM_MS` (1.5 s) or presses nothing (UP7-04);
  - an unrecognised required upload left empty counts (`UnrecognisedUpload.requiredEmpty`, `requiredLeftOf`; RBP7-V3: BambooHR's Submit is no longer pressed with no résumé);
  - required blocks: `orchestrator.ts submitFacts` adds `requiredBlocksNow()`, one review row per block (`PAGE_COPY.requiredBlockLeft`, reason needs-your-click, section `required-blocks`) counted among the required fields left, so Submit Auto holds (jr7-O7);
  - at FILLED, `heldIn` folds in only `unknown-field` and `format-unsupported` (ICIMS7-V4), so iCIMS's page default "Hr." beside an amount, with no profile pay, stays counted (O-3);
  - a choice's words painted in an aria-hidden label of its own input (`labels.ts hiddenOwnLabelSays`: Eightfold's, EFA-4), since E3-1 also in an aria-hidden part of its own shown label (Paycom's radios); since the jr7 review fixes (A-1) in both only the words a person sees drawn count (`visibleText`'s rules with the aria-hidden reason alone lifted, `visibility.ts ariaHiddenLifted`: a box on screen, a font size, ink against its background), and words drawn in another option's declared proxy never name this option (`textBesideOption`);
  - a select hidden only by its own opacity, wholly inside its declared shown `controlBox`, is a visible select (`scan.ts selectInDeclaredBox`, `dom/box-shown.ts`; UKGR-6);
  - on a page that never scrolls itself (`pageClipped`: html or body `overflow-y` hidden or clip), a box below the fold of the inner scroller it moves with is on screen: `onScreen` takes a box within that scroller's range (`scrollerAbove`, UKGR-7), and E4-1's `visibility.ts scrolledWithin` lifts the clips above such a scroller as for one fixed to the viewport (only vertically, only a scroller that scrolls, only a clip that shows its whole visible part; honeypots in the same scroller stay hidden), so UKG Ready's boxes below about 900 px are fields;
  - a declared box that draws its edge with a pseudo-element counts as drawn (`camouflage.ts pseudoEdgeDraws`, `PSEUDO_EDGE_MIN_PX` 0.5; UKGR-5: UKG Ready's inset ring), and so does a declared aria-hidden decoration beside the control that holds none (`declaredDecoration`: MUI's outlined `<fieldset>`); a native select alone in a `role=listbox` wrapper holding no option is a field (`widgets/aria.ts isWidgetPart`). The last two are PC7-04 (Paycom's text boxes, textareas and selects).
- **The doors (E2):** each press goes through `dom/fillers/not-submit.ts` (every never-press rule held there), its door and wrapper lint-held (`eslint.config.mjs` `UPLOAD_PROMPT_RULE`, `EDITOR_OPENER_RULE`, `BLOCK_SAVE_RULE` and their `_WRAPPER_RULE`s; `test/allow-submit.test.ts`).
  - The upload-prompt door (`dom/fillers/upload-prompt.ts answerUploadPrompt`, `clickUploadPrompt`; called by `content/pass.ts` alone): right after the run's own file write it looks every 100 ms for `UPLOAD_PROMPT_MS` (4 s), then answers once: a `'dismiss'` pressed; a `'consent'` pressed only where the run's `consentPrompts` says so ("Auto-apply", the Consents mode), else left for the person; never Populate, never a consent's Cancel.
  - The editor-opener door (`dom/fillers/editor-opener.ts openEditors`, `clickEditorOpener`; called by `content/orchestrator.ts` alone, never in a dry run): before a step's fill, each control `editorOpener` names, once per document.
  - The block-save door (`dom/fillers/block-save.ts saveBlockForm`, `clickBlockSave`; DF7-V4) is built off: `BLOCK_SAVE_DOOR_ON` false and no module calls it, until Q7-1's live check shows Dayforce's Next refuses open editors; then `content/repeatables.ts saveBlocks` tries it after `clickIfAllowed`, and the allow-submit row names that caller.
  - The coordinator's rule for the two live doors: never a control the adapter's `neverPress` names, nor one holding or inside one, with no exemption. So UKG Ready's opener is an Add-type control outside `neverPress`, and Eightfold's Cancel stays in it.
- **The loop (E2):**
  - ADP7-2 (O-5): a required, empty field the planner or the DOM guard skipped `disabled-or-readonly` is held `waiting-writable` (`loop.ts lockedIn`): the loop ends neither nothing-pending nor stalled while `awaitWritable` waits on the locking attributes (`disabled`, `readonly`, `aria-disabled`, `aria-readonly`, by a MutationObserver) and every 200 ms, `WRITABLE_WAIT_MS` (3 s) from the pass that first found it; a writable scan plans it again (cause `writable`); past the bound `recordWritableWait` leaves it `disabled-or-readonly`, flagged, with `REASONS['disabled-or-readonly']`'s words. A locked field that holds a value never waits.
  - ADP7-7: `resumeMode` 'auto' and the resume by load (`core/steps/machine.ts`) only for a run at an email-code handoff or auth-needed, not handed off from review, whose new document shows that application's form (`formOf`) within `RESUME_AUTO_MS`, with Autofill after page turn on Auto.
  - jr7-O1 part 3: `submitUnconfirmed` adds nothing while the run rests at an email-code or CAPTCHA handoff (`restsAtSubmitPause`); the Submit stays pressed and watched.
  - jr6's leftovers: `dependsAgain` `'parent-settled'` plans a dependent again once its parent's row settles no-match or its lazy parent is first read (L1, L2); `FillRun.helloAnswered` wakes a paused run's password wait (L3).
  - WD7-06: `content/sign-in.ts accountErrorShown` also reads the document's shown `[role=alert]` that are none of its errors, after them (Workday's verify notice: `verify-email`, nothing asked).
  - UP7-04: at an email gate a declared ARIA term not marked required is ticked per the Consents mode (`SignInContext.consents`, `ticksAccountTerms`: All, or Required with `privacy` and `terms` pre-approved); with no mode said it is left; a native optional term stays the plan's.
- **E5 (ULTIPRO-7's Register):** a step that reads not final and offers no Next rests the run in wait-transition (`orchestrator.ts awaitStep`), where the step's declared sign-in container was never pressed (only at a review rest). `awaitStep` now calls `signInContainerNow` after `end('waiting')`, with its guards: Sign-in Auto (the agent's run included), no dry run, the top or claimed frame, once per step signature, no page error, no CAPTCHA, no required field left; the press goes through `sign-in.ts`'s door. UltiPro's Register presses Create account once with Consents All.
- **A date control's `min`/`max`** (built by the jr7 review fixes, D-4; before, the scanner read none): a `type=date` or `type=month` control's bounds, in their own shapes alone (`YYYY-MM-DD`, `YYYY-MM`), go into `Constraints.min`/`max` as the value without its dashes (`dom/constraints.ts`: 20210315, 202103); a month-precision date's 1st, or a full date, outside them is not written and the field is flagged (`format.ts dateIso`: `format-unsupported`). Dayforce's controls carry bounds (`min` 1753-01-04).

### The resolver's jr7 rules (RULES-7 R1 to R5)
- **New categories** (`core/resolver/categories.ts`, `core/types/intent.ts`): `identity.nationalId` (sensitive: a national id or a social security number, or its type; read before every other source in `classifyByRules`; its hand-over is `identifier`, a new `NEEDS_USER_CAUSES` value in `labelRules.ts`: never filled, never an earlier answer, never asked of an AI, never the phone; UKGR-14), `experience.detail` (an optional "Reason For Leaving" and the like: skipped, never AI; a required one stays the AI's), `skills.certificationDetail` (a licence's number or state: never a value, never AI), `languages.preferred` (the native language, WD7V-02) and `location.inListedRegion` (from the address, flagged; asked of TailorIT's AI only where the list cannot be read, as `location.inCountry`; RBP7-08). None is in the contract. The panel names no category, so they need no copy.
- **R3-1 (safety):** a job's or a school's own block (`entryOf`) never takes the person's `contact.phone`, `contact.phoneCountry` or `contact.email` (`values.ts OWN_CONTACT_CATEGORIES`, beside jr6's `HOME_PLACE_CATEGORIES`): an "Employer Phone Number" is `no-profile-value`, never the person's own, and is not asked of an AI. **R3-2:** "Legal Middle Name", "Legal Suffix", "Apt / Suite / Other" read as the profile's; "I do not have a middle name" is handed to the person, never ticked.
- **R4:** a help sentence that asks for the name typed as a signature is read before the whole help text (`classifyRules.ts labelRuleVote`, `labelRules.ts asksNameTypedAsSignature`), only on a kind a signature takes, text or checkbox (Rippling's attestation, RBP7-07); a "Secondary" or "Alternate" email and a work phone are left (`no-profile-value`), unless the profile's phone has type `work`; since the jr7 review fixes (D-8) a work phone's own country-code control is left with it (`values.ts`: `contact.phoneCountry` reads R4(b)'s predicate on a code labelled or named for a work, business or office phone), while Home, Cell and Mobile keep theirs, and R4 is asserted on its source capture (UKG Ready's `jr7-saashr-contact` in `outcomes.browser.test.ts`: Work Phone and its code left, Home and Cell planned with theirs).
- **R5:** a date layout needing a day given a month-precision date takes the month's 1st, filled and flagged as a guess: a date control (DF7-04, R1) and, since R5, a text box whose pattern holds a day (MM/DD/YYYY, DD.MM.YYYY: UKG Ready's work "Start date", Ashby's date box) or of format `date` with no pattern (`format.ts dateText`); a date with no month still gives nothing. The amendment to docs/design/core.md §8.5, in RULES-7's words: "a date layout needing a day (a date control, or a text box whose pattern or format holds a day) given a month-precision date takes the month's 1st, filled and flagged as a guess; a date with no month still gives nothing". With it, UP7-11: an attainment short of a degree ("less than", "some", "no", "incomplete", a technical, vocational or trade school: `core/matcher/prepare.ts ATTAINMENT`) is left out of `degree.ts`'s level shares, "HS Graduate" reads as the high-school level and "college" or "university" is no field of study, so UltiPro's eleven options choose the bachelor's level.
- **Other rules (R1, R2):** sponsorship never answered as authorization (WD7-01); a current job's end date is today where the field's own words ask for it ("If presently employed put today's date"), empty where they say to leave it blank or where the block shows an "I currently work here" box, and today, flagged, where the field is required and says neither (ICIMS7-05); iCIMS's "Desired Salary" parts read `salary.currency`, `salary.expected` and `salary.period` (ICIMS7-V3: the list's own spelling of the person's period; with no profile pay, `no-profile-value`); an hourly or daily rate is `salary.expected`, a period that differs never converted (ADP7-8); a citizen-or-resident-of-listed-countries question is handed to the person (`nationality`: Workday's export-control question, Eightfold's residency; WD7-07, EFA-10), as is an employer's AI-use consent (WD7-08); a typed name is the signature, the profile's first and last name, never the page's words (`consent.signature`; UKGR-18, UKGR-V2); Eightfold's two "Acknowledge" boxes read their legend, the AI-policy one an AI attestation ticked only in a frame that used no AI (EFA-10); military experience is never filled from the skills (WD7-11); a lone "Hispanic or Latino" box is ticked only on an explicit yes (jr7-O6); a school list's "Other" beside its companion box takes no free text of its own (`plan/neighbours.ts otherBesideSchoolCompanion`, jr6's leftover); Dayforce's "Not Completed" is ticked only for an entry in progress, and a supervisor's or manager's field is another person's (DF7-11).
- **Left (for the review or the owner):** Paycom's "Currently Enrolled" and "Current Employer" are Yes/No radio groups, while `education.current` and `experience.current` take a checkbox only: giving them radio kinds is a design change, so they stay the person's (R3-2); "Can Contact" has no category; UKG Ready's "Primary phone" radio (which row is primary) is no clean category (R4 c). The gated items' other paths are not built (WD7-07's "Not Applicable" chooser, Q7-3; WD7-08's Consents option, Q7-4; ADP7-6's posting currency, jr6's Q8), each default pinned by a test.

### Auto-apply: success, the agent, the Queue tab and the Consents mode (AUTO-7 a1 to r3)
- **The submissions registry (`background/submissions.ts`):**
  - `ArmSchema` gains `chainedFrom` (an arm from a chained subframe, from the registry's entry for the same document), `pageKey` (the key of the arming frame's own address, a hash, where it differs from the arm's: read from Chrome's sender address before `tailoritFrom` swaps in the chain's) and `handOverAt`. `armed` and `detected` take the frame's own address (`index.ts`).
  - ICIMS7-02: a confirmation-page from the tab's claim frame (`frames.ts claimFrameOf`, frame and document, at the same key) takes a `chainedFrom` arm: an IForm leaf's Submit, its confirmation shown in the portal frame.
  - D-1's rule (AUTOV-01 with J7V-SR-5's worker half): a confirmation-page with no arm under its key pairs the same frame's unconfirmed arm whose `pageKey` is the key the frame's own address reads, within `CONFIRMATION_PAGE_MS` (60 s). SmartRecruiters' one-click run from its posting arms under the posting's key and reports `/success` under the publication's; another publication's confirmation is refused. The runtime's half of J7V-SR-5, a chain page for `/success` and the launcher at the route are not built (D-1).
  - `confirmationAfterOf(platform, armed, here)`: the platform's own adapter (`adapters/index.ts platformAdapter`), on the armed page's origin alone. `confirmedArm` pairs a confirmation-page that has no arm under its key, and `after()` answers `submit/after`, by it, with `armed` the arm's canonical posting address (`lookupAddressOf`, as Applied is recorded with).
  - `ROUTE_WAITS_FOR_SIGN = {'oracle'}` (the coordinator's decision): Oracle's my-profile route alone never records an application, since a person also opens My Applications there after a Submit Oracle refused. The route keeps the arm waiting; the adapter's tile keyed to the arm (`successSigns`), read in the arming document within `SUBMIT_ARM_MS`, records it once as a `success-message`. `confirmedArm` and `submit/after` pair no Oracle arm. If my-profile ever loads as a new document, nothing records it there: the job goes to the person, never recorded wrongly. Since the jr7 review fixes (A-5) Workday's candidate home's completed task (`/jobTasks/completed/application`, where a person also opens their applications after a Submit Workday refused) is held the same way (`routeWaitsForSign`, `ROUTE_ADDRESS_WAITS`): that route, or a new document there, records nothing and keeps the arm, and Workday's finalize answered (its network sign) or its "Application Submitted" task dialog (its drawn sign) records it within `SUBMIT_ARM_MS`; its `?Job_Application_ID=` page stays a sign by its address.
  - A hand-over after the Submit (`handedOver` sets `handOverAt`, log `submit.handed-over`) widens the arm's page window from `CONFIRMATION_PAGE_MS` to `SUBMIT_ARM_MS` (10 min), so an assessment taken in the tab can still record it (ICIMS7-04). A Submit armed again in the same frame and document, unconfirmed and within that window, keeps the earlier arm's `handOverAt` (the jr7 review's B-1, worker half).
- **The runtime (`content/runtime.ts`, `content/submitted.ts`):**
  - a route of the armed document asks `confirmedAfter` (the adapter's `confirmationAfter`, `armed` the document's address at the press), also where `applicationOf(here)` is null; a keyless top document on a host whose adapter declares the hook sends `submit/after` once at start-up (no key, no address; rate-limited by `SUBMIT_MESSAGE_LIMIT`; answered `expired` where no arm pairs), then a hello once taken (WD7-05, UP7-01). So both readers must take either armed address (match the tenant and site, not the exact form path).
  - `signsOf(doc, platform, {adapter, applicationKey})` adds the adapter's `successSigns` for the arm's key to the platform's rules (the "before" set read at the press too). The arm's watch also looks at each `animationend` and `transitionend` (capture) and every `SIGN_RECHECK_MS` (250 ms) until its quiet watch (WD7-04: Workday's dialogs fade in), and ends only once the worker takes the sign, so a refused route keeps watching.
  - the hello of a top or claimed frame says `handOver` and `tailoritAddress`; while a Submit is armed, its watch calls `sayHandOverOnce()`: the first time `handOver` answers a kind this document's hellos have not named, one hello says it (`handOversSaid`; log `content.hand-over`; r3, UP7-07: UltiPro's documents dialog is drawn after Submit in the same document). Once per armed Submit and kind (since the jr7 review fixes, B-1: `arm()` clears `handOversSaid` after sending `submit/armed`, so a second Submit in the same document says its hand-over again), never before an arm, and only once the worker answered it (`armedHeard`).
  - `agent/enter`'s answer gains `declared` (AUTO7-03: with `not-offered`, whether an adapter of the page declares an entry at all).
- **The agent (`background/agent.ts`):**
  - `AGENT_MAX_STARTS` 12 (was 8; AUTO7-15); `notOffered` with no declared entry ends needs-you `unsupported_site` (AUTO7-03); the form wait counts from the latest of the opening, the entry's press, the hello (`helloAt`, AUTO7-10) and the waiting; `agent/enter` is sent within `serial` and awaited outside it, its answer taken in a serial step of its own (AUTOV-03).
  - an identity host (`identityHostOf`: the job's platform adapter's `identityHosts` against the tab's host) ends needs-you `sign_in` (AUTO7-05).
  - holds (`holdForSign`, `heldKind` `'page'` or `'form'`): after the job's Submit, a keyless page of the job's platform, or the top frame's confirmation of another key on it, is held `AGENT_CONFIRM_MS` (90 s) for the sign, then ends needs-you `timed_out` (AUTO7-08 a and b, J7-AG-1). A page of the posting with no form after the Submit also ends `timed_out` (AUTO-7's decision 1: "it may have been sent"); a form shown again keeps `other` or `validation_error`.
  - kept tabs (jr7-O1 part 4; D-9's Q-O2): an email code or a CAPTCHA after the job's Submit ends the job needs-you with its tab kept (`finishRun(…, {keepTab})`: the run cancelled and its settings released, never closed), its window flagged (`windows.update(id, {drawAttention: true})`, no permission), its id kept in `storage.session` `AGENT_KEPT_KEY` (at most `AGENT_KEPT_MAX`, 20) and left out of `othersOpen`.
  - a hand-over after the Submit (`'assessment'`, `'documents'`) ends needs-you `other` with its note (`AGENT_NOTES`; words `AGENT_NOTE_WORDS`) and the tab kept, nothing in it pressed.
- **The Queue tab (`background/queue-runner.ts`; APPLYPLAN's F4b):**
  - a task opens at `agentAddressOf(url) ?? queueAddressOf(url)` (AUTO7-01): the agent's address where the agent takes the platform, else TailorIT's queue address (UltiPro's OpportunityDetail, never OpportunityApply; Dayforce's and Workday's posting; D-4, D-9); the address says hello before any redirect.
  - D-4: only on `QUEUE_SUBMIT_PLATFORMS` (`src/shared/queue-submit.ts`, re-exported by `background/settings.ts`) does a queued tab submit as Submit mode says. Elsewhere `queueSubmitIsPersons` holds Submit on Manual (`contentSettingsFor` sends `ContentHolds.queueSubmitHeld`; the row says `COPY.queue.submitYours`), and a Submit armed in the tab (`pressed`, `pressedAt`, log `queue.pressed`) makes the job Ready for you; a sign that still fires records it. A queued tab closed after an armed Submit is told `timed_out`, never `cancelled` (AUTO-7's decision 3).
  - an `opened` job (Autofill when a queued tab opens on Automatically) whose application frame said hello with no form and showed no wall, choice or entry goes Ready for you `QUEUE_ENTRY_WAIT_MS` later, on SHELL-B's quiet rule, never "Opened" for ever (AUTO7-04; `queue.entry-timeout {opened}`); an identity host holds the item Ready, later hellos taken (AUTO7-05 c, `queue.held {identity}`); a hand-over holds it Ready with its note (`queue.held {handOver}`), which goes when the job ends, submitted, skipped or failed (`finish()`: the jr7 review's B-3).
- **The Consents mode in the content (r2; ENGINE-7 E2's edit 1):** `ContentModesSchema.consents` and `ContentSettingsSchema.preapprovedConsent`, both optional (a message without them says no mode), are sent by `background/settings.ts contentSettingsOf` everywhere, the agent's window too (D-9: the person's mode). The content reads them with `content/sign-in.ts accountConsentsOf` into every `signIn({...})` (`runtime.ts consentsNow`, `consentsArg`: the run's sign-in, the account section's, the container's, the wall step's and the agent's) and into the run (`FillRun.consents`; `runLoop`'s `consentPrompts`). Account terms tick by `ticksAccountTerms`. An upload prompt's consent (Eightfold's Data Privacy Agreement) is pressed by `ticksUploadConsent`: Consents All, or Required with `privacy` pre-approved (the coordinator's decision, D-9's "a privacy acknowledgement under the Consents mode"); with no mode said it is left. r2 also calls `helloAnswered` from `hello()` (jr6 L3) and passes `formOf` to `resumeMode` in `resumeStored` (ADP7-7's log).
- **The Jibe address (ICIMS7-11, D-6):** the hello carries the adapter's `tailoritAddress` (https, at most 2000 characters); the worker takes it only on a Jibe posting page (`frames.ts checkedTailoritAddress`: an https iCIMS posting or form of the same numeric `/jobs/<id>`, no credentials, query or fragment; else `tailorit.address-refused`), keeps it on the top frame's entry alone (`tailoritUrl`), and `index.ts tailoritFrom` gives TailorIT that address (`postingAddress`, the lookup, Add job, Applied). So a Jibe job is an ordinary iCIMS job, which both runs open on iCIMS.
- **The worker's bundle:** `platformAdapter` references `PLATFORM_ADAPTERS`, so `background.js` holds every adapter's code (about 111 KB of AUTO-7's +120 KB) for two address-only readings, `identityHosts` and `confirmationAfter`. The worker has no budget; the coordinator kept it. The review may move the two readings to each platform's `url.ts`.

### Chains (CHAIN-7)
- **Declared forms origins (ICIMS7-01):** a portal whose forms host is not named after it (jr6's `chainChildOrigin` names none there) says in its hello `childOrigins` (`content/chain-frame.ts declaredChildOrigins`, from the adapter's `chainChildOrigins`, on a top or claimed frame; at most four `https://<host>` origins). `index.ts` → `notePortal` keeps those `adapters/chain.ts declaredChildOrigin` takes (an https origin whose `/forms` page is a chain subframe page of the portal's platform) with the portal's document (`storage.session` `chainportal:<tab>:<frame>`); `inheritFrame` keys a chain subframe on the one origin the tab's one fresh claimed portal declared, every other guard kept, the host-name rule the fallback. The relay (`factsPortalOf`, `leavesOf`) follows the leaf's `chainedFrom`. On the leaf, `loadedByPortal` also takes a referrer of its parent frame's origin (`parentOriginOf`: `location.ancestorOrigins[0]`), never `document.referrer` alone.
- **Fresh facts (ICIMS7-V1):** a leaf document says `chain/pressed` once (a click on a submit control of its form, its form's submit, else `pagehide`: `chain-frame.ts watchLeafPress`). The worker numbers each saying of a portal document's facts (`seq`) and marks those said before a press (`pressedSeq`) and the pressing documents (`pressedBy`): a leaf document chained after a press hears only facts said after it, and the presser none newer than its own. None means unknown: nothing is pressed. A (2/2) leaf is never handed (1/2) facts (the STALE/FRESH probe), so its final Submit is never pressed as a Next.
- **The facts-bound leaf adapter (ICIMS7-V2):** `runtime.ts resolvePage` gives a chain subframe `chain-frame.ts leafAdapter(adapter, frame)`: `isFinalStep`, `nextButton`, `submitButton` and `stepSubmitNext.laterStep` asked without facts take `chainFactsOf(doc)`. So `content/transition.ts` (the person's own Next) and `not-submit.ts nearApplicationButtons` read the leaf rightly (SHELL-B's open item), and the person's click on the final Submit arms `page` with the facts.
- **UKG Ready's session (UKGR-3):** the posting (one `ShowJob`) and the applicant session (an `ActiveSessionId`, no `ShowJob`) share the family `ukgready:<host>:<company>`; the session is a top chain page (`chainPageOf`: `{platform: 'ukgready', role: 'top'}`), keyed per job by its referrer, the posting. The referrer is Q7-1's live check; a tenant-level key fallback is not built.
- **Paycom's pair (PC7-03):** family `paycom:<client key>`; `…/applications#/applications/<m>` is a pair page (`pair: true`). `notePair` (`storage.session` `chainpair:<tab>`: the family, the application's key and canonical posting address, the posting's document, when, and whether taken) records `entry/pressed`, `agent/entered`, `signin/credential` and `frame/wall` of the top frame's Paycom posting document; `inheritPair` keys a top pair page of another document of the same family by it within `PAIR_MS` (60 s) of the latest note, once. `chainOfHello` tries it before the hello's own `chain`, and `inheritTop` refuses pair pages: never by referrer, never by the application's number (D-10). Keyed by the posting, so the agent's other-key check takes it.
- Eightfold needs no chain (EFA-2: its posting, form and success page share one key); SmartRecruiters' middle steps chained already; Dice waits (§6.18). Only origins, ids, counts and hashes are kept (docs/PRIVACY.md).

### The platforms (each adapter's header holds its jr7 paragraph)
- **Workday** (`src/adapters/workday/`, WORKDAY-7): the page after submitting, captured for the first time: Workday routes the application's own document, with no new load, to the site's job search with one `Job_Application_ID` (guest tenants, under a "Congratulations!" dialog) or to the candidate home's `/jobTasks/completed/application` (signed in). `confirmationAfter` (in `index.ts`, never `url.ts`, which the bridge bundles) reads both on the same origin and site (since the jr7 review fixes, A-5, the worker holds the completed task for Workday's finalize or its dialog: `routeWaitsForSign`), whatever the locale and the case (`siteOf`), from either armed address (the form's at the press; the worker's canonical posting, which has no locale). The fourth drawn rule, `WORKDAY_CONGRATULATIONS`, is `successSigns`, returned only where the document's own address is such a page (`workdaySignsAt`); no id of it is read into a key, an address or a log. `accountErrorKind` reads the account view's verify notice as `verify-email`, never an error (WD7-06, with E2's `[role=alert]` reading). An account tenant's job ends needs-you at its Sign In or Create Account on Sign-in Manual; on Sign-in Auto the agent signs in or makes the account with the job-site sign-in, and the job ends needs-you if Workday then asks for the email's verification (Workday's design; no guest-tenant filter: the jr7 review's C-4).
- **iCIMS** (`src/adapters/icims/`, ICIMS-7): `chainChildOrigins` (the forms frame's origin); the Assessments step after the last form (`/assessment`) is `handOver` `'assessment'`, its Continue never pressed (Q7-6); "Desired Salary"'s currency, amount and time frame each read as their part (`labelSuffix`, ICIMS7-V3); `identityHosts` `login.icims.com` (the portal's "Log back in"); an "Other <list label>" box under its list (`OTHER_OF`: an "Other School" under "School") depends on that list as NYU's "Enter school name if not in the list above" does (`listAboveOf`, `LIST_LACKS`), so it is decided again once the list is written or settles no match.
- **Jibe** (`src/adapters/jibe/`, ICIMS-7; `<customer>.jibeapply.com/jobs/<id>`, iCIMS's candidate front): a posting-only adapter. No form root, ever; its posting text; its entry the Apply a person sees, `a#link-apply`, only where its href is the same job's iCIMS email gate (`url.ts icimsPostingOf`). That leads to another origin, so the entry door refuses it `'elsewhere'` and presses nothing (APPLYPLAN OQ4, Q7-13); the panel's line then names iCIMS (`COPY.mainView.elsewhereIcims` in `ui/panel/Panel.tsx`, where `page.platform` is `jibe`). `tailoritAddress` gives TailorIT the job's iCIMS posting, built from that link's parts (D-6); every other control is in `neverPress`. One Jibe capture exists (one tenant).
- **UltiPro** (`src/adapters/ultipro/`, ULTIPRO-7): two readings of an address, `parseUltiproUrl` (the posting, the application and the account step, for the page-world scripts) and `parseUltiproAddress` (also the board's page after submitting, for the content runtime and the worker, `URL_PARSERS`); `confirmationAfter` (`url.ts`: the same host, tenant and board as the armed form; the page's `applicationId` read for its presence only, never its value, nor kept in a key or an address; D-10); the sign `div#ApplicationSubmitted` shown, whatever words it draws (UP7-01, UP7-V2); `identityHosts` UKG's sign-in hosts, where nothing of ours runs and no password is typed (UP7-02, Q7-9); the posting's Apply now as its entry (UP7-09, APPLYPLAN's E-UltiPro C2); the Register step's three boxes drawn by their ukg-field's shadow container (`controlBox`) and its privacy box an ARIA checkbox, the sign-in container's term, Create account enabled once filled (`enablesWhenFilled`; UP7-04, UP7-V1; ticked per the Consents mode, pressed with E5); the documents dialog after Submit is the state `documents-dialog`, nothing filled or pressed, and `handOver` `'documents'` (UP7-07, Q7-8); an EEO list whose own decline box is ticked holds no question while it is (UP7-03); the data-use consent radios labelled by their question (UP7-12). Turnstile is drawn in a closed shadow root (a live check).
- **ADP Workforce Now** (`src/adapters/adp/`, ADP-7): Address Line 1, read-only while ADP's per-country address lookup starts, depends on the Country (`dependsOn`: ADP7-2's adapter half; E2's writable wait is the engine half); the signed-in landing's "Complete Your Application" is an entry, one press, resuming the person's own unfinished application (ADP7-10; Q7-14, built on its default yes); a question's number and sum boxes are laid out as one number (`formatFor`, ADP7-13); the Voluntary Self-ID decline box follows the later list a person can choose in (Race, else Ethnicity: `dependsOn`), decided again at that list's first read or write (E2's L2). Never in Run auto-apply: every guest application needs a one-time code the person receives (ADP7-1); the Queue tab opens its job details.
- **Oracle Recruiting Cloud** (`src/adapters/oracle/`, ORACLE-7): the code at Submit (jr7-O1): a tenant that verifies the email after Submit draws a "You're almost done" dialog beside the hidden form; `authState` reads it `'email-code'`, the step signature changes as it shows, Verify is never pressed (the run rests, E2's jr7-O1 part 3; the agent keeps the tab). Success (jr7-O2): `<site>/my-profile` is the page after submitting (`url.ts oracleConfirmationAfter`), and the "My Applications" tile naming the armed application, active with its status shown, is the drawn sign (`successSigns`), never a draft's, a resolved one's or another application's; the route alone records nothing (`ROUTE_WAITS_FOR_SIGN`); the tile's menu (its status button, "Withdraw Application") is never pressed. The tile layout (jr7-O4): the Education and Experience blocks' inline editor is a repeatable block of its kind, its tiles' Edit and Delete never pressed; the Skills editor is the person's (Q7-11). `requiredBlocksLeft` (jr7-O7): the required Skills block with nothing in it holds Submit Auto.
- **Dayforce** (`src/adapters/dayforce/config.ts`, DFPC-7): the posting's Apply is its entry (DF7-01) and its `…/apply` page an entry choice whose entry is "Apply without an Account" (state `apply-choice`, `entryPresses` 2; Sign In and "Create one now." never; DF7-02); Next and Submit as the snapshots name them (AUTO7-13); its success rule, from the cited test ids, is synthetic (no capture after Submit, DF7-03); Education and Employment History are filled in place with no Add (Q7-11), saved cards read by `prefill.readEntries`, each editor's Update declared its Save but refused by the click door (the block-save door is off), so an editor left open is the person's; `quirks.resumeParse` (DF7-V2); `identityHosts` `dfid.dayforcehcm.com`. Its Submit stays the person's in the Queue tab.
- **Paycom** (`src/adapters/paycom/`, DFPC-7; configured): root `#ats-portal` (PC7-01); states `start-choice` (the "Start Application" dialog: entry "Apply Manually" by its words; "Autofill with Resume/CV" never) and `contact` (the "Getting You Started" dialog: filled as a step; its "Continue To Application" the sign-in container's button, pressed by Sign-in Auto alone (or Sign Up & Autofill), asked as an email gate (`CONTAINER_WALL`: no password), since the jr7 review fixes (D-3), when TailorIT's `signInHostCovered` took `www.paycomonline.net`'s two candidate pages (a posting and `…/applications`, matched whole); before, the worker refused it on every Sign-in mode; Sign In and Create Account never; its invisible hCaptcha the person's) (PC7-05); the pre-screen's Next and the one-page application's Submit by their words, never "Save and Complete Later" (PC7-04); question titles drawn beside their fields, the number and dot dropped and the star read as required; drawn radios and boxes; MUI's outlined box as `controlBox` (with E2's decoration rule); a date in three boxes as one date (`dateFor`); Education and Employment blocks with no Add; "Application Submitted!" as its success rule (PC7-06); the signature pad the person's (`requiredBlocksLeft`; Draw, Type and Clear never pressed; PC7-07, Q7-10); its Voluntary Information's closing "Full Name" a signature (`consent.signature`, by the core's `classifyRules.ts selfIdSignature`, the form's own words: the jr7 review's D-5), typed only under the signature pre-approval. Its application page (`…/applications#/applications/<m>`) reads page `application` with no job id (the number never read, D-10), keyed by CHAIN-7's pair. `matchesUrl` is the configured default (the candidate paths), so its staff pages stay off.
- **Rippling, BambooHR and Pinpoint** (RBP-7): Rippling's Apply leads straight to `step=confirmation` (the one-page form is final when Apply shows, RBP7-02), whose action card's `h5` is the drawn sign beside the address (RBP7-04); its select controller's comboboxes are declared with their lists and the Location as a remote search over places (`rippling/widgets.ts`; RBP7-05, -06: live checks, Q7-1); the attestation's help text is its description (RBP7-07's adapter half, read by R4). BambooHR: the posting view's "Apply for This Job" is its entry (RBP7-11), `formInPlace` (RBP7-12), the résumé's hidden required input with its "Choose File*" toggle never pressed (RBP7-13), the Fabric dropdown's menu as the declared list (RBP7-14), the "Thank You" `h2` (RBP7-10). Both adapters' `code.widgetFor` wraps the rules' hook. Pinpoint: a react-select that takes no search (its EEO questions' DummyInput) is declared too, so it is a field (RBP7-20, with E1's reading).
- **The boards** (BOARDS-7): SmartRecruiters' entry compares the posting's company and the link's in lower case (J7-SR-1; no key changes); a screening list's id names its question's UUID and, for a question of several fields, the field or a repeated row's field (`QUESTION_LIST_ID`), its list that field's own (J7-SR-3); "Application submitted!" after Submit is `successSigns` for a `smartrecruiters:` arm (J7-SR-4, Jobright's cited selector). Jobvite's two file inputs read their own question first, whatever their popover shows (`code.labelFirst`, J7V-JV-1). JazzHR's classic checkbox question is one question of its boxes (J7-JZ-3), and its page after submitting draws a second sign (`JAZZHR_RECEIVED`, `code.successSigns`, J7-JZ-2); its Human Check stays the person's. Dice: the dialog the audit read as a cookie consent is the résumé card's own File options menu (J7-DC-2 refuted; a test pins it), so nothing is built there.
- **UKG Ready** (`src/adapters/ukgready/`, UKGREADY-7; configured; Jobright has no UKG Ready support, so every rule is read from the captures): the posting, its "Apply for Job" choice and the "Type it in myself" start form are drawn in place at the posting's address (`formAtPosting`, `formInPlace`; the entry presses Apply, then "Type it in myself": `entryPresses` 2); the start form's Continue opens the applicant session in a new document (CHAIN-7's session key). The session is one React app: one root holds the step body and the portaled dialogs; the header and its menus, the Security Question, the review's read-only summary, the SSN box (UKGR-14) and the reference editor (UKGR-13, section `professional-references`) are excluded. Next is the header's Continue (by its tracking id and its words), "Almost There!"'s "Review & Submit Application" a Next through `stepSubmitNext`, a Contact editor's Save its Next; Submit is "Sign & submit application", then, while the consent shows, its I Agree (never a Next), under one key and recorded once (UKGR-8). Work and education are repeatables (the saved entries and the open editor; `prefill.readEntries`); the editor-opener door opens the Address editor while its summary says "Missing Information", and Personal Information's Edit is never pressed. "Upload Complete" is answered Cancel by the upload-prompt door (never Populate: D-9, Decision 16); "Saving" is busy. Labels "<phone label> country code", "Primary phone", "Full Name to confirm" (its prompt the help text); "We got it!" (`.c-success-screen`) is its success rule. City and State are the person's (UKGR-17, Q7-1); its tracking ids are one tenant's, so another tenant's buttons are not found and nothing is pressed (a second tenant is a live check).
- **JobDiva** (`src/adapters/jobdiva/`, JDEF-7; configured): one address from the posting to the confirmation, every step a Bootstrap dialog drawn in place (`formInPlace`, `formLateMs` 20 s); its entry presses "Apply Now", then "Quick Apply (No Account)" in the choice dialog (`entryPresses` 2, JDV-7), never the account choices; Quick Apply's form and its "Submit Application", then the two "EEO Form" steps, anchored by their radios' names (JDV-8, JDV-V2), then "Thank you!" in the same dialog, recorded once and only after an armed Submit (JDV-10). The tel box's edge is its `.jd-form-phone` (`controlBox`); converted in the live style order, the other boxes draw their own (JDV-5 refuted). Its SMS consent follows the Consents mode (JDV-12).
- **Eightfold** (`src/adapters/eightfold/`, JDEF-7; configured): a company's career site at `<company>.eightfold.ai`; the posting's "Apply Now" link to `/careers/apply?pid=<id>` (the agent may also open the form directly); the page's own "Upload resume" for its AI matching is no application (EFA-12); the one-page application in three section cards, each question titled by its label or legend and required by its class; its question boxes marked `autocomplete="one-time-code"` are no code boxes (`notCodeBox`, EFA-3); the Data Privacy Agreement drawn as a résumé is taken is `uploadPrompt` kind `'consent'`, its I Agree pressed under the Consents mode (EFA-6), its Cancel in `neverPress`; "Save my answers for future applications" is excluded, left as the page chose (Q7-12); the success card at `/careers/apply/success` is its success rule; posting, form and success share one key (EFA-2). The Country code and Country lists are the person's (EFA-9, Q7-1); white-label domains wait (EFA-14).

### The readiness verdict and the three lists (INT-7; FIX7PLAN §8, D-3, D-4)
- **D-3, who joins Run auto-apply:** all must hold: an address that opens it and says hello before any redirect; its entry; each Next; its Submit; a success sign its adapter reads, seen in a capture of the page after Submit, asserted on that fixture and absent on every other capture of the platform; no CAPTCHA, bot check or code on its path in any capture (an empty or invisible challenge widget counts until Q7-2's live check). Platforms already in stay in: Greenhouse's and Ashby's invisible reCAPTCHA Enterprise is Q7-2, default stay.
- **The verdict**, run on the merged tree: the worker, url and chain tests (20 files, 362 passed); every platform's dom folder with the shared content tests (90 files, 2,148 passed); and INT-7's `src/adapters/jr7-readiness.browser.test.ts` (3: Jobvite's sign on its confirmation alone of its six jr7 pages; Workday's platform rules on none of the 41 pages before its three confirmations; iCIMS's success message on both jr7 confirmations' claim frames and on none of the other 45 jr7 pages and frames).
- **`AUTOFILL_AGENT_ATS`** (TailorIT's, `src/contract/tailorit/agent.ts`, API 7) = greenhouse, lever, ashby, workable, icims, pinpoint, and since API 7 jobvite, workday (any posting TailorIT can add: no guest-tenant filter, as `formAddress` takes every posting `addablePosting` accepts; on a tenant that asks for an account, Sign-in Auto signs in or makes it with the job-site sign-in, and Manual leaves the job needing the person at its Sign In or Create Account) and smartrecruiters (the agent opens the publication; the one-click address stays refused). Never: ADP (a code per application), Oracle (a code at Submit or at the gate), JazzHR and BambooHR (a checkbox reCAPTCHA on every form), UltiPro (UKG's sign-in), Dayforce (an image check, and no capture after Submit). Out until Q7-1 and Q7-2 clear them: Rippling, UKG Ready, Paycom, JobDiva, Eightfold (Paycom's account Continue is pressed on Sign-in Auto alone, now that TailorIT's sign-in covers its candidate portal: the jr7 review's D-3; on Manual the agent's job would end needing the person there).
- **`AUTOFILL_QUEUE_ATS`** (the Queue tab's, API 7): every TailorIT kind but Dice, 28 with the four new ones. Their keys after entry were checked: Eightfold's one key (EFA-2), Paycom's pair and UKG Ready's session (CHAIN-7's tests with the real parsers; UKG Ready's referrer stays Q7-1's). Jibe is no kind. Dice waits (§6.18).
- **`QUEUE_SUBMIT_PLATFORMS`** (the extension's, `src/shared/queue-submit.ts`) = the agent's nine with jazzhr, bamboohr, adp, oracle, ultipro, rippling, ukgready, paycom, jobdiva and eightfold (19). Out: Dayforce (its sign synthetic only), Taleo, SuccessFactors, Breezy HR, CareerPlug, Paycor, HiBob, Paylocity, ClearCompany, Dice and a company's own site. Lever's sign (its address after submitting) and Workable's (Jobright's panel selector) rest on their adapters' tests, not on a capture: kept, in this list and in `AUTOFILL_AGENT_ATS`, by the coordinator's decision after the jr7 review (D-7): both were agent sites before jr7 (since v3), with adapter-tested signs; a capture of each one's page after Submit is among Q7-1's wishes (docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md item 439 and "Live checks jr7 still owes").
- **Worker recording tests** (the integration's gap, closed by the jr7 review fixes, D-1, D-2, D-6): Workday through the real worker (`src/background/jr7-fix-workday.test.ts`: the same document's route once, a new document through `submit/after` with Workday's own `confirmationAfter` once, another tenant, site, frame or a late page refused); Paycom (its paired `/applications` arm, then once under the posting's key), ADP, Ashby, BambooHR, Workable, Rippling and Eightfold (`jr7-fix-recording.test.ts`); SmartRecruiters' chained run through the agent (`jr7-fix-sr-agent.test.ts`, the agent twin of `jr7-boards-o8.test.ts`).

### Autofill API version 7 (the 7b pull, INT-7 round 2)
`npm run contract:pull` from TAILORIT-7's `jr7-tailorit-b` (17 files, 435 examples): `AUTOFILL_API_VERSION` 7; `ATS_KINDS` += `ukgready`, `paycom`, `jobdiva`, `eightfold` after `smartrecruiters` (new values in the lookup's `ats.kind`, the page's `employer.kind` and a saved answer's `platform` and `scope`; TailorIT's migration 0819 lets saved answers keep them); `parseAtsUrl` reads their postings, on purpose no job from UKG Ready's session, Paycom's `/applications`, JobDiva's other routes, Eightfold without one pid, or Jibe; `AUTOFILL_AGENT_ATS` and `AUTOFILL_QUEUE_ATS` as above. It adds no scope: an extension speaking 6 gets 409 `conflict` on every token request until updated, so the extension and TailorIT land together. `PLATFORMS_AHEAD_OF_CONTRACT` is `['site', 'jibe']` again. The e2e mock speaks 7. The jr7 review fixes pulled `jr7-tailorit-fix` (`8e3bfad`, still API 7, the same 17 files and 435 examples; docs/CONTRACT.md "What the review fixes changed"): `signInHostCovered` covers Paycom's two candidate pages, so the worker answers its contact dialog's Continue To Application as an email gate under Sign-in Auto (D-3), and a `?gh_jid=` company page's canonical keeps `gh_jid` alone (A-2).

### The content script's budget (INT-7 round 1)
After every merge the production content script was 1,053,899 bytes, 5,323 over its 1 MiB budget (+79,250 since jr6-done: UKG Ready's adapter about 13 KB, Paycom's 10 KB, the resolver's rules 8.7 KB, `src/content` 8.4 KB, …). No budget was raised; test-only data was cut instead (the coordinator's choice):
- **Provenance behind a define:** every configured rule's `from` and each `AtsConfig`'s `snapshots` (362 `from`s and 19 `snapshots` in 22 files) is written `...(__PROVENANCE__ && { … })`. `__PROVENANCE__` is false in every build (`wxt.config.ts`'s `define`: chrome, edge and e2e) and true under Vitest (`vitest.config.ts`), declared in `src/globals.d.ts`; the minifier folds `false && …` away. `from` and `snapshots` are optional (`configured/types.ts`, `success.ts`); `configProblems` (tests only) reads a missing one as a problem, as before; a widget's note reads `''`. Under Vitest the provenance is unchanged (the same 362 `from`s and 170 snapshot entries). A new configured rule follows the same form: a bare `from:` still typechecks, and the guard below catches it in the bundle. Since the jr7 review fixes (A-4) the widget and date-part declarations' `snapshot` (`WidgetDecl.snapshot`, `DatePartsDecl.snapshot`, now optional) are gated the same way: the three jr7 capture names that still shipped (Paycom's date parts, BambooHR's menu, Rippling's widgets) and 41 older fixture paths (Ashby, Greenhouse, iCIMS, Lever, Oracle, SmartRecruiters, Workable, Workday, ADP and UltiPro) are in no build any more (the content script and the worker about 2.4 KB less each).
- **Pure fixture-path lists:** the `*_SNAPSHOTS.map(…)` fixture-path lists (eight, with ADP's two and Workday's jr6 list) are marked `/* @__PURE__ */`, so the builds drop them.
- **The guard:** `scripts/check-build.mjs` fails any bundle of any build that holds a configured rule's provenance. At INT-7 it read six known values (`PROVENANCE_NEEDLES`) and a `from` whose value looked like a capture's name (`PROVENANCE_FROM`), which 79 of the gated values would have passed if written bare. Since the jr7 review fixes (A-4) it reads the exact list `scripts/provenance-values.json` (311 values: every `__PROVENANCE__` gate in `src` and every `from`, `snapshot` and `snapshots` key under `src/adapters`, written by `node scripts/provenance.mjs --write`; `test/provenance.test.ts` fails while the list is stale, a key stands outside its gate, or a value the configs hold at run time is missing): a listed value with a digit, a slash, a dot or a space fails wherever it stands in a bundle; any listed value fails as a `from`, `snapshot` or `snapshots` key's value, part or list element; a key whose value starts like a capture or a fixture path fails for an unlisted value too; a missing list fails every check. `test/check-build.test.ts` holds it (a bare plain-word `from`, a capture-named `snapshot`, a split `from`, an escaped note and an unlisted fixture path fail; the same words outside a key and a date range's `from` pass).
- The cut saved 16,426 bytes: the content script 1,037,473 (11,103 left; the e2e build's 1,040,319, 8,257 left); the worker's 16 KB less.

### The converter (TOOLING-7; docs/FIXTURES.md has the rules)
`scripts/mhtml-to-fixture.mjs` gained the five platforms (candidate paths only), `--style-order archive|live` (Blink saves the page's own styles as sheets above the head's links; live puts them back; JobDiva's default), `--keep-application <job id>` (Oracle's My Applications: only the captured posting's tile kept), `--frame-src <css>=<https address>` (a frame saved unloaded given its public address), a canvas stand-in, `OPENED_ROWS` (UltiPro's Race row opened before the measure), `CHOSEN_ICONS` and `SPELLED_IDS` (Paycom), places for UKG Ready's values, Dayforce's read view, JobDiva's file card, Eightfold's `_csrf` and Oracle's tile summaries, a REDACTED per text run of a searched iCIMS result, more per-load and referral parameters (`ActiveSessionId`, `sft`, `rnd`, `ticket`, `__jvsc`, `src`), SmartRecruiters' chosen list row (r2) and a final check over inlined stylesheets. Its O-7 re-check found the live style order still reads UKG Ready's and Paycom's boxes camouflaged, which is why UKGR-5 and Paycom's `controlBox` were built. INT-7 converted CareerPlug's two forms again (`src=` REDACTED): `SRC_REFERRAL_FIXTURES` and `MARKED_FIXTURES` are empty. The privacy gate on the 628 fixtures jr7 adds or changes: 0 hits.

### Messages, logs and copy
- **Messages:** `frame/hello` gains `handOver` (`HAND_OVER_KINDS`), `tailoritAddress` (an https address, at most 2000 characters) and `childOrigins` (at most four origins); new content messages `submit/after` and `chain/pressed` (both answered `DoneAnswerSchema`; `chain/pressed` answered `expired` from a private tab or from any frame but a keyed chain leaf in its registered document); `agent/enter`'s answer gains `declared`; `ContentSettings` gains `modes.consents` and `preapprovedConsent` (optional) and `ContentHolds` `queueSubmitHeld`. Kept by the worker in `storage.session`: an arm's `chainedFrom`, `pageKey` and `handOverAt`; `chainportal:` records' declared origins; `chainfacts:` records' `seq`, `pressedSeq` and `pressedBy`; `chainpair:<tab>`; `AGENT_KEPT_KEY`; the agent run's `notOffered`, `helloAt` and `heldKind`; a queue item's `pressedAt` and `note`.
- **Logs** (`shared/log.ts`; codes, numbers and flags only): `submit.handed-over`, `queue.held {identity | handOver}`, `queue.pressed {persons}`, `tailorit.address-refused`, `content.hand-over {said}`, `queue.entry-timeout {from, opened}`.
- **Copy** (the settled words; drafts for the owner, Q7-15): `COPY.platforms`' five names; `COPY.strip.none`, the "isn't supported" list (`COPY.panel.unsupported.sites`: UKG Ready `secure<n>.saashr.com`, Paycom `www.paycomonline.net`, JobDiva `www<n>.jobdiva.com/portal`, Eightfold `<company>.eightfold.ai/careers`; Jibe not listed), the options' "Where Autofill works" intro and the counters' platform line name the four; `COPY.mainView.elsewhereIcims` "This job's application continues on iCIMS. Press Apply on the page, then press Autofill there."; `AGENT_NOTE_WORDS` (the agent's line, where they start in lower case inside "Needs you (…)" as the reasons do (the jr7 review's C-5), and `COPY.queue.notes`, as written): `assessment` "This application continues with an assessment", `documents` "This site asks whether to save your documents to your profile there" (no site named: any adapter's 'documents' hand-over); `COPY.queue.submitYours` "Submit is yours on this site"; `REASONS['disabled-or-readonly']` "The page kept this field locked, so it was not filled. If it opens after another answer, fill it in yourself." (also E2's flag past `WRITABLE_WAIT_MS`); `PAGE_COPY.requiredBlockLeft(block)` "<block>: required, still empty" (Oracle's Skills, Paycom's Signature; it replaced E1's "add at least one"); "Record applications I submit myself"'s help names its sites from `RECORDED_SUBMIT_PLATFORMS` (`src/shared/queue-submit.ts`: every platform where the extension reads a sign after Submit, a drawn rule, a confirmation address or a network sign, so the person's own Submit is recorded there; `QUEUE_SUBMIT_PLATFORMS`' 19 and Breezy HR, Dayforce, CareerPlug, Paycor, Paylocity, SuccessFactors and ClearCompany, whose sign no capture has shown yet; the jr7 review's C-2: INT-7 had named the 19 alone, and `copy.test.ts` now derives the list from the adapters) (`recordOwnHelp`, one pure call, so the content script holds neither the words nor the list).

### Disclosure version 16 (`shared/stored.ts` `DISCLOSURE_VERSION`, `COPY.options.connection.disclosure`)
docs/PRIVACY.md holds it word for word (`src/shared/copy.test.ts`). New: UKG Ready, Paycom, JobDiva and Eightfold among the sites whose pages it reads and fills, and among those whose staff pages it never runs on (UKG Ready's saashr.com named); Run auto-apply taking only the jobs on sites whose application it can walk to the end by itself and whose confirmation it can read, a job on any other site marked Queue tab only in TailorIT and waiting for the Queue tab; a site joining it only when none of the applications it was tested on showed a human check or asked for a code (D-3), a site it already took staying (Greenhouse's and iCIMS's emailed code or link); a tab where a site asks for a code or a check after its Submit, or where the application goes on to a step that is the person's (`handOver`: an assessment), left open, TailorIT told the job needs the person; Workday's jobs taken whatever the tenant asks (no guest-tenant filter: `formAddress` takes every posting `addablePosting` accepts), its sign-in or new account done with the job-site email and password on Sign-in Auto, the job left needing the person at its Sign In or Create Account on Manual; the Queue tab opening queued jobs on any site it fills, pressing a job page's own Apply first, and leaving Submit to the person on a site where it cannot read that an application went through, whatever Submit is set to; a privacy notice a site shows before a resume upload agreed to as an agreement box, under Consent boxes; and on an iCIMS Jibe job page the job's iCIMS address, read from its Apply link, told to TailorIT. The integration wrote it in round 2 (`7bebf1f`) with `DISCLOSURE_VERSION` 16 (`stored.ts` says what each version changed) and `copy.test.ts`'s version row. The jr7 review fixes reworded it in place, still version 16 (main is at 15, so it had reached no one; the row re-hashed): its round-2 words said Run auto-apply never takes "a site that shows a check that you are human or sends you a code", which the sites already in (Greenhouse's emailed code, iCIMS's emailed link, invisible checks) and Workday's account tenants under Sign-in Auto make untrue (C-1, A-6); and its third paragraph and "Keep my submitted answers" name a national identity number the person types among what is never sent (A-7: the worker's `NEVER_SENT`). A connected browser shows it again with I understand, and holds what docs/PRIVACY.md says until then. It reaches a release in the same merge as the jr7 code, which speaks TailorIT's API 7.

### check-build and sizes
- `scripts/check-build.mjs`: the bridge's 28 KiB and the sentinel's 20 KiB budgets and the second HR lock (E0), the provenance guard (INT-7). No other budget was raised.
- **Sizes** (production, measured on round 2's build at `c61c4a7`; every budget met; the jr7 review fixes moved them, A-4's gating about 2.4 KB off the content script, and INT-7 round 3 measures them again): the content script 1,037,468 bytes (1 MiB; 11,108 left; the e2e build 1,040,314, 8,262 left; disclosure 16 added nothing to it, and the API 7 pull took 5 bytes off), 62,819 more than at jr6-done; the sidebar's bundle 769,644 (`SIDEBAR_APP_BUDGET` 819,200; 49,556 left); the page-world bridge 26,996 (28,672; 1,676 left), `submit-watch.js` 29,367 (30,720; 1,353 left), `sentinel.js` 19,386 (20,480; 1,094 left), `page-patches.js` 5,109 (8,192); the background 3,318,492 (no budget; the adapters it holds since AUTO-7, above). The margins are thin: new copy is measured against the e2e build's.

## jr8: Workable's repeatables and the engine's rounds, and the job site's Apply now (the owner's reports of 2026-11-13)
The owner's live Workable run filled every field but Work Experience: of a profile's four jobs, two were added and kept, the third editor was left open with only its end date written, and the fourth never added. The capture (`F:\Develop\Refer\snapshot9`, read for its structure only, never its values) shows why.
- **Live Workable draws each new entry, and the open editor, at the top of its list:** Experience's `ul` held the open editor as its first `li` ("+ Add" disabled), then the two entries kept, newest-added first (their periods ascending top to bottom, where the profile is newest-first); Education's two saved entries the same way. The synthetic page appended them (the editor last), so no test saw it.
- **What went wrong:** the adapter's `blocks()` kept page order, so every round's editor was block 0, the section key of the entry saved in round 1 (`experience[0]`). The fill step's rounds (`orchestrator.ts` `fillStep`: add, fill, save, one entry a round) start each loop from the step's books (`this.carries`, `LoopCarry`), which held round 1's fields under those keys with our values' hashes. The new editor's empty fields read as the page resetting our value (a flip), our rewrite as a second; round 3's reset passed `MAX_VALUE_FLIPS` (2), and its fields were stopped (`value-oscillated`) and never written. The end date, unwritten in round 1 (a current job's), had flips to spare: the capture's one value. The rows of the entry saved in round 1 also stood for the open editor's (`savedRows`), so the report showed them verified.
- **The adapter, the root cause** (`adapters/workable/index.ts` `repeatables`, its header holding the snapshot9 shape): the blocks are the ones that show a saved entry first, in page order, then the open editors, in page order. A new editor always takes a fresh index (the number of saved entries), whether the page draws it at the top (live) or at the bottom; `readEntries`, `currentlyHere`, `save` and the scanner's block index (`dom/sections.ts`) all read that order.
- **The engine, in depth** (`orchestrator.ts` `fillStep`, `content/loop.ts` `carryWithout`): when a round's `saveBlocks` saved blocks and the step goes round again, the step's carry loses every entry (`fields`, `written`, `lastWriteAt`, `skipped`) of those section keys, since their fields are gone and whatever is drawn at those keys later is another entry; a saved block's row stands for a key only while the latest scan shows no field there (the live row wins); and the at-rest read-back (`lookBeforeLeaving`) exempts a saved block's row only while the page no longer shows its field (`savedBlocks`). `MAX_VALUE_FLIPS` stays 2: a field of another entry is simply not the same field.
- **The synthetic page** (`fixtures/synthetic/workable-widgets/app.ts` `Repeatable`) draws as live: the editor first, the saved entries newest-added first (`log.entries` keeps the order they were kept in). e2e 39's saved-company assertions follow it, each naming the cause.
- **Tests:** `src/adapters/workable/workable-widgets.browser.test.ts` (a page drawn as snapshot9: the saved entries blocks 0 and 1, the editor block 2 for `readEntries` and the scan; the restored entry stays block 0 under the editor drawn first); `src/content/workable-rounds.browser.test.ts` (the content runtime with the fake worker and a four-job profile, golden plus two synthetic jobs: all four added, filled and kept, the current job ticked with its end date left, no needs-click; and an editor the run cannot fill left open with its own rows and read back at rest; each with the Workable adapter and with a test-local adapter in page order, which the engine's guard alone must carry); e2e 39's four-job case (the mock's `profile-four-jobs`, docs/CONTRACT.md); `test/mock-server.test.ts`. Undo proofs: the adapter's page order fails the adapter test; the guard reverted fails the page-order runtime case (the owner's bug: four Adds, two entries kept, the third editor open); both reverted fail the real-adapter case too; each of the guard's three parts reverted alone fails its own page-order case.

### The job site's "Apply now" (snapshot12)
The owner's second report: "Currently `Apply Now` button is not working to show the form" on jobs.workable.com. Its four captures (`F:\Develop\Refer\snapshot12`, two postings before and after "Apply now", read for their structure only; the posting before it converted as `fixtures/workable/jobs-tyk-posting.html`) show the posting with no form and no dialog, its `button[data-ui=overview-apply-now]` (and the sticky bar's `sticky-apply-now`, not shown at the page's top), each a button with no type, no href and no form, and after the press the same address with `div[data-role=modal-wrapper] > dialog[role=dialog][aria-modal=true][open]` holding the application's title, the dialog's own Cancel and Submit, and `form[data-ui=application-form]` (34 and 23 fields).
- **What went wrong:** the Workable adapter declared no entry there (its `entryControl` read apply.workable.com's overview links alone), and both Apply now buttons are in its `neverPress`, so the person's Autofill (and an agent's entry) had nothing to press and never opened the form.
- **The fix** (`adapters/workable/index.ts` `jobsSiteApply`, the header holding the snapshot12 shape): the job site's posting declares its Apply now as its `entryControl` (the posting's own, else the sticky bar's, the first a person can see), and only while no application dialog or form shows (open, loading or drawn): Autofill presses it once through the existing entry path (`content/runtime.ts` `atWall`, `enter`; `content/press.ts` `pressEntry`; `dom/fillers/not-submit.ts`'s entry door, which `entryNeverPressed` lets through for that one element), the form the dialog draws is claimed at the posting's own address (`afterPress`) and the worker's grant starts its run. Both buttons stay in `neverPress`, so no other door presses them; the dialog's Cancel and Submit, "Share job" and "Search for jobs" stay never pressed; the form's Submit is Submit mode's. apply.workable.com's overview links are read by their address as before.
- **Tests:** `src/adapters/workable/jobs-site-entry.browser.test.ts` (on `jobs-tyk-posting`: the entry is Apply now, pressed once by the entry door, both buttons never pressed by any other; on `jobs-salvo-apply` and on the posting with a loading dialog: no entry, nothing pressed); `src/content/workable-jobs-site.browser.test.ts` (the content runtime on the synthetic job site, `fixtures/synthetic/workable-widgets` `MountOptions.view` 'jobs': Autofill presses Apply now once, the dialog's form is claimed and filled; the sticky bar's Apply now where the posting's own is not shown; with the dialog already open and loading, Apply now is never pressed and the run starts once the form shows); e2e 39's job-site case (the panel's Autofill, the real worker's grant); `jr6-broad-gate` lists the new page among the declared entries at an application's address, and `jr6-entry`'s P-2 Workable case reads apply.workable.com's form page (the job site's posting now offers its Apply). Undo proofs: no job-site entry fails the runtime case and the adapter's posting case; no open-dialog guard has Apply now pressed while the dialog loads.
- **TailorIT:** untouched. TailorIT's agent and Queue tab do not open Workable's job site today (its addresses for Workable are apply.workable.com's), so the entry serves the person's Autofill there.

- **Sizes:** the content script 1,037,217 bytes in production (1 MiB; 11,359 left), 1,036 more than at `c022ba1` (603 for the rounds, 433 for the job site's entry), and 1,040,063 in the e2e build (8,513 left); no budget raised.

## jr8: iCIMS from Apply to the form (the owner's report of 2026-11-13, snapshot11)
The owner: iCIMS "is not working well from `apply` button to fill the content", and its embedded form's run ended "Stopped" mid-fill. Measured in Chrome on the converted captures (`fixtures/icims/jr8-*`; e2e 71):
- **The claim frame is seen by its wrapper (R1; `content/claim-seen.ts`, `runtime.ts` `frameShown`):** the run's frame check (S1) asked a frame inside another page for its launcher's IntersectionObserver v2 visibility, the launcher at the form's top-right corner. An iCIMS portal sizes `iframe#icims_content_iframe` to its content, so that corner was hidden by what hides no form: our own sidebar (careers-healthedge pins its content at `min-width: 980px`, so under the page push the frame stays under the sidebar at a 1280 px window), a scroll past the form's top, the wrapper's sticky header, another extension's panel. The run stopped with `form-hidden` before filling anything. The frame that claims its wrapper's application (`chain-frame.ts claimsApplication`: the top frame of its own origin names it its application frame) is now seen when the wrapper shows its frame element by the fields' rules (`dom/visibility.ts isUserVisible`); a wrapper hiding it (opacity 0, off-screen, a zero-height wrapper) still stops the run; every other frame inside another page keeps the launcher rule. An IForm's leaf frame (a chain frame on `<co>.icims.com/forms`) keeps it too.
- **The gate after Apply starts behind a reloading wrapper (R2; `background/index.ts` `claimsWaiting`):** Apply in the claim frame moves the top to `/login`, which reloads itself with its layout parameters (`icims/url.ts`). The gate's frame could say hello while the top's registered document (the first `/login` load) wrapped nothing, so no claim frame could be named and the entry grant's form (`sign-up.ts takeFill`) was never started; the wrapper's later hello offered nothing (the agent's hello does). A claimed frame whose form found no claim frame now waits, per tab in memory (ids only), for the top's next document: a wrapper whose claim frame it is offers it the grant's form, once; any other top document lets it go.
- **The email typed again (R3; `core/resolver/labelRules.ts` `EMAIL_AGAIN`, `label.email-again` in `JR6_RULES`):** careers-healthedge's profile asks the email again in a required text box, "Email Verification", which no rule read, so the AI guessed it. Confirm, verify, re-enter, retype or repeat (your) email, and email verification, confirmation or again, anchored (never "Email verification code"), on a text box, are `contact.email`; SmartRecruiters', SuccessFactors', ClearCompany's and Dayforce's own wordings read alike.
- **The converter (`INJECTED_MARKS`):** the captures were taken with Jobright's helper panel and our sidebar open; their marks on the site's elements (`data-jobright-*`, `data-tailorit-*`, `--jobright-*` custom properties, Jobright's page-push `<style>`) go before anything is measured.

## jr8: the integration (INT-8, 2026-11-13)
The four jr8 branches (Workable's rounds and job site, Ashby's Yes/No and state, Workday's prompts, skeletons and Next, iCIMS's Apply and frame) merged in that order onto `c022ba1`. Their code merged without a conflict; the docs' sections are kept side by side, and SPEC-AMENDMENTS has one §21 with a part per platform.
- **Where they meet** (`content/orchestrator.ts` `fillStep`): the rows the step ends with are Workable's (a saved block's row stands only while the page shows no field at its key) with Ashby's marks (`pressedIn`: a Yes/No the page shows answered reads filled); the step's one required-field check (`content/required-left.ts`, Workday's) reads them at the validation, before Next's click and for the panel's Continue, and at FILLED counts the step's rows as the page shows them (`countedAsShown`), so a Yes/No the page answered holds no Next on Auto by its shown answer alone, with or without the mark. A row marked so is never a trap's, a hard stop or an attention check (`pressedIn`).
- **Cross-checks** (`src/content/jr8-int.browser.test.ts`): Workable's four jobs with Next on Auto are all kept and nothing is pressed, and its one step reports itself the last (the dock offers Submit, never Continue); Ashby's form read as a step before the last presses Next on Auto once both Yes/No show the page's answers, and holds it while they show none.
- **Privacy:** the fixture leak gate read three synthetic sources jr8 edited, each holding a generic word of the owner's list that the golden profile also holds; the job site's synthetic posting has another title, the restored Workable entry reads its title from the golden profile (`fixtures/synthetic/workable-widgets/main.ts`), and Ashby's Field of Study placeholder names another field.
- **Sizes** (production, `npm run check:build -- --all`; every budget met, none raised): the content script 1,042,327 bytes (6,249 left of 1 MiB; 6,146 more than at `c022ba1`), 1,045,173 in the e2e build (3,403 left); the bridge 26,996 (1,676 left), the submit watcher 29,367 (1,353 left), the sentinel 19,386 (1,094 left), the sidebar's bundle 771,215 (47,985 left), the page patches 5,109 (3,083 left).

## Where the work happens
- **The extension:** a new git repository at `F:\Develop\TailorIT-autofill`, with no remote until the owner adds one; nothing is pushed.
  - One npm package, Node 24, exact dependency versions, a committed lockfile, and Windows-first Node scripts (TailorIT's conventions).
  - Its pure core cannot import `@tailorit/shared`; the few pure helpers it needs are rewritten there.
- **TailorIT:** the owner's checkout, `F:\Develop\TailorIT` (branch `v1.2`), on 3100 and 4100. Its autofill backend adds pairing, the profile, the job lookup, the resume file, the AI tasks, saved answers and the settings pages.
  - Phases 2-6 built it on the branch `autofill`, cut from `v1.1` in its own git worktree, `F:\Develop\TailorIT-wt-autofill`, with its own `npm install`. The owner's checkout stayed on v1.1 meanwhile, so their dev server (`tsx watch`, which migrates the database on every restart) never loaded an unreviewed migration.
  - The worktree had a git-ignored `.env`: `DATA_DIR=%LOCALAPPDATA%\TailorIT-autofill-dev`, web 3300, API 4300, and the fake Claude CLI.
